function ico(name, size = 18){
  return `<svg width="${size}" height="${size}" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">${ICON_PATHS[name] || ''}</svg>`;
}

const ACTIONS_LIVE_REFRESH_MS = 3000;
let activeLiveBuildMonitor = null;
const repoBranchSelection = {}; // fullName -> nom de branche actuellement choisie

function stopLiveBuildMonitor(){
  if(activeLiveBuildMonitor?.timer)clearTimeout(activeLiveBuildMonitor.timer);
  activeLiveBuildMonitor=null;
}
function startLiveBuildMonitor(key,refresh){
  stopLiveBuildMonitor();
  const monitor={key,refresh,timer:null,busy:false};
  activeLiveBuildMonitor=monitor;
  const schedule=delay=>{if(activeLiveBuildMonitor===monitor)monitor.timer=setTimeout(tick,delay);};
  async function tick(){
    if(activeLiveBuildMonitor!==monitor)return;
    if(document.visibilityState!=='visible'){schedule(ACTIONS_LIVE_REFRESH_MS);return;}
    if(monitor.busy){schedule(ACTIONS_LIVE_REFRESH_MS);return;}
    monitor.busy=true;
    let keepWatching=true;
    try{keepWatching=await monitor.refresh()!==false;}catch(error){console.warn('Actualisation du build impossible; nouvel essai prévu.',error);}
    monitor.busy=false;
    if(activeLiveBuildMonitor!==monitor)return;
    if(!keepWatching){stopLiveBuildMonitor();return;}
    schedule(ACTIONS_LIVE_REFRESH_MS);
  }
  monitor.tick=tick;
  schedule(ACTIONS_LIVE_REFRESH_MS);
}
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{
  if(document.visibilityState==='visible'&&activeLiveBuildMonitor){
    if(activeLiveBuildMonitor.timer)clearTimeout(activeLiveBuildMonitor.timer);
    activeLiveBuildMonitor.timer=setTimeout(activeLiveBuildMonitor.tick,0);
  }
});

/**
 * Filet de sécurité global : jusqu'ici, une erreur JS survenant hors d'un
 * try/catch explicite (ex. dans un .then() oublié, une exception levée
 * DANS un bloc catch lui-même) ne provoquait RIEN de visible — l'écran se
 * contentait de recharger silencieusement, ce qui rendait le vrai bug
 * (reset qui échoue sans message) impossible à diagnostiquer. Désormais,
 * toute erreur non interceptée ailleurs remonte au moins sous forme de
 * toast, au lieu de disparaître.
 */
window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', (e) => {
  console.error('Promesse non gérée :', e.reason);
  toast(`Erreur inattendue : ${friendly(e.reason)}`, 'error', 6000);
});
window.addEventListener('error', (e) => {
  console.error('Erreur JS :', e.error || e.message);
  toast(`Erreur inattendue : ${(e.error && e.error.message) || e.message || 'inconnue'}`, 'error', 6000);
});

/* ============================================================
   HELPERS UI : toast / modal / confirm / prompt
============================================================ */
function escapeHtml(s){
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.textContent = s === undefined || s === null ? '' : String(s);
  return d.innerHTML;
}
function sanitizeFilename(name){
  return (name || 'export').replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|\s]+/g, '_').slice(0, 120) || 'export';
}

/**
 * Formatage de date "à la GitHub" : relatif et court (aujourd'hui, hier, 12j…)
 * au lieu d'une date absolue type 10/31 — c'est ce que montre l'interface
 * réelle de GitHub, donc c'est ce que l'app doit reproduire.
 */
function relDate(iso){
  if (!iso) return '—';
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const now = new Date();
  const startD = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate());
  const startNow = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
  const diffJ = Math.round((startNow - startD) / 86400000);
  if (diffJ <= 0) return "aujourd'hui";
  if (diffJ === 1) return 'hier';
  if (diffJ < 30) return `${diffJ}j`;
  if (diffJ < 365) return `${Math.round(diffJ / 30)} mois`;
  const ans = Math.round(diffJ / 365);
  return `${ans} an${ans > 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
}

function formatRunDateTime(iso){
  if(!iso)return '—';
  const date=new Date(iso);
  if(Number.isNaN(date.getTime()))return '—';
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('fr-FR',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit',year:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hourCycle:'h23'}).format(date);
}

/** Durée compacte et lisible dans les listes/logs : 10 sec, 30 min, 1 h 10 sec. */
function formatCompactDuration(totalSeconds){
  const seconds=Math.max(0,Math.round(Number(totalSeconds)||0));
  if(seconds<60)return `${seconds} sec`;
  const minutes=Math.floor(seconds/60),rest=seconds%60;
  if(minutes<60)return `${minutes} min${rest?` ${rest} sec`:''}`;
  const hours=Math.floor(minutes/60),restMinutes=minutes%60;
  return `${hours} h${restMinutes?` ${restMinutes} min`:''}${rest?` ${rest} sec`:''}`;
}
function runDuration(run){
  const start = run.run_started_at || run.created_at;
  if (!start) return '—';
  const endTime = run.status === 'completed' && run.updated_at ? new Date(run.updated_at).getTime() : Date.now();
  return formatCompactDuration(Math.max(0,Math.round((endTime-new Date(start).getTime())/1000)));
}

function toast(message, type = 'info', duration = 3600){
  const wrap = document.getElementById('toastWrap');
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = `toast toast-${type}`;
  const icName = type === 'success' ? 'check' : type === 'error' ? 'xCircle' : 'alert';
  el.innerHTML = `<span class="toast-ic">${ico(icName, 16)}</span><span>${escapeHtml(message)}</span>`;
  wrap.appendChild(el);
  requestAnimationFrame(() => el.classList.add('show'));
  setTimeout(() => { el.classList.remove('show'); setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 220); }, duration);
}

function ensureOverlay(){
  let o = document.getElementById('modalOverlay');
  if (!o){
    o = document.createElement('div');
    o.id = 'modalOverlay';
    o.className = 'modal-overlay';
    document.body.appendChild(o);
    o.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === o) closeModal(); });
  }
  return o;
}
function openModal({ title, bodyHTML, footerHTML = '' }){
  const o = ensureOverlay();
  const wasOpen = o.classList.contains('open');
  o.innerHTML = `<div class="modal-box">
      <div class="modal-header"><span>${title}</span><button class="modal-close" id="mClose">${ico('close', 16)}</button></div>
      <div class="modal-body">${bodyHTML}</div>
      ${footerHTML ? `<div class="modal-footer">${footerHTML}</div>` : ''}
    </div>`;
  document.getElementById('mClose').onclick = closeModal;
  document.body.classList.add('modal-open');
  presentModal(o, wasOpen);
  return o;
}
/* Anime l'entrée d'une fenêtre : feuille qui monte avec ressort, fond qui s'assombrit.
   Si une fenêtre était déjà ouverte (contenu remplacé), on ne rejoue pas l'entrée. */
function presentModal(o, wasOpen){
  clearTimeout(o._blurT);
  o.classList.remove('blur-ready','is-dragging');
  o.style.removeProperty('--sheet-dim');
  const box = o.querySelector('.modal-box');
  if (box && wasOpen) box.classList.add('instant');
  void o.offsetWidth; /* l'état « fermé » est calculé avant de lancer la transition */
  o.classList.add('open');
  o._blurT = setTimeout(() => { if (o.classList.contains('open') && !o.classList.contains('is-dragging')) o.classList.add('blur-ready'); }, 480);
}
function closeModal(){
  const o = document.getElementById('modalOverlay');
  document.body.classList.remove('modal-open');
  if (!o || !o.classList.contains('open')) return;
  clearTimeout(o._blurT);
  const box = o.querySelector('.modal-box');
  if (box){ box.classList.remove('instant'); box.style.transform = ''; box.style.transition = ''; }
  o.classList.remove('blur-ready', 'is-dragging', 'open');
  o.style.removeProperty('--sheet-dim');
}

/* Fermer une fenêtre en la glissant vers le bas (mobile) : par l'en-tête,
   ou par le corps quand il est déjà tout en haut. */
(function initSheetDrag(){
  const mobile = window.matchMedia('(max-width:699px)');
  let st = null;
  function cleanup(){
    document.removeEventListener('touchmove', onMove);
    document.removeEventListener('touchend', onEnd);
    document.removeEventListener('touchcancel', onEnd);
    st = null;
  }
  function onMove(e){
    if (!st) return;
    const t = e.touches[0]; if (!t) return;
    const dy = t.clientY - st.y0, dx = t.clientX - st.x0;
    if (!st.drag){
      if (Math.abs(dx) > 8 && Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy) * 1.2){ cleanup(); return; }
      if (dy > 8 && (!st.fromBody || st.body.scrollTop <= 0)){
        st.drag = true; st.y0 = t.clientY; st.lastY = t.clientY; st.lastT = e.timeStamp;
        st.o.classList.add('is-dragging'); st.o.classList.remove('blur-ready');
        st.box.style.transition = 'none';
      } else {
        if (dy < -8 || (st.fromBody && st.body.scrollTop > 0)) cleanup();
        return;
      }
    }
    if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
    const d = Math.max(0, t.clientY - st.y0);
    const dt = Math.max(1, e.timeStamp - st.lastT);
    st.v = (t.clientY - st.lastY) / dt; st.lastY = t.clientY; st.lastT = e.timeStamp;
    st.dy = d;
    st.box.style.transform = `translate3d(0,${d}px,0)`;
    st.o.style.setProperty('--sheet-dim', String(Math.max(0, 1 - d / st.h)));
  }
  function onEnd(){
    if (!st) return;
    const s = st;
    cleanup();
    if (!s.drag) return;
    if (s.v > 0.6 || s.dy > Math.max(110, s.h * 0.28)){
      closeModal();
    } else {
      s.box.style.transition = 'transform .38s cubic-bezier(.3,1.25,.5,1)';
      s.box.style.transform = 'translate3d(0,0,0)';
      s.o.classList.remove('is-dragging');
      s.o.style.removeProperty('--sheet-dim');
      setTimeout(() => {
        s.box.style.transition = ''; s.box.style.transform = '';
        if (s.o.classList.contains('open')) s.o.classList.add('blur-ready');
      }, 400);
    }
  }
  document.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
    if (st || !mobile.matches || e.touches.length !== 1) return;
    const box = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#modalOverlay .modal-box');
    if (!box || box.classList.contains('modal-fullscreen')) return;
    const o = box.parentElement;
    if (!o || !o.classList.contains('open')) return;
    const inHeader = !!e.target.closest('.modal-header');
    const body = box.querySelector('.modal-body');
    const inBody = !inHeader && body && body.contains(e.target) && !e.target.closest('textarea,input,select,pre,[contenteditable="true"],.editor-shell');
    if (!inHeader && !inBody) return;
    const t = e.touches[0];
    st = { box, o, body, fromBody: !!inBody, x0: t.clientX, y0: t.clientY, lastY: t.clientY, lastT: e.timeStamp, dy: 0, v: 0, drag: false, h: Math.max(200, box.offsetHeight) };
    document.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: false });
    document.addEventListener('touchend', onEnd);
    document.addEventListener('touchcancel', onEnd);
  }, { passive: true });
})();

/* ---------- Copier / Exporter / Partager (robuste mobile) ---------- */
/**
 * Copie le texte d'un <textarea readonly> visible à l'écran.
 * IMPORTANT : on ne vérifie PAS window.isSecureContext avant de tenter
 * navigator.clipboard.writeText — cette vérification est trop stricte et
 * bloquait à tort la copie dans les visionneuses en content://, alors que
 * l'API fonctionne très bien là-bas en pratique (confirmé par test terrain).
 * On tente directement l'API moderne, puis execCommand, puis en dernier
 * recours on laisse le texte sélectionné pour la copie manuelle native.
 */
async function copyFromTextarea(id){
  const ta = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!ta) return;
  const text = ta.value;
  let autoCopied = false;

  if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText){
    try{
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      autoCopied = true;
    } catch(e){ autoCopied = false; }
  }

  if (!autoCopied){
    try{
      ta.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      ta.setSelectionRange(0, text.length);
      autoCopied = document.execCommand('copy');
    } catch(e){ autoCopied = false; }
  }

  if (autoCopied){
    toast('Copié dans le presse-papiers', 'success', 2200);
  } else {
    // Repli garanti : le texte reste sélectionné à l'écran, l'utilisateur peut
    // copier via le menu natif Android qui apparaît sur la sélection.
    ta.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    ta.setSelectionRange(0, text.length);
    toast('Texte sélectionné — appuie sur "Copier" dans le menu qui apparaît au-dessus', 'info', 5000);
  }
}

// Convertit un Blob en base64 pur (sans le préfixe "data:...;base64,"), seul
// format transmissible à travers un pont JavascriptInterface Android (qui ne
// sait passer que des chaînes/primitives, jamais un objet Blob).
function sumByteSizes(values){
  // Addition centralisée : les tailles GitHub arrivent parfois comme chaînes.
  // On arrondit chaque valeur à l’octet avant la somme pour éviter les décimales
  // flottantes et ignorer proprement les valeurs invalides.
  return values.reduce((total,value)=>{
    const bytes=Number(value);
    return total+(Number.isFinite(bytes)&&bytes>0?Math.round(bytes):0);
  },0);
}
function formatTransferBytes(bytes){
  const safeBytes=Number.isFinite(Number(bytes))&&Number(bytes)>=0?Math.round(Number(bytes)):0;
  if(safeBytes<1024)return `${safeBytes} o`;
  if(safeBytes<1048576)return `${(safeBytes/1024).toFixed(1)} Ko`;
  return `${(safeBytes/1048576).toFixed(1)} Mo`;
}

function createTransferProgress(title,filename,totalBytes=0,initialStage='Préparation…'){
  let activeTotalBytes=totalBytes;
  const overlay=openModal({title:escapeHtml(title),bodyHTML:`<div class="transfer-progress" role="status" aria-live="polite">
    <div class="transfer-progress-head"><div><div class="transfer-progress-stage" id="transferStage">${escapeHtml(initialStage)}</div><div class="transfer-progress-name">${escapeHtml(filename)}</div></div><div class="transfer-progress-percent" id="transferPercent">0%</div></div>
    <div class="transfer-progress-track indeterminate" id="transferTrack" role="progressbar" aria-label="Progression du transfert" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100"><span class="transfer-progress-fill" id="transferFill"></span></div>
    <div class="transfer-progress-meta"><span id="transferBytes">${totalBytes?`0 o sur ${formatTransferBytes(totalBytes)}`:'Opération en cours'}</span><span id="transferHint">L’application reste réactive.</span></div>
  </div>`});
  const modal=overlay.querySelector('.modal-box');
  const update=(percent,stage,loaded=null,total=activeTotalBytes)=>{
    const stageEl=document.getElementById('transferStage'),percentEl=document.getElementById('transferPercent'),fill=document.getElementById('transferFill'),track=document.getElementById('transferTrack'),bytesEl=document.getElementById('transferBytes');
    if(stageEl&&stage)stageEl.textContent=stage;
    if(Number.isFinite(percent)){
      const value=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,percent));
      if(percentEl)percentEl.textContent=`${Math.round(value)}%`;
      if(fill)fill.style.width=`${value}%`;
      if(track){track.classList.remove('indeterminate');track.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(Math.round(value)));}
    }else{
      if(percentEl)percentEl.textContent='…';
      if(track){track.classList.add('indeterminate');track.removeAttribute('aria-valuenow');}
    }
    if(bytesEl&&Number.isFinite(loaded)&&Number.isFinite(total)&&total>0)bytesEl.textContent=`${formatTransferBytes(loaded)} sur ${formatTransferBytes(total)}`;
  };
  return{
    update,
    setTotal(bytes){activeTotalBytes=bytes;},
    finish(message='Terminé') { update(100,message,activeTotalBytes,activeTotalBytes); setTimeout(()=>{if(document.getElementById('modalOverlay')===overlay&&overlay.querySelector('.modal-box')===modal)closeModal();},650); },
    fail(){closeModal();}
  };
}

function blobToBase64(blob,onProgress=()=>{}){
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onprogress=event=>{if(event.lengthComputable)onProgress(event.loaded,event.total);};
    reader.onloadend = () => {
      const result = reader.result || '';
      const idx = result.indexOf(',');
      resolve(idx >= 0 ? result.slice(idx + 1) : result);
    };
    reader.onerror = reject;
    reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
  });
}

function bytesToBase64(bytes){
  let binary='';
  for(let offset=0;offset<bytes.length;offset+=0x8000)binary+=String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(offset,offset+0x8000));
  return btoa(binary);
}

async function streamResponseToAndroid(filename,response,mime,ui,{startPercent=8,endPercent=88,share=false,openAfter=false,installAfter=false,expectedBytes=0}={}){
  const bridge=window.AndroidBridge;
  if(!androidSupportsChunkedFileTransfer())return null;
  const responseLength=Number(response.headers?.get('content-length'))||0;
  const total=responseLength||expectedBytes||0;
  const id=bridge.beginFileTransfer(sanitizeFilename(filename),mime,responseLength,!!share,!!openAfter&&!share,!!installAfter&&!share);
  if(!id)throw new Error('Android n’a pas pu préparer le fichier.');
  let loaded=0;
  try{
    if(response.body?.getReader){
      const reader=response.body.getReader();
      while(true){
        const {done,value}=await reader.read();if(done)break;
        if(value?.byteLength){
          if(bridge.appendFileTransfer(String(id),bytesToBase64(value))===false)throw new Error('Android n’a pas pu écrire une partie du fichier.');
          loaded+=value.byteLength;
          const ratio=total?Math.min(1,loaded/total):null;
          ui?.update(ratio===null?null:startPercent+ratio*(endPercent-startPercent),`Réception · ${formatTransferBytes(loaded)}${total?` / ${formatTransferBytes(total)}`:''}`,loaded,total||undefined);
        }
      }
    }else{
      const blob=await response.blob();
      const uri=await streamBlobToAndroid(filename,blob,mime,share,{update:(percent,stage,bytes,size)=>ui?.update(startPercent+(percent/100)*(endPercent-startPercent),stage,bytes,size)},openAfter,installAfter);
      return uri;
    }
    const uri=bridge.finishFileTransfer(String(id));
    if(uri===false||uri==null||uri==='')throw new Error('Android n’a pas pu finaliser le fichier.');
    return typeof uri==='string'?uri:null;
  }catch(error){try{bridge.cancelFileTransfer?.(String(id));}catch(_){}throw error;}
}

async function transferGithubResource(url,filename,mime,expectedBytes=0,{share=false,openAfter=false,installAfter=false,showInstallPanel=false,accept='application/octet-stream',title='Téléchargement'}={}){
  const transfer=createTransferProgress(title,filename,expectedBytes,'Connexion à GitHub…');
  try{
    const response=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store',headers:{Authorization:`token ${authToken}`,Accept:accept}});
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`GitHub a renvoyé HTTP ${response.status}.`);
    if(androidSupportsChunkedFileTransfer()){
      const uri=await streamResponseToAndroid(filename,response,mime,transfer,{expectedBytes,share,openAfter,installAfter});
      if(!uri)throw new Error('Android n’a pas renvoyé le fichier terminé.');
      transfer.finish(share?'Fichier prêt à partager':openAfter?'Ouverture avec une application externe…':'Téléchargement terminé');
      if(showInstallPanel&&installAfter&&window.AndroidBridge?.installDownloadedApk)promptInstallDownloadedApk({uri,filename:sanitizeFilename(filename)},{autoInstall:false});
      return uri;
    }
    const blob=await responseToBlobWithProgress(response,transfer,{endPercent:58,expectedBytes});
    if(share){await shareBlobFile(filename,blob,mime,transfer,60,100);return true;}
    if(openAfter){
      const objectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([blob],{type:mime||blob.type||'application/octet-stream'}));
      const link=document.createElement('a');link.href=objectUrl;link.target='_blank';link.rel='noopener';document.body.appendChild(link);link.click();link.remove();
      setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl),60000);
      transfer.finish('Ouverture sans enregistrer dans Téléchargements');
      return true;
    }
    const uri=await saveBlob(filename,blob,mime,transfer,60,100);
    return uri||true;
  }catch(error){transfer.fail();showError(`${title} impossible`,friendly(error));return false;}
}

function promptInstallDownloadedApk(apk,{autoInstall=false}={}){
  openModal({title:'Installation de l’application',bodyHTML:`<div class="install-banner"><span class="install-banner-icon">${ico('download',20)}</span><div class="install-banner-copy"><strong>${STUDIO_APP_NAME} est prête à être installée</strong><p>${escapeHtml(apk.filename)}
<span id="apkInstallStatus">${autoInstall?'Installation Android en cours…':'Prêt à lancer l’installation Android.'}</span></p></div></div>`,footerHTML:`<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="apkPanelClose">Fermer</button><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="apkInstallNow">${ico('download',14)} Installer / réinstaller</button>`});
  document.getElementById('apkPanelClose').onclick=closeModal;
  const install=()=>{const status=document.getElementById('apkInstallStatus');if(status)status.textContent='Installation lancée par Android. Le bouton reste disponible pour réinstaller.';if(window.AndroidBridge?.installDownloadedApk)window.AndroidBridge.installDownloadedApk(apk.uri,apk.filename);else showError('Installation impossible','L’installation directe nécessite l’application Android GitHub Studio.');};
  document.getElementById('apkInstallNow').onclick=install;
  if(autoInstall)setTimeout(install,250);
}

async function transferGithubArtifactApk(url,artifactName,expectedBytes=0){
  const transfer=createTransferProgress('Téléchargement de l’APK',artifactName,expectedBytes,'Connexion à GitHub…');
  const bridge=window.AndroidBridge;
  let transferId=null;
  try{
    if(!androidSupportsChunkedFileTransfer()||typeof bridge.beginArtifactTransfer!=='function'||typeof bridge.finishArtifactTransfer!=='function'){
      throw new Error('L’extraction automatique des artefacts APK est disponible dans l’application Android GitHub Studio.');
    }
    const response=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store',headers:{Authorization:`token ${authToken}`,Accept:'application/vnd.github+json'}});
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`GitHub a renvoyé HTTP ${response.status}.`);
    const contentLength=Number(response.headers?.get('content-length'))||0;
    const total=contentLength||expectedBytes||0;
    transfer.setTotal(total);
    transferId=bridge.beginArtifactTransfer(`${sanitizeFilename(artifactName)}.zip`,contentLength);
    if(!transferId)throw new Error('Android n’a pas pu préparer l’extraction de l’artefact.');
    let loaded=0;
    const append=bytes=>{
      if(!bytes?.byteLength)return;
      if(bridge.appendFileTransfer(String(transferId),bytesToBase64(bytes))===false)throw new Error('Android n’a pas pu écrire une partie de l’artefact.');
      loaded+=bytes.byteLength;
      const ratio=total?Math.min(1,loaded/total):null;
      transfer.update(ratio===null?null:8+ratio*82,`Téléchargement · ${formatTransferBytes(loaded)}${total?` / ${formatTransferBytes(total)}`:''}`,loaded,total||undefined);
    };
    if(response.body?.getReader){
      const reader=response.body.getReader();
      while(true){const {done,value}=await reader.read();if(done)break;append(value);}
    }else{
      const blob=await response.blob();
      const chunkSize=192*1024;
      for(let offset=0;offset<blob.size;offset+=chunkSize){
        append(new Uint8Array(await blob.slice(offset,Math.min(blob.size,offset+chunkSize)).arrayBuffer()));
        await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
      }
    }
    transfer.update(94,'Extraction de l’APK…',loaded,total||loaded);
    const raw=bridge.finishArtifactTransfer(String(transferId));
    transferId=null;
    if(!raw)throw new Error('Aucun APK n’a pu être extrait de cet artefact.');
    const apk=JSON.parse(raw);
    if(!apk.uri||!apk.filename)throw new Error('Android n’a pas renvoyé le fichier APK extrait.');
    transfer.finish(`APK extrait · ${apk.filename}`);
    promptInstallDownloadedApk(apk,{autoInstall:true});
    return apk;
  }catch(error){
    if(transferId)try{bridge.cancelFileTransfer?.(String(transferId));}catch(_){}
    transfer.fail();
    showError('Téléchargement de l’APK impossible',friendly(error));
    return false;
  }
}

async function responseToBlobWithProgress(response,ui,{endPercent=58,expectedBytes=0,stage='Téléchargement depuis GitHub…'}={}){
  const headerLength=Number(response.headers?.get('content-length'))||0;
  const total=headerLength||expectedBytes||0;
  if(!response.body?.getReader){
    const blob=await response.blob();
    ui.update(endPercent,`Réception terminée · ${formatTransferBytes(blob.size)}`,blob.size,blob.size);
    return blob;
  }
  const reader=response.body.getReader(),chunks=[];
  let loaded=0;
  while(true){
    const {done,value}=await reader.read();
    if(done)break;
    chunks.push(value);loaded+=value.byteLength;
    const fraction=total?Math.min(1,loaded/total):null;
    ui.update(fraction===null?null:fraction*endPercent,`${stage} ${formatTransferBytes(loaded)}${total?` / ${formatTransferBytes(total)}`:''}`,loaded,total||undefined);
  }
  ui.update(endPercent,`Réception terminée · ${formatTransferBytes(loaded)}`,loaded,total||loaded);
  return new Blob(chunks,{type:response.headers?.get('content-type')||'application/octet-stream'});
}

async function fetchGithubBlobWithProgress(fullName,sha,onProgress){
  const response=await fetch(`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/git/blobs/${sha}`,{headers:{Authorization:`token ${authToken}`,Accept:'application/vnd.github.raw'}});
  if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`Téléchargement du fichier impossible (HTTP ${response.status}).`);
  let lastLoaded=0;
  return responseToBlobWithProgress(response,{update:(_percent,stage,loaded,total)=>{
    const loadedNow=Number.isFinite(loaded)?loaded:0;
    const delta=Math.max(0,loadedNow-lastLoaded);lastLoaded=loadedNow;
    onProgress(delta,loadedNow,total,stage);
  }},{endPercent:100,expectedBytes:0,stage:'Lecture du dépôt…'});
}

function androidSupportsChunkedFileTransfer(){
  const bridge=window.AndroidBridge;
  return !!(bridge&&typeof bridge.beginFileTransfer==='function'&&typeof bridge.appendFileTransfer==='function'&&typeof bridge.finishFileTransfer==='function');
}

async function streamBlobToAndroid(filename,blob,mime,share,progress,openExternal=false,installAfter=false){
  const bridge=window.AndroidBridge;
  if(!androidSupportsChunkedFileTransfer())return false;
  const id=bridge.beginFileTransfer(sanitizeFilename(filename),mime,blob.size,!!share,!!openExternal&&!share,!!installAfter&&!share);
  if(!id)throw new Error('Android n’a pas pu préparer le fichier.');
  try{
    const chunkSize=192*1024;
    for(let offset=0;offset<blob.size;offset+=chunkSize){
      const end=Math.min(blob.size,offset+chunkSize);
      const bytes=new Uint8Array(await blob.slice(offset,end).arrayBuffer());
      if(bridge.appendFileTransfer(String(id),bytesToBase64(bytes))===false)throw new Error('Android n’a pas pu écrire une partie du fichier.');
      const ratio=end/blob.size;
      progress?.update(12+ratio*80,share?'Préparation du partage…':openExternal?'Préparation de l’ouverture temporaire…':`Enregistrement · ${formatTransferBytes(end)} / ${formatTransferBytes(blob.size)}`,end,blob.size);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
    }
    const completedUri=bridge.finishFileTransfer(String(id));
    if(completedUri===false||completedUri==null||completedUri==='')throw new Error('Android n’a pas pu finaliser le fichier.');
    return typeof completedUri==='string'?completedUri:null;
  }catch(error){
    try{bridge.cancelFileTransfer?.(String(id));}catch(_){}
    throw error;
  }
}

async function downloadText(filename, content){
  const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
  return saveBlob(filename,blob,'text/plain');
}

async function saveBlob(filename, blob, mime = 'application/octet-stream', transfer=null, progressStart=0, progressEnd=100){
  const ui=transfer||createTransferProgress('Téléchargement',filename,blob.size,'Préparation du fichier…');
  const report=(ratio,stage,loaded,total)=>ui.update(progressStart+Math.max(0,Math.min(1,ratio))*(progressEnd-progressStart),stage,loaded,total);
  try{
    if(androidSupportsChunkedFileTransfer()){
      report(0,'Préparation de l’enregistrement…',0,blob.size);
      const uri=await streamBlobToAndroid(filename,blob,mime,false,{update:(pct,stage,loaded,total)=>ui.update(progressStart+(pct/100)*(progressEnd-progressStart),stage,loaded,total)});
      ui.finish('Enregistré dans Téléchargements');
      toast('Fichier enregistré dans Téléchargements','success',3000);
      return uri;
    }
    if(window.AndroidBridge&&window.AndroidBridge.downloadFile){
      report(0,'Encodage du fichier…',0,blob.size);
      const b64=await blobToBase64(blob,(loaded,total)=>report(loaded/total*78,'Encodage du fichier…',loaded,total));
      report(.88,'Transmission à Android…',blob.size,blob.size);
      const saved=window.AndroidBridge.downloadFile(b64,sanitizeFilename(filename),mime);
      if(saved===false)throw new Error('Android n’a pas pu enregistrer le fichier dans Téléchargements.');
      ui.finish('Enregistré dans Téléchargements');
      toast('Fichier téléchargé dans Téléchargements','success',3000);
      return;
    }
    report(.7,'Transmission au navigateur…',blob.size,blob.size);
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),anchor=document.createElement('a');
    anchor.href=url;anchor.download=sanitizeFilename(filename);document.body.appendChild(anchor);anchor.click();anchor.remove();
    setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),5000);
    ui.finish('Téléchargement transmis au navigateur');
    toast('Téléchargement transmis au navigateur','success',2600);
  }catch(error){ui.fail();showError('Téléchargement impossible',friendly(error));return false;}
}

async function shareBlobFile(filename, blob, mime = 'application/octet-stream', transfer=null, progressStart=0, progressEnd=100){
  const ui=transfer||createTransferProgress('Préparation du partage',filename,blob.size,'Préparation du fichier…');
  const mapUpdate=(percent,stage,loaded,total)=>ui.update(progressStart+(percent/100)*(progressEnd-progressStart),stage,loaded,total);
  if(window.AndroidBridge&&(androidSupportsChunkedFileTransfer()||window.AndroidBridge.shareFile)){
    try{
      if(androidSupportsChunkedFileTransfer()){await streamBlobToAndroid(filename,blob,mime,true,{update:mapUpdate});}
      else{const b64=await blobToBase64(blob,(loaded,total)=>mapUpdate(loaded/total*88,'Préparation du partage…',loaded,total));window.AndroidBridge.shareFile(b64,sanitizeFilename(filename),mime);}
      ui.finish('Ouverture du partage…');
      return;
    }catch(error){ui.fail();showError('Partage impossible',friendly(error));return false;}
  }
  try{
    const file=new File([blob],sanitizeFilename(filename),{type:mime});
    if(navigator.canShare&&navigator.canShare({files:[file]})&&navigator.share){ui.update(96,'Ouverture du partage…',blob.size,blob.size);await navigator.share({files:[file],title:filename});ui.finish('Partage terminé');return;}
    await saveBlob(filename,blob,mime,ui,progressStart,progressEnd);
  }catch(error){ui.fail();if(error?.name!=='AbortError')showError('Partage impossible',friendly(error));}
}

async function shareText(filename, content){
  // Dans l'app Android empaquetée, la Web Share API (navigator.share) n'existe
  // tout simplement pas dans une WebView standard — ce n'est pas un problème
  // d'encodage, la fonction est absente. On passe donc par le pont natif qui
  // déclenche un vrai Intent.ACTION_SEND avec le fichier encodé en base64.
  if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.shareFile){
    try{
      const blob = new Blob([content], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
      const b64 = await blobToBase64(blob);
      window.AndroidBridge.shareFile(b64, sanitizeFilename(filename), 'text/plain');
    } catch(e){
      toast('Partage impossible sur cet appareil', 'error');
    }
    return;
  }
  // Le partage de FICHIERS (navigator.share({files:[...]})) est peu supporté
  // dans les navigateurs/visionneuses restreintes et échoue silencieusement en
  // tombant sur le téléchargement. Le partage de TEXTE brut est bien plus
  // largement supporté (confirmé sur un autre outil qui fonctionne) — on le
  // tente donc en priorité, et on ne bascule sur un fichier / téléchargement
  // que si le texte est trop volumineux ou si le partage échoue.
  const TEXT_SHARE_LIMIT = 60000; // au-delà, beaucoup d'apps tronquent ou refusent le partage texte
  try{
    if (navigator.share && content.length <= TEXT_SHARE_LIMIT){
      await navigator.share({ title: filename, text: content });
      return;
    }
    if (navigator.canShare && navigator.share){
      const file = new File([content], sanitizeFilename(filename), { type: 'text/plain' });
      if (navigator.canShare({ files: [file] })){
        await navigator.share({ files: [file], title: filename });
        return;
      }
    }
    throw new Error('Web Share indisponible');
  } catch(e){
    if (e && e.name === 'AbortError') return; // l'utilisateur a annulé le partage
    downloadText(filename, content);
  }
}

// Partage un simple LIEN (ex. l'URL github.com d'un run) plutôt qu'un fichier —
// réutilise le même pont AndroidBridge.shareText déjà en place, un lien étant
// juste du texte comme un autre pour un Intent.ACTION_SEND.
async function shareLink(url, title){
  if (window.AndroidBridge && window.AndroidBridge.shareText){
    window.AndroidBridge.shareText(url, title || '');
    return;
  }
  try{
    if (navigator.share){ await navigator.share({ title, url }); return; }
  } catch(e){
    if (e && e.name === 'AbortError') return;
  }
  await copyLink(url);
}

async function copyTextValue(value,label='Identifiant'){
  const text=String(value??'');
  if(!text)return false;
  try{
    if(window.AndroidBridge&&typeof window.AndroidBridge.copyText==='function'){window.AndroidBridge.copyText(text);toast(`${label} copié`,'success',2200);return true;}
    if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);toast(`${label} copié`,'success',2200);return true;}
    const input=document.createElement('textarea');input.value=text;input.setAttribute('readonly','');input.style.position='fixed';input.style.opacity='0';document.body.appendChild(input);input.select();
    const ok=document.execCommand('copy');input.remove();if(!ok)throw new Error('Copie indisponible');toast(`${label} copié`,'success',2200);return true;
  }catch(error){toast(text,'info',6000);return false;}
}

async function copyLink(url){
  if(!url)return false;
  try{
    if(window.AndroidBridge&&typeof window.AndroidBridge.copyText==='function'){
      window.AndroidBridge.copyText(url);return true;
    }
    if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);toast('Lien copié dans le presse-papiers','success',2200);return true;}
    const input=document.createElement('textarea');input.value=url;input.setAttribute('readonly','');input.style.position='fixed';input.style.opacity='0';document.body.appendChild(input);input.select();
    const ok=document.execCommand('copy');input.remove();if(!ok)throw new Error('Copie indisponible');toast('Lien copié dans le presse-papiers','success',2200);return true;
  }catch(error){toast(url,'info',6000);return false;}
}

async function confirmExternalNavigation(rawUrl,label='lien externe'){
  let target;
  try{target=new URL(rawUrl,location.href);}catch(e){toast('Lien invalide','error');return false;}
  if(!['http:','https:'].includes(target.protocol)){toast('Type de lien non pris en charge','error');return false;}
  const isGithub=/(^|\.)github\.com$/i.test(target.hostname)||/(^|\.)githubusercontent\.com$/i.test(target.hostname);
  const host=target.hostname;
  const message=isGithub
    ? `Tu vas quitter GitHub Studio pour ouvrir ce lien GitHub :\n${host}\n\nContinuer ?`
    : `Tu vas ouvrir un lien externe (${host}) hors de GitHub Studio. Continuer ?`;
  closeModal();
  const confirmed=await confirmModal(message,{title:'Confirmer l’ouverture du lien',confirmLabel:'Ouvrir le lien',danger:false});
  if(!confirmed)return false;
  closeOverflowMenus();
  try{
    if(window.AndroidBridge&&typeof window.AndroidBridge.openExternalUrl==='function')window.AndroidBridge.openExternalUrl(target.href);
    else location.assign(target.href);
    return true;
  }catch(error){showError('Ouverture impossible',`${label} : ${friendly(error)}`);return false;}
}

document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
  const anchor=event.target.closest?.('a[href]');
  if(!anchor)return;
  const raw=anchor.href;
  if(!/^https?:/i.test(raw))return;
  let target;
  try{target=new URL(raw,location.href);}catch(e){return;}
  if(target.origin===location.origin)return;
  event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();
  confirmExternalNavigation(target.href,anchor.getAttribute('aria-label')||anchor.title||anchor.textContent.trim()||'lien externe');
},true);

async function rerunWorkflowRun(fullName, runId, btn, runLabel = `run #${runId}`){
  const confirmed = await confirmModal(`Relancer ${runLabel} ? GitHub créera une nouvelle exécution à partir du workflow et de ses paramètres d’origine.`, { confirmLabel: 'Relancer', danger: false, title: 'Confirmer le rerun' });
  if (!confirmed) return false;
  setBtnLoading(btn, true);
  try{
    await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${runId}/rerun`, { method: 'POST' });
    toast('Workflow relancé', 'success');
    return true;
  } catch(err){
    showError('Erreur de relance', friendly(err));
    return false;
  } finally {
    setBtnLoading(btn, false, ico('refresh', 14));
  }
}


function closeOverflowMenus(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.overflow-menu.open').forEach(m => {
    m.classList.remove('open');
    if (m.id === 'editorOverflowMenu'){
      m.style.position = ''; m.style.top = ''; m.style.bottom = ''; m.style.left = ''; m.style.right = ''; m.style.maxHeight = ''; m.style.overflowY = '';
      document.getElementById('editorMoreBtn')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    }
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.screen-overflow-menu.open').forEach(menu=>menu.classList.remove('open'));
  document.getElementById('screenOverflowBtn')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
  document.getElementById('editorMoreMenu')?.classList.remove('open');
  document.getElementById('editorMoreBtn')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
}
function positionEditorOverflowMenu(){
  const menu = document.getElementById('editorOverflowMenu');
  const button = document.getElementById('editorMoreBtn');
  if (!menu || !button || !menu.classList.contains('open')) return;
  const buttonRect = button.getBoundingClientRect();
  menu.style.position = 'fixed';
  menu.style.top = '0px'; menu.style.bottom = 'auto'; menu.style.left = '0px'; menu.style.right = 'auto';
  menu.style.maxHeight = `${Math.max(120, window.innerHeight - 16)}px`;
  menu.style.overflowY = 'auto';
  const menuRect = menu.getBoundingClientRect();
  const gap = 8, edge = 8;
  const canOpenAbove = buttonRect.top - menuRect.height - gap >= edge;
  const top = canOpenAbove ? buttonRect.top - menuRect.height - gap : Math.min(window.innerHeight - menuRect.height - edge, buttonRect.bottom + gap);
  const left = Math.min(window.innerWidth - menuRect.width - edge, Math.max(edge, buttonRect.right - menuRect.width));
  menu.style.top = `${Math.max(edge, top)}px`;
  menu.style.left = `${left}px`;
}
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const menu = document.getElementById('overflowMenu');
  const btn = document.getElementById('overflowBtn');
  if (menu && menu.classList.contains('open') && !menu.contains(e.target) && e.target !== btn && !(btn && btn.contains(e.target))){
    menu.classList.remove('open');
  }
  const editorMenu = document.getElementById('editorOverflowMenu');
  const editorBtn = document.getElementById('editorMoreBtn');
  const compactEditorMenu = document.getElementById('editorMoreMenu');
  if ((editorMenu && editorMenu.classList.contains('open') && !editorMenu.contains(e.target) && !(editorBtn && editorBtn.contains(e.target))) || (compactEditorMenu && compactEditorMenu.classList.contains('open') && !compactEditorMenu.contains(e.target) && !(editorBtn && editorBtn.contains(e.target)))) closeOverflowMenus();
  const screenMenu = document.getElementById('screenOverflowMenu');
  const screenBtn = document.getElementById('screenOverflowBtn');
  if (screenMenu && screenMenu.classList.contains('open') && !screenMenu.contains(e.target) && !(screenBtn && screenBtn.contains(e.target))) closeOverflowMenus();
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeOverflowMenus(); });
window.addEventListener('resize', positionEditorOverflowMenu);
document.getElementById('screenBody').addEventListener('scroll', () => {
  const menu = document.getElementById('editorOverflowMenu');
  if (menu && menu.classList.contains('open')) closeOverflowMenus();
}, { passive: true });

/**
 * Modale "plein texte" (logs, erreurs) : contenu affiché dans un textarea
 * sélectionnable (copie garantie), bouton plein écran, et menu ⋮ regroupant
 * Exporter / Partager / Ouvrir sur GitHub pour ne pas surcharger l'écran de
 * boutons qu'on peut louper.
 */
function renderTextModal(title, text, { filename = 'export.txt', jobUrl = null } = {}){
  window.__modalText = text;
  const menuItems = [
    { label: 'Exporter (.txt)', icon: 'download', fn: () => { downloadText(filename, window.__modalText); closeOverflowMenus(); } },
    { label: 'Partager', icon: 'share', fn: () => { shareText(filename, window.__modalText); closeOverflowMenus(); } }
  ];
  if (jobUrl) menuItems.push({ label: 'Ouvrir sur GitHub', icon: 'external', fn: () => { closeOverflowMenus(); confirmExternalNavigation(jobUrl,'log GitHub Actions'); } });
  const menuHTML = menuItems.map((m, i) => `<div class="overflow-item" data-idx="${i}">${ico(m.icon, 15)}<span>${escapeHtml(m.label)}</span></div>`).join('');

  const o = ensureOverlay();
  const wasOpenText = o.classList.contains('open');
  o.innerHTML = `<div class="modal-box" id="textModalBox">
      <div class="modal-header">
        <span>${title}</span>
        <div class="modal-header-actions">
          <button class="modal-icon-btn" id="fsToggleBtn" title="Plein écran">${ico('expand', 17)}</button>
          <div class="overflow-wrap">
            <button class="modal-icon-btn" id="overflowBtn" title="Plus d'options">${ico('more', 17)}</button>
            <div class="overflow-menu" id="overflowMenu">${menuHTML}</div>
          </div>
          <button class="modal-icon-btn" id="mClose" title="Fermer">${ico('close', 17)}</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="modal-body">
        <textarea class="modal-textarea" id="modalTextArea" readonly>${escapeHtml(text)}</textarea>
        <button class="btn btn-block" id="copyPrimaryBtn" style="margin-top:10px;">${ico('copy', 15)} Copier</button>
        <div class="modal-hint">Astuce : si la copie automatique ne marche pas sur cet appareil, le texte reste sélectionné — utilise le menu de copie qui apparaît au-dessus du clavier.</div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  document.getElementById('mClose').onclick = closeModal;
  document.getElementById('fsToggleBtn').onclick = () => {
    const box = document.getElementById('textModalBox');
    const fs = box.classList.toggle('modal-fullscreen');
    document.getElementById('fsToggleBtn').innerHTML = ico(fs ? 'collapse' : 'expand', 17);
  };
  document.getElementById('overflowBtn').onclick = (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); document.getElementById('overflowMenu').classList.toggle('open'); };
  document.getElementById('copyPrimaryBtn').onclick = () => copyFromTextarea('modalTextArea');
  document.querySelectorAll('#overflowMenu .overflow-item').forEach(el => {
    el.addEventListener('click', () => menuItems[Number(el.dataset.idx)].fn());
  });
  presentModal(o, wasOpenText);
}

function showError(title, text){
  renderTextModal(`${ico('alert', 15)} ${escapeHtml(title)}`, text, { filename: 'erreur.txt' });
}

/**
 * Feuille d'actions générique (liste de gros boutons tactiles dans une modale) —
 * utilisée pour le menu ⋮ des runs (Git : reset / branche / revert).
 * Utilise addEventListener + tableau JS (pas de onclick en chaîne de caractères)
 * pour ne jamais casser sur un nom contenant des apostrophes ou guillemets.
 * Style compact volontaire : une ligne icône + libellé par action, sans pavé
 * de description — comme un vrai menu contextuel, pas un tutoriel.
 */
function openActionSheet(title, items, context={}){
  const info=context.url?{url:context.url,title:context.title||title}:currentScreenShareInfo();
  const explicitItems=(items||[]).filter(item=>!(info?.url&&isGitHubOpenMenuLabel(item.label)));
  const allItems=uniqueActionItems([...explicitItems,...linkMenuItems(info?.url,info?.title),
    {label:'Aller à l’écran d’accueil',icon:'home',fn:goToHome},
    {label:'Actualiser cet écran',icon:'refresh',fn:refreshCurrentScreen}]);
  const bodyHTML = allItems.map((it, i) => `
    <div class="sheet-item ${it.danger ? 'danger' : ''}" data-idx="${i}">
      <span class="sheet-ico">${ico(it.icon, 17)}</span>
      <span class="sheet-label">${escapeHtml(it.label)}</span>
    </div>`).join('');
  openModal({ title: escapeHtml(title), bodyHTML: `<div style="margin:-15px -16px;">${bodyHTML}</div>`,footerHTML:'<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="actionSheetClose">Fermer</button>' });
  document.getElementById('actionSheetClose').onclick=closeModal;
  document.querySelectorAll('.modal-body .sheet-item').forEach(el => {
    el.addEventListener('click', () => {
      closeModal();
      allItems[Number(el.dataset.idx)].fn?.();
    });
  });
}

function confirmModal(message, { confirmLabel = 'Confirmer', danger = true, title = 'Confirmation' } = {}){
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    openModal({
      title,
      bodyHTML: `<div class="confirm-banner ${danger?'danger':''}"><span class="confirm-banner-icon">${ico(danger?'alert':'info',20)}</span><div class="confirm-banner-copy"><strong>${escapeHtml(title)}</strong><p>${escapeHtml(message)}</p></div></div>`,
      footerHTML: `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="cNo">Annuler</button><button class="btn ${danger ? 'btn-danger' : 'btn-primary'} btn-sm" id="cYes">${escapeHtml(confirmLabel)}</button>`
    });
    document.getElementById('cNo').onclick = () => { closeModal(); resolve(false); };
    document.getElementById('cYes').onclick = () => { closeModal(); resolve(true); };
  });
}

 function promptModal(message, { placeholder = '', value = '', okLabel = 'Créer', title = 'Nouveau fichier' } = {}){
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    openModal({
      title,
      bodyHTML: `<p style="color:var(--text-muted);margin-bottom:6px;">${escapeHtml(message)}</p><input type="text" id="pInput" class="modal-input" placeholder="${escapeHtml(placeholder)}" value="${escapeHtml(value)}">`,
      footerHTML: `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="pNo">Annuler</button><button class="btn btn-sm" id="pYes">${escapeHtml(okLabel)}</button>`
    });
    const input = document.getElementById('pInput');
    setTimeout(() => input.focus(), 50);
    const finish = v => { closeModal(); resolve(v); };
    document.getElementById('pNo').onclick = () => finish(null);
    document.getElementById('pYes').onclick = () => finish(input.value.trim() || null);
    input.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') finish(input.value.trim() || null); });
  });
}

function setBtnLoading(btn, loading, idleHTML){
  if (!btn) return;
  if (loading){ btn.dataset.html = btn.innerHTML; btn.innerHTML = `<span class="spin"></span> Patientez…`; btn.disabled = true; }
  else{ btn.innerHTML = idleHTML ?? btn.dataset.html ?? btn.innerHTML; btn.disabled = false; }
}

/* ============================================================
   ENCODAGE / DÉCODAGE ROBUSTE
============================================================ */
function b64ToUtf8(b64){
  try{
    const clean = b64.replace(/[\r\n\s]/g, ''); // GitHub renvoie le base64 avec des retours à la ligne : atob() plante sinon
    const binary = atob(clean);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    return new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: false }).decode(bytes);
  } catch(e){
    throw new Error("Le contenu de ce fichier n'a pas pu être décodé (encodage invalide ou fichier binaire).");
  }
}
function utf8ToB64(str){
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
  let binary = '';
  const chunk = 0x8000;
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunk) binary += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, i + chunk));
  return btoa(binary);
}
function encodePath(path){ return path.split('/').filter(Boolean).map(encodeURIComponent).join('/'); }
function joinPath(base, name){ return base ? `${base}/${name}` : name; }

/* ============================================================
   APPEL API GITHUB (wrapper commun)
============================================================ */
function friendly(err){
  if (!err) return 'Erreur inconnue';
  if (/failed to fetch|networkerror|load failed/i.test(err.message || '')) return "Connexion impossible. Vérifie ta connexion internet et réessaie.";
  return err.message;
}

async function gh(path, opts = {}){
  let res;
  try{
    res = await fetch(`https://api.github.com${path}`, {
      cache: 'no-store',
      ...opts,
      headers: { Authorization: `token ${authToken}`, ...(opts.headers || {}) }
    });
  } catch(e){
    throw new Error("Connexion impossible. Vérifie ta connexion internet et réessaie.");
  }
  let data = null;
  try{ data = await res.json(); } catch(e){ data = null; }
  if (!res.ok){
    const msg = (data && data.message) ? data.message : `Erreur GitHub (statut ${res.status})`;
    throw new Error(msg);
  }
  return data;
}

async function ghAll(path, itemsKey = null, perPage = 100, maxPages = 1000){
  const all = [];
  const separator = path.includes('?') ? '&' : '?';
  for(let page = 1; page <= maxPages; page++){
    const data = await gh(`${path}${separator}per_page=${perPage}&page=${page}`);
    const items = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (itemsKey && Array.isArray(data?.[itemsKey]) ? data[itemsKey] : []);
    all.push(...items);
    if(items.length < perPage) break;
  }
  return all;
}

/* ============================================================
   NAVIGATION PAR ÉCRANS
============================================================ */
const nav = { stack: [] };
const NAVIGATION_STATE_KEY='nps_gh_studio_navigation_v1';
let navScrollPersistTimer=null;

function persistNavigationState(){
  try{
    if(!authToken){localStorage.removeItem(NAVIGATION_STATE_KEY);return;}
    const stack=nav.stack.map(item=>({renderer:item.renderFn?.name,title:item.title,args:item.args||[],subtitle:item.subtitle||'',scrollTop:Number(item.scrollTop)||0})).filter(item=>item.renderer);
    localStorage.setItem(NAVIGATION_STATE_KEY,JSON.stringify({version:1,stack,branches:repoBranchSelection,workflows:actionsWorkflowSelection,releases:releasePanelSelection,repoFilter:typeof repoFilter==='string'?repoFilter:'all'}));
  }catch(_){ }
}

function restoreNavigationState(){
  try{
    const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NAVIGATION_STATE_KEY)||'null');
    if(!saved||saved.version!==1||!Array.isArray(saved.stack))return false;
    const stack=saved.stack.map(item=>{
      const renderFn=typeof item?.renderer==='string'?window[item.renderer]:null;
      if(typeof renderFn!=='function')return null;
      return {renderFn,title:String(item.title||'GitHub Studio'),args:Array.isArray(item.args)?item.args:[],subtitle:String(item.subtitle||''),scrollTop:Math.max(0,Number(item.scrollTop)||0)};
    }).filter(Boolean);
    if(!stack.length)return false;
    Object.assign(repoBranchSelection,saved.branches||{});
    Object.assign(actionsWorkflowSelection,saved.workflows||{});
    Object.assign(releasePanelSelection,saved.releases||{});
    if(typeof saved.repoFilter==='string')repoFilter=saved.repoFilter;
    nav.stack=stack;
    return true;
  }catch(_){
    try{localStorage.removeItem(NAVIGATION_STATE_KEY);}catch(__){}
    return false;
  }
}

document.getElementById('screenBody').addEventListener('scroll',()=>{
  const current=nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1];if(!current)return;
  current.scrollTop=document.getElementById('screenBody').scrollTop;
  clearTimeout(navScrollPersistTimer);
  navScrollPersistTimer=setTimeout(persistNavigationState,350);
},{passive:true});
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)persistNavigationState();});
window.addEventListener('pagehide',persistNavigationState);

function navTo(renderFn, title, args = [], subtitle = ''){
  nav.stack.push({ renderFn, title, args, subtitle, scrollTop:0 });
  persistNavigationState();
  paint(true);
}
function navBack(){
  if (nav.stack.length <= 1) return;
  nav.stack.pop();
  persistNavigationState();
  paint(false);
}
function navReset(renderFn, title, args = [], subtitle = ''){
  nav.stack = [{ renderFn, title, args, subtitle, scrollTop:0 }];
  persistNavigationState();
  paint(false);
}
/** Remplace titre/args de l'écran courant sans empiler une nouvelle entrée
 * (utile après une action qui change l'identité de l'écran, ex. renommer
 * un dépôt : on reste sur le même écran mais avec un nouveau fullName). */
function navReplaceTop(title, args, subtitle){
  const top = nav.stack[nav.stack.length - 1];
  top.title = title;
  top.args = args;
  if (subtitle !== undefined) top.subtitle = subtitle;
  persistNavigationState();
  document.getElementById('screenTitle').textContent = title;
  if (subtitle !== undefined) document.getElementById('screenSubtitle').textContent = subtitle;
}
function paint(isForward){
  stopLiveBuildMonitor();
  if(activeMediaObjectUrl){ URL.revokeObjectURL(activeMediaObjectUrl); activeMediaObjectUrl = null; }
  const top = nav.stack[nav.stack.length - 1];
  document.body.classList.toggle('editor-screen', top.renderFn===renderEditor);
  document.getElementById('backBtn').style.display = nav.stack.length > 1 ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('homeBtn').style.display = authToken ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('screenTitle').textContent = top.title;
  document.getElementById('screenSubtitle').textContent = top.subtitle || '';
  setScreenActions('');
  const body = document.getElementById('screenBody');
  const tx = prepareScreenTransition(isForward);
  body.className = 'screen-body';
  body.innerHTML = skeletonHTML();
  if (tx) runScreenTransition(tx);
  const restoreScroll=()=>{if(nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1]===top)body.scrollTop=top.scrollTop||0;};
  const rendered=top.renderFn(...top.args);
  if(rendered&&typeof rendered.then==='function')rendered.then(()=>requestAnimationFrame(restoreScroll),()=>requestAnimationFrame(restoreScroll));
  else requestAnimationFrame(restoreScroll);
}
function currentScreenShareInfo(){
  const top=nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1];
  if(!top)return null;
  const args=top.args||[];
  const repoPattern=/^[^/\s]+\/[^/\s]+$/;
  if(top.renderFn===renderRepos){
    const url=currentUser?.login?`https://github.com/${encodeURIComponent(currentUser.login)}?tab=repositories`:'https://github.com/faustinpataule12-art/GitHub-Studio';
    return {url,title:top.title||'Dépôts GitHub'};
  }
  const fullName=String(args.find(value=>typeof value==='string'&&repoPattern.test(value))||'');
  if(!fullName)return null;
  const repoUrl=`https://github.com/${fullName}`;
  if(top.renderFn===renderRepoHome)return {url:repoUrl,title:fullName};
  if(top.renderFn===renderFiles){
    const path=args[1]||'',branch=args[2]||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main';
    const suffix=path?`/${path.split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/')}`:'';
    return {url:`${repoUrl}/tree/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}${suffix}`,title:path||fullName};
  }
  if(top.renderFn===renderEditor){
    const path=args[1]||'',branch=args[3]||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main';
    return {url:`${repoUrl}/blob/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}/${path.split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/')}`,title:path.split('/').pop()||fullName};
  }
  if(top.renderFn===renderMediaPreview||top.renderFn===renderBinaryFile){
    const path=args[1]||'',branch=args[2]||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main';
    return {url:`${repoUrl}/blob/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}/${path.split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/')}`,title:path.split('/').pop()||fullName};
  }
  if(top.renderFn===renderActions){
    const branch=args[1]||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main';
    return {url:`${repoUrl}/actions?query=${encodeURIComponent(`branch:${branch}`)}`,title:`Actions · ${fullName}`};
  }
  if(top.renderFn===renderReleases){const artifacts=releasePanelSelection[fullName]==='artifacts';return artifacts?{url:`${repoUrl}/actions`,title:`Artefacts Actions · ${fullName}`}:{url:`${repoUrl}/releases`,title:`Releases · ${fullName}`};}
  if(top.renderFn===renderRunDetail){
    const sha=args[1]||'';
    return {url:sha?`${repoUrl}/commit/${encodeURIComponent(sha)}/checks`:`${repoUrl}/actions`,title:`Build · ${fullName}`};
  }
  return {url:repoUrl,title:top.title||fullName};
}
function goToHome(){navReset(renderRepos,'Dépôts');}
function refreshCurrentScreen(){
  const top=nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1];if(!top)return;
  const rerender=()=>{stopLiveBuildMonitor();top.renderFn(...top.args);};
  if(top.renderFn===renderEditor&&document.getElementById('dirtyBadge')?.style.display==='inline-block'){
    confirmModal('Ce fichier contient des modifications non enregistrées. Actualiser abandonnera ces changements. Continuer ?',{title:'Actualiser le fichier',confirmLabel:'Actualiser',danger:true}).then(ok=>{if(ok)rerender();});
    return;
  }
  rerender();
}
function makeMenuItem(menu,label,icon,fn){
  const button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.className='screen-menu-item';button.setAttribute('role','menuitem');button.innerHTML=`${ico(icon,16)}<span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span>`;button.onclick=()=>{closeOverflowMenus();fn?.();};menu.appendChild(button);return button;
}
function linkMenuItems(url,title){
  if(!url)return [];
  return [
    {label:'Copier le lien GitHub',icon:'copy',fn:()=>copyLink(url)},
    {label:'Ouvrir dans GitHub',icon:'github',fn:()=>confirmExternalNavigation(url,title||'page GitHub')},
    {label:'Partager le lien',icon:'share',fn:()=>shareLink(url,title||'GitHub')}
  ];
}
function normalizeMenuLabel(label){return String(label||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');}
function isGitHubOpenMenuLabel(label){const key=normalizeMenuLabel(label);return /ouvrir.*(github|depot)|github.*ouvrir|open.*github|github.*open/.test(key);}
function uniqueActionItems(items){
  const seen=new Set();
  return items.filter(item=>{const key=normalizeMenuLabel(item.label);if(key&&seen.has(key))return false;if(key)seen.add(key);return true;});
}
document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
});

function setScreenActions(html='',extraItems=[]){
  const host=document.getElementById('screenActions');if(!host)return;
  host.innerHTML='';
  const wrapper=document.createElement('div');wrapper.className='overflow-wrap screen-overflow-wrap';
  const trigger=document.createElement('button');trigger.type='button';trigger.className='icon-btn';trigger.id='screenOverflowBtn';trigger.title='Actions de cette page';trigger.setAttribute('aria-label','Actions de cette page');trigger.setAttribute('aria-haspopup','menu');trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');trigger.innerHTML=ico('more',18);
  const menu=document.createElement('div');menu.className='screen-overflow-menu';menu.id='screenOverflowMenu';menu.setAttribute('role','menu');
  wrapper.append(trigger,menu);host.appendChild(wrapper);
  const info=currentScreenShareInfo();
  const automaticLinks=info?linkMenuItems(info.url,info.title):[];
  const seenLabels=new Set(automaticLinks.map(item=>normalizeMenuLabel(item.label)));
  automaticLinks.forEach(item=>makeMenuItem(menu,item.label,item.icon,item.fn));
  const temp=document.createElement('div');temp.innerHTML=html||'';
  let hasRefresh=false;
  [...temp.children].forEach(control=>{
    const label=control.getAttribute('aria-label')||control.title||control.textContent.trim()||'Action';
    const labelKey=normalizeMenuLabel(label);
    if((info?.url&&isGitHubOpenMenuLabel(label))||seenLabels.has(labelKey))return;
    if(labelKey)seenLabels.add(labelKey);
    if(/actualis|refresh/i.test(label+' '+control.id))hasRefresh=true;
    const isDanger=control.classList.contains('btn-danger')||control.dataset.danger==='true';
    control.classList.remove('icon-btn','btn','btn-primary','btn-secondary','btn-danger','btn-sm');control.classList.add('screen-menu-item');
    if(isDanger)control.classList.add('danger');control.setAttribute('role','menuitem');
    if(!control.textContent.trim())control.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span>`);
    menu.appendChild(control);
  });
  (extraItems||[]).forEach(item=>{
    const labelKey=normalizeMenuLabel(item.label);
    if((info?.url&&isGitHubOpenMenuLabel(item.label))||seenLabels.has(labelKey))return;
    if(labelKey)seenLabels.add(labelKey);
    const button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.className=`screen-menu-item${item.danger?' danger':''}`;button.setAttribute('role','menuitem');
    button.innerHTML=`${ico(item.icon||'chevronRight',16)}<span>${escapeHtml(item.label)}</span>`;button.onclick=()=>{closeOverflowMenus();item.fn?.();};menu.appendChild(button);
    if(/actualis|refresh/i.test(item.label))hasRefresh=true;
  });
  const topScreen=nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1];
  if(topScreen&&topScreen.renderFn===renderRepos)makeMenuItem(menu,'Mes tokens','key',()=>navTo(renderSavedTokens,'Mes tokens',[],'Stockés sur ce téléphone'));
  makeMenuItem(menu,'Aller à l’écran d’accueil','home',goToHome);
  if(!hasRefresh)makeMenuItem(menu,'Actualiser cet écran','refresh',refreshCurrentScreen);
  menu.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(event.target.closest('.screen-menu-item'))setTimeout(closeOverflowMenus,0);});
  trigger.onclick=event=>{event.stopPropagation();const opening=!menu.classList.contains('open');closeOverflowMenus();if(opening){menu.classList.add('open');trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');}};
}

function setBody(html){
  const body=document.getElementById('screenBody');
  const isLoading=/^\s*<div class="loading-row"/.test(html);
  /* on n'anime le contenu que lorsqu'il remplace un chargement (pas à chaque rafraîchissement) */
  const wasLoading=!!body.querySelector('.skel-screen,.loading-row');
  if(isLoading)html=skeletonHTML();
  else if(wasLoading)armStagger();
  body.innerHTML=html;
  if(!isLoading&&wasLoading&&!REDUCE_MOTION.matches&&typeof body.animate==='function'){
    Array.from(body.children).forEach(el=>{
      if(el.matches(STAGGER_SEL))return;
      el.animate([{opacity:0},{opacity:1}],{duration:220,easing:'ease-out'});
    });
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   ANIMATIONS : écrans, squelettes, cascade, toucher
============================================================ */
const REDUCE_MOTION=window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');

/* --- 1) Glissement d'écran façon appli native --- */
let screenTx=null;
function endScreenTransition(){
  if(!screenTx)return;
  const {anims,ghost,body}=screenTx;screenTx=null;
  anims.forEach(a=>{try{a.cancel();}catch(_){}});
  if(ghost)ghost.remove();
  ['background','box-shadow','z-index','will-change'].forEach(p=>body.style.removeProperty(p));
}
function prepareScreenTransition(isForward){
  endScreenTransition();
  const body=document.getElementById('screenBody');
  if(!body||REDUCE_MOTION.matches||typeof body.animate!=='function'||!body.firstElementChild)return null;
  const r=body.getBoundingClientRect();
  if(r.width<40||r.height<40)return null;
  const ghost=body.cloneNode(true);
  ghost.removeAttribute('id');
  ghost.querySelectorAll('[id]').forEach(n=>n.removeAttribute('id'));
  ghost.querySelectorAll('video,audio,iframe,script').forEach(n=>n.remove());
  ghost.className='screen-body screen-ghost';
  ghost.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  ghost.inert=true;
  ghost.style.cssText=`position:fixed;left:${r.left}px;top:${r.top}px;width:${r.width}px;height:${r.height}px;margin:0;box-sizing:border-box;z-index:${isForward?1:3};`;
  const scroll=body.scrollTop;
  document.body.appendChild(ghost);
  ghost.scrollTop=scroll;
  return {ghost,body,isForward};
}
function runScreenTransition({ghost,body,isForward}){
  const D=340,E='cubic-bezier(.22,.8,.24,1)';
  const shadow='0 0 28px rgba(0,0,0,.42)';
  body.style.background='var(--bg)';
  body.style.willChange='transform';
  const anims=[];
  if(isForward){
    body.style.zIndex='2';body.style.boxShadow=shadow;
    anims.push(ghost.animate([{transform:'translate3d(0,0,0)',opacity:1},{transform:'translate3d(-22%,0,0)',opacity:.4}],{duration:D,easing:E,fill:'forwards'}));
    anims.push(body.animate([{transform:'translate3d(100%,0,0)'},{transform:'translate3d(0,0,0)'}],{duration:D,easing:E}));
  }else{
    body.style.zIndex='1';ghost.style.boxShadow=shadow;
    anims.push(ghost.animate([{transform:'translate3d(0,0,0)'},{transform:'translate3d(100%,0,0)'}],{duration:D,easing:E,fill:'forwards'}));
    anims.push(body.animate([{transform:'translate3d(-22%,0,0)',opacity:.4},{transform:'translate3d(0,0,0)',opacity:1}],{duration:D,easing:E}));
  }
  const tx={anims,ghost,body};screenTx=tx;
  const done=()=>{if(screenTx===tx)endScreenTransition();};
  anims[1].onfinish=done;anims[1].oncancel=done;
  setTimeout(done,D+150);
}

/* --- 3) Squelettes de chargement --- */
function skeletonHTML(){
  const top=nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1];
  const fn=top&&top.renderFn;
  const label='<span class="skel-sr">Chargement…</span>';
  const open='<div class="skel-screen" role="status" aria-label="Chargement">';
  if(fn===renderRepoHome){
    return `${open}${label}<span class="skel skel-hero"></span>${'<span class="skel skel-tile"></span>'.repeat(5)}</div>`;
  }
  if(fn===renderEditor||fn===renderRunDetail||fn===renderBinaryFile||fn===renderMediaPreview){
    const widths=[92,78,86,64,90,72,58,84,70,46];
    return `${open}${label}<span class="skel skel-bar"></span>${widths.map(w=>`<span class="skel skel-code" style="width:${w}%"></span>`).join('')}</div>`;
  }
  const w1=[62,48,70,55,66,44,58],w2=[38,30,45,34,40,28,36];
  const rows=w1.map((w,i)=>`<div class="skel-row"><span class="skel skel-ico"></span><span class="skel-lines"><span class="skel skel-line" style="width:${w}%"></span><span class="skel skel-line short" style="width:${w2[i]}%"></span></span></div>`).join('');
  return `${open}${label}<div class="skel-chips"><span class="skel skel-chip"></span><span class="skel skel-chip"></span><span class="skel skel-chip"></span></div>${rows}</div>`;
}

/* --- 4) Apparition en cascade des listes (30 ms d'écart) --- */
const STAGGER_SEL='.list-item,.nav-tile,.step-item,.zip-entry-row,.card,.tok-card';
const staggerState={until:0,count:0};
function armStagger(){staggerState.until=performance.now()+700;staggerState.count=0;}
function staggerApply(root){
  if(REDUCE_MOTION.matches)return;
  const found=[];
  if(root.matches&&root.matches(STAGGER_SEL))found.push(root);
  if(root.querySelectorAll)root.querySelectorAll(STAGGER_SEL).forEach(n=>found.push(n));
  for(const el of found){
    if(staggerState.count>=14)break;
    if(el.classList.contains('stg')||el.classList.contains('release-card'))continue;
    el.style.setProperty('--stg',(staggerState.count*30)+'ms');
    el.classList.add('stg');
    staggerState.count++;
  }
}
new MutationObserver(records=>{
  if(performance.now()>staggerState.until)return;
  records.forEach(rec=>rec.addedNodes.forEach(n=>{if(n.nodeType===1)staggerApply(n);}));
}).observe(document.getElementById('screenBody'),{childList:true,subtree:true});

/* --- 5) Réaction au toucher : onde (ripple) partant du doigt --- */
(function initRipple(){
  const SEL='.btn,.icon-btn,.account-btn,.back-btn,.chip,.wf-chip,.list-item,.nav-tile,.sheet-item,.screen-menu-item,.overflow-item,.modal-close,.modal-icon-btn,.zip-entry-row,.step-item,.home-usage-card,.editor-find-menu-item';
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(REDUCE_MOTION.matches||(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0))return;
    const el=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest(SEL);
    if(!el||el.disabled||el.getAttribute('aria-disabled')==='true')return;
    const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
    if(cs.cursor==='default'||cs.display==='none')return;
    const rect=el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(rect.width<8||rect.height<8||rect.width*rect.height>900000)return;
    const x=e.clientX-rect.left,y=e.clientY-rect.top;
    const size=Math.hypot(Math.max(x,rect.width-x),Math.max(y,rect.height-y))*2;
    const host=document.createElement('span');
    host.className='rip-host';host.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    host.style.cssText=`left:${rect.left}px;top:${rect.top}px;width:${rect.width}px;height:${rect.height}px;border-radius:${cs.borderRadius};`;
    const dot=document.createElement('span');
    dot.className='rip-dot';
    dot.style.cssText=`width:${size}px;height:${size}px;left:${x-size/2}px;top:${y-size/2}px;`;
    host.appendChild(dot);document.body.appendChild(host);
    void dot.offsetWidth;dot.classList.add('go');
    const t0=performance.now();let released=false;
    const release=()=>{
      if(released)return;released=true;
      window.removeEventListener('pointerup',release,true);
      window.removeEventListener('pointercancel',release,true);
      setTimeout(()=>{dot.classList.add('out');setTimeout(()=>host.remove(),380);},Math.max(0,150-(performance.now()-t0)));
    };
    window.addEventListener('pointerup',release,true);
    window.addEventListener('pointercancel',release,true);
    setTimeout(release,2500);
  },{passive:true});
})();

document.getElementById('backBtn').addEventListener('click', navBack);
document.getElementById('homeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>navReset(renderRepos,'Dépôts'));


/* ============================================================
   MES TOKENS — enregistrés uniquement dans le stockage du téléphone
   (localStorage de l'appli) : aucune requête réseau, aucun envoi.
============================================================ */
const SAVED_TOKENS_KEY='nps_gh_studio_saved_tokens_v1';
function loadSavedTokens(){
  try{
    const list=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SAVED_TOKENS_KEY)||'[]');
    return Array.isArray(list)?list.filter(t=>t&&typeof t.token==='string'&&t.token&&typeof t.id==='string'):[];
  }catch(_){return [];}
}
function storeSavedTokens(list){
  try{localStorage.setItem(SAVED_TOKENS_KEY,JSON.stringify(list));return true;}
  catch(_){toast('Enregistrement impossible sur cet appareil.','error');return false;}
}
function maskToken(token){
  const t=String(token||'');
  return t.length>10?`${t.slice(0,4)}••••••••${t.slice(-4)}`:'••••••••';
}
function renderSavedTokens(){
  const list=loadSavedTokens();
  const cards=list.map(item=>{
    const active=!!authToken&&authToken===item.token;
    const id=escapeHtml(item.id),name=escapeHtml(item.name||'Sans nom');
    return `<div class="tok-card${active?' active':''}" data-tok-id="${id}">
      <div class="tok-head"><span class="tok-ico">${ico('key',18)}</span><div class="tok-main"><div class="tok-name">${name}</div><div class="tok-mask">${escapeHtml(maskToken(item.token))}</div></div></div>
      <div class="tok-actions">
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm" data-tok-copy="${id}" aria-label="Copier le token ${name}">${ico('copy',15)} Copier</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" data-tok-connect="${id}" ${active?'disabled':''} aria-label="Se connecter avec ${name}">${ico(active?'check':'user',15)} ${active?'Connecté':'Se connecter'}</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm tok-del" data-tok-del="${id}" aria-label="Supprimer le token ${name}" title="Supprimer">${ico('trash',16)}</button>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
  const empty=`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('key',30)}</div>Aucun token enregistré.<br>Appuie sur « Ajouter un token ».</div>`;
  setBody(`<div class="tok-note">${ico('lock',16)}<span>Les tokens restent enregistrés uniquement sur ce téléphone. Ils ne sont jamais envoyés ni déplacés.</span></div>
    <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-block tok-add" id="tokAddBtn">${ico('plus',16)} Ajouter un token</button>${cards||empty}`);
  document.getElementById('tokAddBtn').onclick=openAddTokenModal;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-tok-copy]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
    const item=loadSavedTokens().find(t=>t.id===b.dataset.tokCopy);
    if(item)copyTextValue(item.token,'Token');
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-tok-connect]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>connectSavedToken(b.dataset.tokConnect,b));
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-tok-del]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>deleteSavedToken(b.dataset.tokDel));
}
function openAddTokenModal(){
  openModal({
    title:'Ajouter un token',
    bodyHTML:`<label class="tok-label" for="tokNameInput">Nom du token</label>
      <input type="text" id="tokNameInput" class="modal-input tok-field" placeholder="Ex. Compte principal" maxlength="40" autocomplete="off">
      <label class="tok-label" for="tokValueInput">Token GitHub</label>
      <input type="password" id="tokValueInput" class="modal-input" placeholder="ghp_xxxxxxxxxxxx" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false">`,
    footerHTML:`<button type="button" class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="tokCancelBtn">Annuler</button><button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="tokSaveBtn">${ico('check',14)} Enregistrer</button>`
  });
  const nameInput=document.getElementById('tokNameInput'),valueInput=document.getElementById('tokValueInput');
  setTimeout(()=>nameInput&&nameInput.focus(),340);
  const save=()=>{
    const name=nameInput.value.trim(),token=valueInput.value.trim();
    if(!name){toast('Donne un nom au token.','error');nameInput.focus();return;}
    if(!token){toast('Colle le token à enregistrer.','error');valueInput.focus();return;}
    const list=loadSavedTokens();
    const same=list.find(t=>t.token===token);
    if(same){toast(`Ce token est déjà enregistré sous « ${same.name} ».`,'error',3200);return;}
    list.push({id:Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7),name,token,createdAt:Date.now()});
    if(!storeSavedTokens(list))return;
    closeModal();
    toast('Token enregistré','success',2000);
    const top=nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1];
    if(top&&top.renderFn===renderSavedTokens)renderSavedTokens();
  };
  document.getElementById('tokCancelBtn').onclick=closeModal;
  document.getElementById('tokSaveBtn').onclick=save;
  nameInput.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();valueInput.focus();}});
  valueInput.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();save();}});
}
async function deleteSavedToken(id){
  const list=loadSavedTokens(),item=list.find(t=>t.id===id);
  if(!item)return;
  const ok=await confirmModal(`Supprimer « ${item.name} » de ce téléphone ? Le token reste valide sur GitHub : il n’est pas révoqué.`,{title:'Supprimer le token',confirmLabel:'Supprimer',danger:true});
  if(!ok)return;
  if(!storeSavedTokens(list.filter(t=>t.id!==id)))return;
  toast('Token supprimé','success',1800);
  const top=nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1];
  if(top&&top.renderFn===renderSavedTokens)renderSavedTokens();
}
async function connectSavedToken(id,btn){
  const item=loadSavedTokens().find(t=>t.id===id);
  if(!item)return;
  const previous=authToken,idle=btn.innerHTML;
  setBtnLoading(btn,true);
  await connectWithToken(item.token);
  if(authToken!==item.token){
    if(previous&&!authToken)authToken=previous; /* échec : on garde la connexion précédente */
    if(btn.isConnected)setBtnLoading(btn,false,idle);
  }
}

document.getElementById('accountBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
  if (authToken && currentUser){
    openModal({
      title: 'Compte GitHub',
      bodyHTML: `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;">
          <img src="${escapeHtml(currentUser.avatar_url)}" style="width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:50%;">
          <div><div style="font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(currentUser.login)}</div><div style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:12px;">Connecté</div></div>
        </div>`,
      footerHTML: `<button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" id="disconnectBtn">Se déconnecter</button><button class="btn btn-sm" onclick="closeModal()">Fermer</button>`
    });
    document.getElementById('disconnectBtn').onclick = async () => {
      closeModal();
      const confirmed=await confirmModal(`Tu es connecté en tant que ${currentUser.login}. Confirmer la déconnexion de GitHub Studio ? Le token enregistré sur cet appareil sera supprimé.`,{title:'Confirmer la déconnexion',confirmLabel:'Se déconnecter',danger:true});
      if(!confirmed)return;
      authToken = null; currentUser = null; allRepos = [];
      localStorage.removeItem('nps_gh_studio_token');
      document.getElementById('connDot').classList.remove('on');
      document.getElementById('accountBtn').innerHTML = ico('lock', 16);
      document.getElementById('accountBtn').style.borderRadius = 'var(--radius-sm)';
      stopLiveBuildMonitor();
      closeModal();
      toast('Déconnecté', 'info', 2000);
      navReset(renderRepos, 'Dépôts');
    };
  } else {
    navTo(renderSavedTokens, 'Mes tokens', [], 'Stockés sur ce téléphone');
  }
});

function confirmExitApplication(){
  openModal({
    title:'Quitter GitHub Studio ?',
    bodyHTML:`<div class="exit-confirm-content"><span class="exit-confirm-icon">${ico('alert',22)}</span><div><strong>Fermer l’application ?</strong><p>Les modifications non enregistrées pourraient être perdues.</p></div></div>`,
    footerHTML:`<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="exitStayBtn">Rester</button><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm exit-confirm-btn" id="exitConfirmBtn">${ico('close',14)} Quitter</button>`
  });
  document.getElementById('exitStayBtn').onclick=closeModal;
  document.getElementById('exitConfirmBtn').onclick=()=>{
    closeModal();
    if(window.AndroidBridge&&typeof window.AndroidBridge.exitApp==='function')window.AndroidBridge.exitApp();
    else toast('Tu peux fermer cet onglet depuis le navigateur.', 'info', 3500);
  };
}
function githubStudioHandleBack(){
  const overlay=document.getElementById('modalOverlay');
  if(document.body.classList.contains('modal-open')||overlay?.classList.contains('open')){closeModal();return true;}
  if(nav.stack.length>1){navBack();return true;}
  confirmExitApplication();return true;
}
window.githubStudioHandleBack=githubStudioHandleBack;
window.addEventListener('github-studio-back',githubStudioHandleBack);

async function connectWithToken(token){
  if (!token){ toast('Entrez un token GitHub.', 'error'); return; }
  const btn = document.getElementById('connectModalBtn');
  setBtnLoading(btn, true);
  try{
    authToken = token;
    const [repos, user] = await Promise.all([
      ghAll('/user/repos?sort=updated&affiliation=owner,collaborator,organization_member'),
      gh('/user')
    ]);
    allRepos = repos;
    currentUser = user;
    usageArtifactsCache = { token, expiresAt: 0, data: null, promise: null };
    localStorage.setItem('nps_gh_studio_token', token);
    document.getElementById('connDot').classList.add('on');
    const accBtn = document.getElementById('accountBtn');
    accBtn.innerHTML = `<img src="${escapeHtml(user.avatar_url)}">`;
    accBtn.style.borderRadius = '50%';
    closeModal();
    toast(`Connecté en tant que ${user.login} · ${repos.length} dépôt(s)`, 'success');
    navReset(renderRepos, 'Dépôts');
  } catch(err){
