    authToken = null;
    setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Connexion');
    showError('Connexion impossible', friendly(err));
    return;
  }
  setBtnLoading(btn, false, 'Connexion');
}

/* ============================================================
   ÉCRAN 1 : DÉPÔTS
============================================================ */
let repoFilter = 'all'; // 'all' | 'public' | 'private'

const actionRunMetadataCache=new Map();
async function getActionRunMetadata(fullName,runId){
  if(!runId)return null;
  const key=`${fullName}:${runId}`;
  if(!actionRunMetadataCache.has(key))actionRunMetadataCache.set(key,gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${runId}`).catch(()=>null));
  return actionRunMetadataCache.get(key);
}

async function loadUsageArtifacts(repos, forceRefresh=false){
  const now=Date.now();
  if(!forceRefresh&&usageArtifactsCache.token===authToken&&usageArtifactsCache.data&&usageArtifactsCache.expiresAt>now)return usageArtifactsCache.data;
  if(!forceRefresh&&usageArtifactsCache.token===authToken&&usageArtifactsCache.promise)return usageArtifactsCache.promise;
  const token=authToken,queue=repos.slice(),artifacts=[];let failedRepos=0;
  const promise=(async()=>{
    async function worker(){
      while(queue.length){
        const repo=queue.shift();
        try{
          const list=await ghAll(`/repos/${repo.full_name}/actions/artifacts`,'artifacts');
          list.filter(item=>!item.expired).forEach(item=>artifacts.push({...item,repoFullName:repo.full_name,repoName:repo.name}));
        }catch(_){failedRepos++;}
      }
    }
    await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,Math.max(1,repos.length))},()=>worker()));
    artifacts.sort((a,b)=>String(b.created_at||'').localeCompare(String(a.created_at||'')));
    artifacts.unavailableRepos=failedRepos;
    if(authToken===token)usageArtifactsCache={token,expiresAt:Date.now()+USAGE_CACHE_MS,data:artifacts,promise:null};
    return artifacts;
  })();
  usageArtifactsCache={token,expiresAt:0,data:null,promise};
  try{return await promise;}catch(error){if(usageArtifactsCache.promise===promise)usageArtifactsCache={token:null,expiresAt:0,data:null,promise:null};throw error;}
}

async function updateHomeUsageTotal(repoBytes){
  const value=document.getElementById('homeUsageValue'),label=document.getElementById('homeUsageLabel');
  if(value)value.textContent='…';
  try{
    const repos=allRepos.slice(),artifacts=await loadUsageArtifacts(repos);
    const total=repoBytes+artifacts.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+Number(item.size_in_bytes||0),0);
    const liveValue=document.getElementById('homeUsageValue'),liveLabel=document.getElementById('homeUsageLabel');
    if(liveValue)liveValue.textContent=formatTransferBytes(total);
    if(liveLabel)liveLabel.textContent=artifacts.unavailableRepos?`Total partiel · ${artifacts.unavailableRepos} dépôt(s) non vérifié(s)`:`Total dépôts + ${artifacts.length} artefact(s) actif(s)`;
  }catch(_){
    const liveValue=document.getElementById('homeUsageValue'),liveLabel=document.getElementById('homeUsageLabel');
    if(liveValue)liveValue.textContent=formatTransferBytes(repoBytes);
    if(liveLabel)liveLabel.textContent='Dépôts · artefacts indisponibles';
  }
}

async function renderUsageDashboard(forceRefresh=false){
  setScreenActions(`<button class="icon-btn" id="refreshUsageBtn" title="Actualiser l’espace" aria-label="Actualiser l’espace">${ico('refresh',17)}</button>`);
  setBody(`<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Calcul des fichiers et des artefacts…</div>`);
  try{
    const repos=allRepos.length?allRepos:await ghAll('/user/repos?sort=updated&affiliation=owner,collaborator,organization_member');
    const artifacts=await loadUsageArtifacts(repos,forceRefresh),files=[],queue=repos.slice();
    const artifactRefs=new Map();
    artifacts.forEach(item=>{const runId=item.workflow_run?.id||item.workflow_run?.run_id||item.workflow_run_id;if(runId)artifactRefs.set(String(runId),item);});
    const artifactRunIds=[...artifactRefs.keys()],artifactRuns=new Map();let runCursor=0;
    await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,artifactRunIds.length)},async()=>{
      while(runCursor<artifactRunIds.length){const runId=artifactRunIds[runCursor++],artifact=artifactRefs.get(runId);const run=await getActionRunMetadata(artifact.repoFullName,runId);if(run)artifactRuns.set(runId,run);}
    }));
    let failedTrees=0,truncatedTrees=0;
    async function usageWorker(){
      while(queue.length){
        const repo=queue.shift(),branch=repo.default_branch||'main';
        try{
          const ref=await gh(`/repos/${repo.full_name}/git/ref/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`);
          const tree=await gh(`/repos/${repo.full_name}/git/trees/${ref.object.sha}?recursive=1`);
          if(tree.truncated)truncatedTrees++;
          (tree.tree||[]).filter(item=>item.type==='blob').forEach(item=>files.push({
            fullName:repo.full_name,repoName:repo.name,branch,path:item.path,size:Number(item.size)||0,sha:item.sha
          }));
        }catch(_){failedTrees++;}
      }
    }
    await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,Math.max(1,repos.length))},()=>usageWorker()));
    files.sort((a,b)=>a.repoName.localeCompare(b.repoName)||a.path.localeCompare(b.path));
    const repoStorageBytes=sumByteSizes(repos.map(repo=>(Number(repo.size)||0)*1024));
    const fileBytes=sumByteSizes(files.map(item=>item.size));
    const artifactBytes=sumByteSizes(artifacts.map(item=>item.size_in_bytes));
    const totalBytes=sumByteSizes([fileBytes,artifactBytes]),fmt=bytes=>formatTransferBytes(bytes);
    const filePaneHint=`${files.length} fichier(s) sur les branches par défaut · ${fmt(fileBytes)} cumulés. Calcul exact : ${fmt(fileBytes)} + ${fmt(artifactBytes)} = ${fmt(totalBytes)} (fichiers + artefacts).`;
    setBody(`<div class="usage-summary-grid"><div class="usage-summary-card"><strong>${files.length}</strong><span>Fichiers · ${fmt(fileBytes)}</span></div><div class="usage-summary-card"><strong>${artifacts.length}</strong><span>Artefacts · ${fmt(artifactBytes)}</span></div><div class="usage-summary-card"><strong>${fmt(totalBytes)}</strong><span>Total fichiers + artefacts</span></div></div><div class="modal-hint">Le total affiché additionne uniquement les tailles réellement listées : <strong>${fmt(fileBytes)} + ${fmt(artifactBytes)} = ${fmt(totalBytes)}</strong>. L’estimation GitHub des dépôts, qui inclut l’historique, vaut ${fmt(repoStorageBytes)} et reste séparée. Les artefacts expirés sont exclus.${artifacts.unavailableRepos?` ${artifacts.unavailableRepos} dépôt(s) n’ont pas permis de vérifier les artefacts; le total peut être incomplet.`:''}</div><div class="filter-row usage-view-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Catégorie d’espace consommé"><button class="chip active" type="button" id="usageFilesTab" role="tab" aria-selected="true">Fichiers (${files.length})</button><button class="chip" type="button" id="usageArtifactsTab" role="tab" aria-selected="false">Artefacts (${artifacts.length})</button></div><section id="usageFilesPane" role="tabpanel"><div class="modal-hint" style="margin:0 0 8px;">${escapeHtml(filePaneHint)}</div><input id="usageFilesSearch" class="search-input" type="search" placeholder="Filtrer par dépôt, chemin ou extension…" autocomplete="off"><div id="usageFilesList"></div></section><section id="usageArtifactsPane" role="tabpanel" hidden><div id="usageArtifactsList"></div></section>${failedTrees?`<div class="modal-hint">${failedTrees} dépôt(s) n’ont pas pu être indexés. Vérifie les droits du token et les branches par défaut.</div>`:''}${truncatedTrees?`<div class="modal-hint">L’arborescence de ${truncatedTrees} dépôt(s) est tronquée par GitHub; certains fichiers ne sont pas compris dans le décompte.</div>`:''}`);

    const filesTab=document.getElementById('usageFilesTab'),artifactsTab=document.getElementById('usageArtifactsTab');
    const filesPane=document.getElementById('usageFilesPane'),artifactsPane=document.getElementById('usageArtifactsPane');
    function selectUsagePane(mode){
      const showFiles=mode==='files';filesPane.hidden=!showFiles;artifactsPane.hidden=showFiles;
      filesTab.classList.toggle('active',showFiles);artifactsTab.classList.toggle('active',!showFiles);
      filesTab.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(showFiles));artifactsTab.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(!showFiles));
    }
    filesTab.onclick=()=>selectUsagePane('files');artifactsTab.onclick=()=>selectUsagePane('artifacts');

    const filesList=document.getElementById('usageFilesList');let fileLimit=100;
    function renderUsageFiles(){
      const query=(document.getElementById('usageFilesSearch')?.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
      const matches=query?files.filter(item=>`${item.repoName} ${item.fullName} ${item.path} ${fileExtension(item.path)}`.toLowerCase().includes(query)):files;
      const visible=matches.slice(0,fileLimit);
      filesList.innerHTML=visible.map(item=>{
        const index=files.indexOf(item);
        return `<div class="list-item" data-usage-open-file="${index}" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="li-ico">${ico('file',16)}</span><span class="li-main"><span class="li-title">${escapeHtml(item.path)}</span><span class="li-sub">${escapeHtml(item.repoName)} · ${escapeHtml(item.branch)} · ${fmt(item.size)} · modifié <span data-usage-file-date="${index}">date en chargement…</span></span></span><button class="icon-btn" type="button" data-usage-file-menu="${index}" title="Autres actions" aria-label="Autres actions pour ${escapeHtml(item.path)}">${ico('more',15)}</button></div>`;
      }).join('')||'<div class="empty-state">Aucun fichier correspondant.</div>';
      const dateTargets=[...filesList.querySelectorAll('[data-usage-file-date]')];let dateCursor=0;
      Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,dateTargets.length)},async()=>{
        while(dateCursor<dateTargets.length){const target=dateTargets[dateCursor++],item=files[Number(target.dataset.usageFileDate)];if(!item)continue;
          try{const date=await githubFileLastUpdatedAt(item.fullName,item.path,item.branch);if(target.isConnected)target.textContent=date?`${relDate(date)} · ${formatRunDateTime(date)}`:'date indisponible';}
          catch(error){if(target.isConnected)target.textContent='date indisponible';}
        }
      }));
      if(matches.length>visible.length)filesList.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-block" id="usageFilesMore">Afficher ${Math.min(100,matches.length-visible.length)} fichier(s) supplémentaire(s) · ${visible.length}/${matches.length}</button>`);
      filesList.querySelectorAll('[data-usage-open-file]').forEach(row=>{
        const open=()=>{const item=files[Number(row.dataset.usageOpenFile)];if(item)openRepositoryFile(item.fullName,item.path,item.branch,item.size);};
        row.onclick=event=>{if(!event.target.closest('[data-usage-file-menu]'))open();};
        row.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.target.closest('[data-usage-file-menu]'))return;if(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' '){event.preventDefault();open();}};
      });
      filesList.querySelectorAll('[data-usage-file-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{event.stopPropagation();const item=files[Number(button.dataset.usageFileMenu)];if(item)openRepositoryFileActions(item.fullName,item.path,item.branch,item.size);});
      document.getElementById('usageFilesMore')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{fileLimit+=100;renderUsageFiles();});
    }
    document.getElementById('usageFilesSearch').addEventListener('input',()=>{fileLimit=100;renderUsageFiles();});
    renderUsageFiles();

    const artifactBox=document.getElementById('usageArtifactsList');
    artifactBox.innerHTML=artifacts.length?`<div class="action-row" style="justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:7px;"><span class="modal-hint" style="margin:0;">${artifacts.length} artefact(s) actif(s) · ${fmt(artifactBytes)}. Seuls les artefacts peuvent être supprimés.</span><button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" id="deleteSelectedArtifacts" disabled>${ico('trash',14)} Supprimer</button></div>`+artifacts.map((item,index)=>`<div class="usage-select-row"><input type="checkbox" data-usage-artifact="${index}"><span class="li-main"><span class="li-title">#${escapeHtml(artifactRuns.get(String(item.workflow_run?.id||item.workflow_run?.run_id||item.workflow_run_id))?.run_number??'—')} · ${escapeHtml(item.name)}</span><span class="li-sub">${escapeHtml(item.repoName)} · ${fmt(item.size_in_bytes)} · ${escapeHtml(relDate(artifactRuns.get(String(item.workflow_run?.id||item.workflow_run?.run_id||item.workflow_run_id))?.created_at||item.created_at))} · ${escapeHtml(formatRunDateTime(artifactRuns.get(String(item.workflow_run?.id||item.workflow_run?.run_id||item.workflow_run_id))?.created_at||item.created_at))}</span></span><button type="button" class="icon-btn" data-usage-artifact-menu="${index}" title="Autres actions" aria-label="Autres actions pour ${escapeHtml(item.name)}">${ico('more',15)}</button></div>`).join(''):`<div class="empty-state">Aucun artefact Actions actif · 0 o utilisés.</div>`;
    const deleteBtn=document.getElementById('deleteSelectedArtifacts');
    artifactBox.querySelectorAll('[data-usage-artifact]').forEach(input=>input.onchange=()=>{if(deleteBtn)deleteBtn.disabled=!artifactBox.querySelector('[data-usage-artifact]:checked');});
    artifactBox.querySelectorAll('[data-usage-artifact-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{
      event.stopPropagation();const item=artifacts[Number(button.dataset.usageArtifactMenu)];if(!item)return;
      const zipUrl=`https://api.github.com/repos/${item.repoFullName}/actions/artifacts/${item.id}/zip`,zipName=`${item.name}.zip`;
      openActionSheet(item.name,[
        {label:'Ouvrir l’archive ZIP avec une application externe',icon:'external',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(zipUrl,zipName,'application/zip',item.size_in_bytes,{title:'Ouverture de l’archive',accept:'application/vnd.github+json',openAfter:true})},
        {label:'Télécharger l’archive ZIP complète',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(zipUrl,zipName,'application/zip',item.size_in_bytes,{title:'Téléchargement de l’archive',accept:'application/vnd.github+json'})}
      ],{url:`https://github.com/${item.repoFullName}/actions`,title:`Artefact · ${item.name}`});
    });
    deleteBtn?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
      const selected=[...artifactBox.querySelectorAll('[data-usage-artifact]:checked')].map(input=>artifacts[Number(input.dataset.usageArtifact)]);
      if(!selected.length)return;
      const ok=await confirmModal(`Supprimer ${selected.length} artefact(s) sélectionné(s) ? Cette action est irréversible.`,{title:'Supprimer les artefacts ?',confirmLabel:'Supprimer',danger:true});
      if(!ok)return;
      let deleted=0;for(const item of selected){try{await gh(`/repos/${item.repoFullName}/actions/artifacts/${item.id}`,{method:'DELETE'});deleted++;}catch(_){}}
      usageArtifactsCache={token:authToken,expiresAt:0,data:null,promise:null};
      toast(`${deleted} artefact(s) supprimé(s).`,'success');renderUsageDashboard(true);
    });
    document.getElementById('refreshUsageBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>renderUsageDashboard(true));
  }catch(error){setBody(`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert',28)}</div>Calcul impossible<p class="modal-hint">${escapeHtml(friendly(error))}</p></div>`);}
}


function openNewProjectModal(){
  if (!authToken){ toast('Connecte-toi à GitHub avant de créer un projet.', 'error'); return; }
  openModal({
    title: 'Nouveau projet',
    bodyHTML: `<div class="zip-form-row"><label for="newProjectName">Nom du dépôt</label><input id="newProjectName" class="modal-input" type="text" placeholder="mon-nouveau-projet" autocomplete="off"><div class="zip-form-note">Le projet sera créé dans ton compte GitHub.</div></div><div class="zip-form-row"><label for="newProjectDescription">Description (facultatif)</label><textarea id="newProjectDescription" class="modal-input" rows="3" placeholder="Décris brièvement ton projet…" style="resize:vertical;"></textarea></div><div class="zip-form-row"><label for="newProjectVisibility">Visibilité</label><select id="newProjectVisibility" class="modal-input"><option value="private">Privé</option><option value="public">Public</option></select></div>`,
    footerHTML: `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="newProjectCancel">Annuler</button><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="newProjectCreate" disabled>${ico('plus', 14)} Créer le projet</button>`
  });
  const nameInput = document.getElementById('newProjectName');
  const createBtn = document.getElementById('newProjectCreate');
  const validName = () => /^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$/.test(nameInput.value.trim()) && !['.', '..'].includes(nameInput.value.trim());
  nameInput.addEventListener('input', () => { createBtn.disabled = !validName(); });
  document.getElementById('newProjectCancel').onclick = closeModal;
  nameInput.addEventListener('keydown', event => { if (event.key === 'Enter' && !createBtn.disabled) createBtn.click(); });
  setTimeout(() => nameInput.focus(), 80);
  createBtn.onclick = async () => {
    const name = nameInput.value.trim();
    if (!validName()) { toast('Le nom doit contenir uniquement des lettres, chiffres, points, tirets et underscores.', 'error'); return; }
    setBtnLoading(createBtn, true);
    try{
      const created = await gh('/user/repos', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ name, description: document.getElementById('newProjectDescription').value.trim(), private: document.getElementById('newProjectVisibility').value === 'private', auto_init: true }) });
      allRepos = [created, ...allRepos.filter(repo => repo.full_name !== created.full_name)];
      closeModal();
      toast(`Projet « ${created.name || name} » créé`, 'success');
      navReset(renderRepoHome, created.full_name, [created.full_name]);
    } catch(err){
      setBtnLoading(createBtn, false, `${ico('plus', 14)} Créer le projet`);
      showError('Création du projet impossible', friendly(err));
    }
  };
}

function renderRepos(){
  if (!authToken){
    setBody(`<div class="hero">
        <div class="hero-ico">${ico('lock', 24)}</div>
        <h2>Connecte-toi à GitHub</h2>
        <p>Colle ton token pour voir tes dépôts, analyser les erreurs de build et éditer ton code depuis le téléphone.</p>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="document.getElementById('accountBtn').click()">Se connecter</button>
      </div>`);
    return;
  }
  setScreenActions(`<button class="icon-btn" id="repoSearchToggleBtn" title="Rechercher un dépôt" aria-label="Rechercher un dépôt" aria-controls="repoSearchWrap" aria-expanded="false">${ico('search', 18)}</button><button class="icon-btn" title="Actualiser" aria-label="Actualiser les dépôts" onclick="refreshRepos()">${ico('refresh', 17)}</button>`,[
    {label:'Nouveau projet',icon:'plus',fn:openNewProjectModal},
    {label:'Importer un ZIP',icon:'upload',fn:()=>openZipUpload()}
  ]);
  if (!allRepos.length){
    setBody(`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('inbox', 30)}</div><h2>Aucun projet pour le moment</h2><p>Crée un nouveau projet ou importe directement une archive ZIP pour commencer.</p><div class="action-row" style="justify-content:center;margin-top:14px;"><button class="btn btn-primary" id="emptyNewProjectBtn">${ico('plus', 16)} Nouveau projet</button><button class="btn btn-secondary" id="emptyZipUploadBtn">${ico('upload', 16)} Importer un ZIP</button></div></div>`);
    document.getElementById('emptyNewProjectBtn').onclick = openNewProjectModal;
    document.getElementById('emptyZipUploadBtn').onclick = () => openZipUpload();
    return;
  }
  setBody(`
    <div class="home-usage-grid"><button class="home-usage-card" id="openUsageDashboard"><span class="usage-icon">${ico('inbox',18)}</span><span style="min-width:0;"><span class="usage-value" id="homeUsageValue">Calcul…</span><span class="usage-label" id="homeUsageLabel">Dépôts + artefacts Actions actifs</span></span></button><button class="home-usage-card" id="openRepoCount"><span class="usage-icon">${ico('git',18)}</span><span style="min-width:0;"><span class="usage-value">${allRepos.length}</span><span class="usage-label">Dépôts GitHub</span></span></button></div>
    <div class="filter-row">
      <button class="chip ${repoFilter === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="setRepoFilter('all', this)">Tous</button>
      <button class="chip ${repoFilter === 'public' ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="setRepoFilter('public', this)">${ico('globe', 12)} Publics</button>
      <button class="chip ${repoFilter === 'private' ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="setRepoFilter('private', this)">${ico('lock', 12)} Privés</button>
    </div>
    <div class="repo-search-wrap" id="repoSearchWrap" hidden><div class="repo-search-field">${ico('search',16)}<input type="search" class="search-input" id="repoSearchInput" placeholder="Filtrer les dépôts…" autocomplete="off"><button type="button" class="icon-btn" id="repoSearchCloseBtn" title="Fermer la recherche" aria-label="Fermer la recherche">${ico('close',17)}</button></div></div>
    <div id="repoListWrap"></div>
  `);
  document.getElementById('openUsageDashboard').onclick=()=>navTo(renderUsageDashboard,'Espace consommé',[],'Fichiers et artefacts Actions');
  document.getElementById('openRepoCount').onclick=()=>document.getElementById('repoListWrap')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});
  updateHomeUsageTotal(allRepos.reduce((sum,repo)=>sum+(Number(repo.size)||0)*1024,0));
  document.getElementById('repoSearchToggleBtn').onclick=()=>{
    const wrap=document.getElementById('repoSearchWrap'),input=document.getElementById('repoSearchInput'),button=document.getElementById('repoSearchToggleBtn');
    wrap.hidden=false;button.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');requestAnimationFrame(()=>input.focus());
  };
  document.getElementById('repoSearchCloseBtn').onclick=()=>{
    const wrap=document.getElementById('repoSearchWrap'),input=document.getElementById('repoSearchInput');
    input.value='';wrap.hidden=true;document.getElementById('repoSearchToggleBtn').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');renderRepoListFiltered();document.getElementById('repoSearchToggleBtn').focus();
  };
  document.getElementById('repoSearchInput').addEventListener('input', renderRepoListFiltered);
  renderRepoListFiltered();
}
function renderRepoListFiltered(){
  const q = (document.getElementById('repoSearchInput')?.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const filtered = allRepos.filter(r => {
    if (repoFilter === 'public' && r.private) return false;
    if (repoFilter === 'private' && !r.private) return false;
    if (q && !r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
    return true;
  });
  const wrap = document.getElementById('repoListWrap');
  if (!wrap) return;
  if (!filtered.length){
    wrap.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('inbox', 28)}</div>Aucun dépôt ne correspond.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  wrap.innerHTML = filtered.map(r => `
    <div class="list-item" data-open-repo="${escapeHtml(r.full_name)}">
      <span class="li-ico">${ico('git', 18)}</span>
      <div class="li-main">
        <div class="li-title">${escapeHtml(r.name)}</div>
        <div class="li-sub">${r.private ? 'Privé' : 'Public'} · maj ${relDate(r.updated_at)}</div>
      </div>
      <button class="icon-btn" data-repo-menu="${escapeHtml(r.full_name)}" title="Actions du dépôt" aria-label="Actions de ${escapeHtml(r.full_name)}">${ico('more',16)}</button>
      <span class="chevron">${ico('chevronRight', 16)}</span>
    </div>`).join('');
  wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-open-repo]').forEach(item=>item.onclick=()=>openRepo(item.dataset.openRepo));
  wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-repo-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{event.stopPropagation();const repo=allRepos.find(item=>item.full_name===button.dataset.repoMenu);if(repo)openRepositoryActions(repo);});
}
function openRepositoryActions(repo){
  openActionSheet(repo.full_name,[
    {label:'Télécharger le ZIP complet',icon:'download',fn:()=>exportCompleteRepoZip(repo.full_name,repo.default_branch,false)},
    {label:'Partager le ZIP complet',icon:'share',fn:()=>exportCompleteRepoZip(repo.full_name,repo.default_branch,true)},
    {label:'Importer un ZIP',icon:'upload',fn:()=>openZipUpload(repo.full_name,repo.default_branch)},
    {label:'Télécharger un APK release',icon:'download',fn:()=>navTo(renderReleases,'Releases',[repo.full_name,repo.default_branch],`dépôt ${repo.full_name}`)},
    {label:'Modifier icône & splash screen',icon:'image',fn:()=>navTo(renderIconSplashStudio,'Icône & splash',[repo.full_name,repo.default_branch],`dépôt ${repo.full_name}`)},
    {label:'Ouvrir dans GitHub',icon:'external',fn:()=>confirmExternalNavigation(repo.html_url||`https://github.com/${repo.full_name}`,'dépôt GitHub')},
    {label:'Renommer le dépôt',icon:'pencil',fn:()=>{openRepo(repo.full_name);setTimeout(()=>confirmRenameRepo(repo.full_name),180)}},
    {label:'Supprimer le dépôt',icon:'trash',danger:true,fn:()=>confirmDeleteRepo(repo.full_name)}
  ],{url:repo.html_url||`https://github.com/${repo.full_name}`,title:repo.full_name});
}
function setRepoFilter(f, el){
  repoFilter = f;
  persistNavigationState();
  document.querySelectorAll('.filter-row .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
  if (el) el.classList.add('active');
  renderRepoListFiltered();
}
async function refreshRepos(){
  try{
    allRepos = await ghAll('/user/repos?sort=updated&affiliation=owner,collaborator,organization_member');
    usageArtifactsCache={token:authToken,expiresAt:0,data:null,promise:null};
    toast('Liste actualisée', 'success', 1800);
    renderRepos();
  } catch(err){ showError('Erreur', friendly(err)); }
}
function openRepo(fullName){ navTo(renderRepoHome, fullName, [fullName]); }

async function createBranchFrom(fullName, sourceBranch){
  let branches;
  try{
    const data = await ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/branches`);
    branches = (data || []).map(item => item.name);
  } catch(err){ showError('Chargement des branches impossible', friendly(err)); return; }
  openModal({
    title: 'Créer une branche',
    bodyHTML: `<div class="zip-form-row"><label for="newBranchName">Nom de la nouvelle branche</label><input id="newBranchName" class="modal-input" type="text" placeholder="feature/ma-fonction" autocomplete="off"></div><div class="zip-form-row"><label for="newBranchSource">Créer depuis</label><select id="newBranchSource" class="modal-input">${branches.map(name => `<option value="${escapeHtml(name)}" ${name === sourceBranch ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(name)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`,
    footerHTML: `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="createBranchCancel">Annuler</button><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="createBranchConfirm" disabled>${ico('plus', 14)} Créer la branche</button>`
  });
  const nameInput = document.getElementById('newBranchName');
  const confirmBtn = document.getElementById('createBranchConfirm');
  const sourceSelect = document.getElementById('newBranchSource');
  nameInput.addEventListener('input', () => { confirmBtn.disabled = !nameInput.value.trim(); });
  document.getElementById('createBranchCancel').onclick = closeModal;
  confirmBtn.onclick = async () => {
    const name = nameInput.value.trim();
    if (!name) return;
    const source = sourceSelect.value;
    setBtnLoading(confirmBtn, true);
    try{
      const ref = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/ref/heads/${encodeURIComponent(source)}`);
      await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/refs`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ ref: `refs/heads/${name}`, sha: ref.object.sha }) });
      closeModal();
      repoBranchSelection[fullName] = name;
      toast(`Branche « ${name} » créée`, 'success');
      renderRepoHome(fullName);
    } catch(err){
      setBtnLoading(confirmBtn, false);
      showError('Création de branche impossible', `${friendly(err)}\n\nVérifie le nom et la permission Contents en écriture du token GitHub.`);
    }
  };
}

async function compareRepoBranches(fullName, branch, defaultBranch){
  if (branch === defaultBranch){ toast(`La branche sélectionnée est déjà « ${defaultBranch} ».`, 'info'); return; }
  openModal({ title: 'Comparer les branches', bodyHTML: `<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Comparaison de ${escapeHtml(branch)} avec ${escapeHtml(defaultBranch)}…</div>` });
  try{
    const data = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/compare/${encodeURIComponent(defaultBranch)}...${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`);
    const files = data.files || [];
    const fileRows = files.slice(0, 80).map((file,index) => `<div class="branch-diff-row"><button class="diff-file-row" data-branch-diff="${index}"><span class="diff-status diff-${escapeHtml(file.status)}">${escapeHtml(file.status)}</span><span class="diff-file-name">${escapeHtml(file.filename)}</span><span class="diff-count">+${file.additions} −${file.deletions}</span></button><button type="button" class="icon-btn" data-branch-diff-menu="${index}" title="Actions du fichier" aria-label="Actions de ${escapeHtml(file.filename)}">${ico('more',15)}</button></div>`).join('');
    document.querySelector('#modalOverlay .modal-body').innerHTML = `<div class="diff-summary"><strong>${escapeHtml(data.ahead_by)} commit(s) en avance</strong><span>${escapeHtml(data.behind_by)} en retard</span><span>${escapeHtml(data.total_commits)} commit(s) comparé(s)</span></div><div class="diff-title">${escapeHtml(defaultBranch)} ← ${escapeHtml(branch)}</div>${fileRows || '<div class="modal-hint">Aucun fichier modifié entre ces branches.</div>'}${files.length > 80 ? `<div class="modal-hint">Affichage limité aux 80 premiers fichiers sur ${files.length}.</div>` : ''}`;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-branch-diff]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      const file=files[Number(button.dataset.branchDiff)];
      if(!file.patch){toast('GitHub ne fournit pas de patch en texte pour ce fichier (fichier binaire ou patch trop volumineux).','info');return;}
      const patchRows=file.patch.split('\n').map(line=>`<div class="diff-line ${line.startsWith('+')&&!line.startsWith('+++')?'add':line.startsWith('-')&&!line.startsWith('---')?'remove':'context'}">${escapeHtml(line)||'&nbsp;'}</div>`).join('');
      openModal({title:`Diff · ${escapeHtml(file.filename)}`,bodyHTML:`<div class="diff-summary"><strong>+${file.additions}</strong><span style="color:var(--danger);">−${file.deletions}</span></div><div class="diff-file-list">${patchRows}</div>`});
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-branch-diff-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{
      event.stopPropagation();const file=files[Number(button.dataset.branchDiffMenu)];if(!file)return;
      const sourceBranch=file.status==='removed'?defaultBranch:branch,url=githubContentsApiUrl(fullName,file.filename,sourceBranch),mime=mimeForPath(file.filename),size=0;
      openActionSheet(file.filename,[
        {label:'Télécharger le fichier',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,file.filename,mime,size,{title:'Téléchargement du fichier',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw'})},
        {label:'Ouvrir avec une application externe',icon:'external',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,file.filename,mime,size,{title:'Ouverture du fichier',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',openAfter:true})},
        {label:'Partager le fichier',icon:'share',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,file.filename,mime,size,{title:'Préparation du partage',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',share:true})}
      ],{url:githubBlobPageUrl(fullName,file.filename,sourceBranch),title:file.filename});
    });
  } catch(err){ showError('Comparaison impossible', friendly(err)); }
}

const STUDIO_APP_NAME='GitHub Studio';
function releaseAssetDisplayName(name){
  return String(name||'fichier')==='app-debug.apk'?`${STUDIO_APP_NAME}.apk`:String(name||'fichier');
}

async function downloadLatestRelease(fullName){
  try{
    const releases=await ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/releases`);
    const release=releases.find(item=>!item.draft&&!item.prerelease);
    if(!release){showError('Aucune release','Ce dépôt ne contient pas de release publiée.');return false;}
    const assets=release.assets||[];
    const asset=assets.find(item=>/\.apk$/i.test(item.name))||assets.find(item=>/\.(aab|zip)$/i.test(item.name))||assets[0];
    if(!asset){showError('Release sans fichier','La dernière release ne contient aucun fichier téléchargeable.');return false;}
    return await transferGithubResource(`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/releases/assets/${asset.id}`,asset.name,asset.content_type||mimeForPath(asset.name),asset.size,{title:`Téléchargement de ${release.tag_name}`,accept:'application/octet-stream',installAfter:/\.apk$/i.test(asset.name),showInstallPanel:/\.apk$/i.test(asset.name)});
  }catch(error){showError('Téléchargement de la release impossible',friendly(error));return false;}
}

async function renderReleases(fullName, branch){
  setScreenActions(`<button class="icon-btn" id="createReleaseBtn" title="Créer une release" aria-label="Créer une release">${ico('plus',16)}</button><button class="icon-btn" id="refreshReleasesBtn" title="Actualiser les releases" aria-label="Actualiser les releases">${ico('refresh',16)}</button>`);
  document.getElementById('createReleaseBtn').onclick=()=>createNewRelease(fullName,branch);
  document.getElementById('refreshReleasesBtn').onclick=()=>renderReleases(fullName,branch);
  const active=releasePanelSelection[fullName]||'releases';
  setBody(`<div class="release-tabs"><button class="chip ${active==='releases'?'active':''}" id="releaseTabBtn">Releases</button><button class="chip ${active==='artifacts'?'active':''}" id="artifactTabBtn">Artefacts Actions</button></div><div id="releasePanelContent"><div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement…</div></div>`);
  document.getElementById('releaseTabBtn').onclick=()=>{releasePanelSelection[fullName]='releases';persistNavigationState();renderReleases(fullName,branch);};
  document.getElementById('artifactTabBtn').onclick=()=>{releasePanelSelection[fullName]='artifacts';persistNavigationState();renderReleases(fullName,branch);};
  const panel=document.getElementById('releasePanelContent');
  if(active==='artifacts'){
    await loadRepositoryArtifacts(fullName,panel);
    return;
  }
  try{
    const releases=await ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/releases`);
    if(!panel.isConnected)return;
    const cards=releases.map((release,index)=>{
      const releaseDate=release.published_at||release.created_at;
      const buildNumber=(release.tag_name||'').match(/^build-(\d+)$/i)?.[1];
      return `<section class="list-item release-card" style="display:block;cursor:default;"><div class="release-card-head"><span class="li-ico">${ico('tag',17)}</span><div class="li-main"><div class="li-title">${escapeHtml(release.name||release.tag_name)}</div><div class="li-sub release-meta">${escapeHtml(release.tag_name)}${buildNumber?` · #${buildNumber}`:''} · ${escapeHtml(relDate(releaseDate))} · ${escapeHtml(formatRunDateTime(releaseDate))}${release.draft?' · brouillon':''}${release.prerelease?' · pré-version':''}</div></div><button class="icon-btn" data-release-menu="${index}" title="Actions de la release" aria-label="Actions de la release">${ico('more',15)}</button></div>${release.body?`<div class="li-sub" style="white-space:pre-wrap;margin:9px 0;max-height:90px;overflow:auto;">${escapeHtml(release.body)}</div>`:''}${(release.assets||[]).map((asset,assetIndex)=>`<div class="release-asset"><span class="li-ico">${ico(asset.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.apk')?'download':'file',15)}</span><span class="release-asset-name li-title">${escapeHtml(releaseAssetDisplayName(asset.name))} · ${(asset.size/1048576).toFixed(1)} Mo</span><button class="icon-btn" data-release-download="${index}:${assetIndex}" title="Télécharger ${escapeHtml(asset.name)}" aria-label="Télécharger ${escapeHtml(asset.name)}">${ico('download',15)}</button>${/\.apk$/i.test(asset.name)?`<button class="icon-btn" data-release-install="${index}:${assetIndex}" title="Installer ${escapeHtml(asset.name)}" aria-label="Installer ${escapeHtml(asset.name)}">${ico('play',15)}</button>`:''}<button class="icon-btn" data-release-asset-menu="${index}:${assetIndex}" title="Autres actions" aria-label="Autres actions pour ${escapeHtml(asset.name)}">${ico('more',15)}</button></div>`).join('')||'<div class="modal-hint">Aucun fichier joint à cette release.</div>'}</section>`;
    }).join('');
    panel.innerHTML=cards||`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('tag',28)}</div>Aucune release publiée.<br><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="firstReleaseBtn" style="margin-top:12px;">${ico('plus',14)} Créer une release</button></div>`;
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-release-download]').forEach(button=>{
      const remove=document.createElement('button');remove.type='button';remove.className='icon-btn';remove.title='Supprimer ce fichier joint';remove.setAttribute('aria-label','Supprimer ce fichier joint');remove.innerHTML=ico('trash',15);remove.dataset.releaseAssetDelete=button.dataset.releaseDownload;button.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',remove);
    });
    document.getElementById('firstReleaseBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>createNewRelease(fullName,branch));
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-release-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      const release=releases[Number(button.dataset.releaseMenu)];if(!release)return;
      const asset=(release.assets||[]).find(item=>/\.apk$/i.test(item.name))||(release.assets||[])[0];
      const items=asset?[{label:`Télécharger ${asset.name}`,icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/releases/assets/${asset.id}`,asset.name,asset.content_type||mimeForPath(asset.name),asset.size,{title:'Téléchargement de la release',accept:'application/octet-stream',installAfter:/\.apk$/i.test(asset.name),showInstallPanel:/\.apk$/i.test(asset.name)})}]:[];
      items.push({label:'Supprimer la release et ses fichiers joints',icon:'trash',danger:true,fn:()=>deleteRelease(fullName,release,branch)});
      openActionSheet(`Release · ${release.tag_name}`,items,{url:release.html_url,title:release.name||release.tag_name});
    });
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-release-download]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=async()=>{
      const [releaseIndex,assetIndex]=button.dataset.releaseDownload.split(':').map(Number),asset=releases[releaseIndex]?.assets?.[assetIndex];if(!asset)return;
      setBtnLoading(button,true);
      await transferGithubResource(`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/releases/assets/${asset.id}`,asset.name,asset.content_type||mimeForPath(asset.name),asset.size,{title:'Téléchargement de la release',accept:'application/octet-stream',installAfter:/\.apk$/i.test(asset.name),showInstallPanel:/\.apk$/i.test(asset.name)});
      if(button.isConnected)setBtnLoading(button,false,ico('download',15));
    });
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-release-install]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=async()=>{
      const [releaseIndex,assetIndex]=button.dataset.releaseInstall.split(':').map(Number),asset=releases[releaseIndex]?.assets?.[assetIndex];if(!asset)return;
      setBtnLoading(button,true);
      await transferGithubResource(`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/releases/assets/${asset.id}`,asset.name,asset.content_type||mimeForPath(asset.name),asset.size,{title:'Installation de la release',accept:'application/octet-stream',installAfter:true,showInstallPanel:true});
      if(button.isConnected)setBtnLoading(button,false,ico('play',15));
    });
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-release-asset-delete]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      const [releaseIndex,assetIndex]=button.dataset.releaseAssetDelete.split(':').map(Number),release=releases[releaseIndex],asset=release?.assets?.[assetIndex];
      if(release&&asset)deleteReleaseAsset(fullName,release,asset,branch);
    });
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-release-asset-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      const [releaseIndex,assetIndex]=button.dataset.releaseAssetMenu.split(':').map(Number),release=releases[releaseIndex],asset=release?.assets?.[assetIndex];if(!asset)return;
      const url=`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/releases/assets/${asset.id}`,mime=asset.content_type||mimeForPath(asset.name);
      openActionSheet(asset.name,[
        {label:'Télécharger le fichier',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,asset.name,mime,asset.size,{title:'Téléchargement de la release',accept:'application/octet-stream',installAfter:/\.apk$/i.test(asset.name),showInstallPanel:/\.apk$/i.test(asset.name)})},
        {label:'Ouvrir avec une application externe',icon:'external',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,asset.name,mime,asset.size,{title:'Ouverture du fichier',accept:'application/octet-stream',openAfter:true})},
        {label:'Partager le fichier',icon:'share',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,asset.name,mime,asset.size,{title:'Préparation du partage',accept:'application/octet-stream',share:true})}
      ],{url:release.html_url,title:`${asset.name} · ${release.tag_name}`});
    });
    document.getElementById('createReleaseBtn').onclick=()=>createNewRelease(fullName,branch);
  }catch(err){if(panel.isConnected)panel.innerHTML=`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert',28)}</div>${escapeHtml(friendly(err))}</div>`;}
}

async function deleteReleaseAsset(fullName,release,asset,branch){
  const confirmed=await confirmModal(`Supprimer définitivement « ${asset.name} » de la release « ${release.name||release.tag_name} » ?`,{title:'Supprimer ce fichier ?',confirmLabel:'Supprimer le fichier',danger:true});
  if(!confirmed)return;
  try{
    await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/releases/assets/${asset.id}`,{method:'DELETE'});
    toast(`Fichier supprimé : ${asset.name}`,'success');
    await renderReleases(fullName,branch);
  }catch(error){showError('Suppression du fichier impossible',friendly(error));}
}

async function deleteRelease(fullName,release,branch){
  const confirmed=await confirmModal(`Supprimer la release « ${release.name||release.tag_name} » et tous ses fichiers joints ? Le tag Git « ${release.tag_name} » sera conservé.`,{title:'Supprimer cette release ?',confirmLabel:'Supprimer la release',danger:true});
  if(!confirmed)return;
  try{
    await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/releases/${release.id}`,{method:'DELETE'});
    toast(`Release supprimée : ${release.tag_name}`,'success');
    await renderReleases(fullName,branch);
  }catch(error){showError('Suppression de la release impossible',friendly(error));}
}

async function loadRepositoryArtifacts(fullName,panel){
  panel.innerHTML=`<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement des artefacts…</div>`;
  try{
    const [artifacts,runs]=await Promise.all([
      ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/artifacts`,'artifacts'),
      ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs`,'workflow_runs')
    ]);
    const runsById=new Map(runs.map(run=>[String(run.id),run]));
    if(!panel.isConnected)return;
    if(!artifacts.length){panel.innerHTML=`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('inbox',28)}</div>Aucun artefact Actions disponible pour ce dépôt.<div class="modal-hint" style="margin-top:10px;">Si un run a dépassé le quota de stockage GitHub Actions, son artefact n’a pas été créé. L’APK éventuellement publiée reste dans l’onglet Releases.</div></div>`;return;}
    panel.innerHTML=artifacts.map((artifact,index)=>{
      const runId=artifact.workflow_run?.id||artifact.workflow_run?.run_id||artifact.workflow_run_id;
      const run=runsById.get(String(runId));
      const runNumber=run?.run_number;
      const runDate=run?.created_at||artifact.created_at;
      const pageUrl=runId?`https://github.com/${fullName}/actions/runs/${runId}`:`https://github.com/${fullName}/actions`;
      return `<div class="list-item artifact-card" style="cursor:default;"><span class="li-ico">${ico('download',16)}</span><div class="li-main"><div class="li-title">${escapeHtml(artifact.name)}</div><div class="li-sub artifact-meta">${(artifact.size_in_bytes/1048576).toFixed(2)} Mo · run #${escapeHtml(runNumber??'—')} · ${escapeHtml(relDate(runDate))} · ${escapeHtml(formatRunDateTime(runDate))}${artifact.expired?' · expiré':''}</div></div><button class="icon-btn" data-repo-artifact-download="${index}" title="Télécharger l’artefact" ${artifact.expired?'disabled':''}>${ico('download',15)}</button><button class="icon-btn" data-repo-artifact-menu="${index}" title="Autres actions">${ico('more',15)}</button></div>`;
    }).join('');
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-repo-artifact-download]').forEach(button=>{
      const remove=document.createElement('button');remove.type='button';remove.className='icon-btn';remove.title='Supprimer tout l’artefact';remove.setAttribute('aria-label','Supprimer tout l’artefact Actions');remove.innerHTML=ico('trash',15);remove.dataset.repoArtifactDelete=button.dataset.repoArtifactDownload;button.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',remove);
    });
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-repo-artifact-download]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=async()=>{
      const artifact=artifacts[Number(button.dataset.repoArtifactDownload)];if(!artifact||artifact.expired){toast('Cet artefact a expiré.','error');return;}
      setBtnLoading(button,true);
      await transferGithubArtifactApk(`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/actions/artifacts/${artifact.id}/zip`,artifact.name,artifact.size_in_bytes);
      if(button.isConnected)setBtnLoading(button,false,ico('download',15));
    });
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-repo-artifact-delete]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      const artifact=artifacts[Number(button.dataset.repoArtifactDelete)];if(artifact)deleteActionArtifact(fullName,artifact,panel);
    });
    panel.querySelectorAll('[data-repo-artifact-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      const artifact=artifacts[Number(button.dataset.repoArtifactMenu)];if(!artifact)return;
      const runId=artifact.workflow_run?.id||artifact.workflow_run?.run_id||artifact.workflow_run_id;
      const pageUrl=runId?`https://github.com/${fullName}/actions/runs/${runId}`:`https://github.com/${fullName}/actions`;
      const items=[];
      if(!artifact.expired){
        const zipUrl=`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/actions/artifacts/${artifact.id}/zip`,zipName=`${artifact.name}.zip`;
        items.push({label:'Ouvrir l’archive ZIP avec une application externe',icon:'external',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(zipUrl,zipName,'application/zip',artifact.size_in_bytes,{title:'Ouverture de l’archive',accept:'application/vnd.github+json',openAfter:true})});
        items.push({label:'Télécharger l’archive ZIP complète',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(zipUrl,zipName,'application/zip',artifact.size_in_bytes,{title:'Téléchargement de l’archive',accept:'application/vnd.github+json'})});
        items.push({label:'Télécharger l’APK uniquement',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubArtifactApk(zipUrl,artifact.name,artifact.size_in_bytes)});
      }
      items.push({label:'Supprimer tout l’artefact Actions',icon:'trash',danger:true,fn:()=>deleteActionArtifact(fullName,artifact,panel)});
      openActionSheet(artifact.name,items,{url:pageUrl,title:`Run · ${artifact.name}`});
    });
  }catch(error){if(panel.isConnected)panel.innerHTML=`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert',28)}</div>${escapeHtml(friendly(error))}</div>`;}
}

async function deleteActionArtifact(fullName,artifact,panel){
  const confirmed=await confirmModal(`Supprimer l’artefact Actions « ${artifact.name} » ? Tous les fichiers de son archive — APK, médias et autres — seront supprimés ensemble.`,{title:'Supprimer l’artefact Actions ?',confirmLabel:'Supprimer l’artefact',danger:true});
  if(!confirmed)return;
  try{
    await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/artifacts/${artifact.id}`,{method:'DELETE'});
    toast('Artefact Actions supprimé.','success');
    if(panel?.isConnected)await loadRepositoryArtifacts(fullName,panel);
  }catch(error){showError('Suppression de l’artefact impossible',friendly(error));}
}

async function createNewRelease(fullName,branch){
  openModal({title:'Créer une release',bodyHTML:`<div class="zip-form-row"><label for="releaseTag">Tag de version</label><input id="releaseTag" class="modal-input" placeholder="v1.0.0" autocomplete="off"></div><div class="zip-form-row"><label for="releaseName">Nom de la release</label><input id="releaseName" class="modal-input" placeholder="Version 1.0.0"></div><div class="zip-form-row"><label for="releaseNotes">Notes de version</label><textarea id="releaseNotes" class="modal-input" rows="5" style="resize:vertical;" placeholder="Nouveautés, corrections…"></textarea></div><label class="zip-overwrite-option"><input type="checkbox" id="releaseGenerateNotes"><span>Générer automatiquement les notes à partir des changements GitHub</span></label><label class="zip-overwrite-option"><input type="checkbox" id="releasePrerelease"><span>Marquer comme pré-version</span></label><label class="zip-overwrite-option"><input type="checkbox" id="releaseDraft" checked><span>Créer comme brouillon (non publiée)</span></label><div id="releasePublishWarning" class="delete-repo-warning" style="margin-top:9px;" hidden><div class="delete-repo-warning-icon">${ico('alert',17)}</div><div><strong>Cette release sera publiée et visible sur GitHub.</strong><label class="zip-overwrite-option" style="padding:8px;margin-top:7px;"><input type="checkbox" id="releasePublicConfirm"><span>Je confirme la publication immédiate</span></label></div></div><div class="zip-form-row" style="margin-top:12px;"><label for="releaseAssets">Fichiers à joindre (facultatif)</label><input id="releaseAssets" class="modal-input" type="file" multiple><div id="releaseAssetNames" class="zip-form-note">Aucun fichier sélectionné.</div></div><div class="modal-hint">Branche/tag source : ${escapeHtml(branch)}. Le tag sera créé si nécessaire.</div>`,footerHTML:`<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="releaseCancel">Annuler</button><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="releaseCreate">${ico('tag',14)} Créer la release</button>`});
  const files=document.getElementById('releaseAssets');
  files.onchange=()=>{document.getElementById('releaseAssetNames').textContent=files.files.length?[...files.files].map(file=>`${file.name} (${(file.size/1048576).toFixed(1)} Mo)`).join(' · '):'Aucun fichier sélectionné.';};
  document.getElementById('releaseDraft').onchange=event=>{document.getElementById('releasePublishWarning').hidden=event.target.checked;};
  document.getElementById('releaseCancel').onclick=closeModal;
  document.getElementById('releaseCreate').onclick=async()=>{
    const tag=document.getElementById('releaseTag').value.trim();
    const name=document.getElementById('releaseName').value.trim()||tag;
    const body=document.getElementById('releaseNotes').value;
    const draft=document.getElementById('releaseDraft').checked;
    const prerelease=document.getElementById('releasePrerelease').checked;
    const generateNotes=document.getElementById('releaseGenerateNotes').checked;
    const assets=[...files.files];
    if(!tag){document.getElementById('releaseTag').focus();toast('Saisis un tag de version.', 'error');return;}
    if(!draft&&!document.getElementById('releasePublicConfirm').checked){toast('Confirme la publication dans la case affichée.', 'error');document.getElementById('releasePublicConfirm').focus();return;}
    const btn=document.getElementById('releaseCreate');setBtnLoading(btn,true);
    let createdRelease=null;
    try{
      const release=createdRelease=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/releases`,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({tag_name:tag,target_commitish:branch,name,body,draft,prerelease,generate_release_notes:generateNotes})});
      let uploaded=0;
      for(let index=0;index<assets.length;index++){
        const file=assets[index];
        if(btn.isConnected)btn.innerHTML=`<span class="spin"></span> Envoi ${index+1}/${assets.length}`;
        const uploadUrl=`https://uploads.github.com/repos/${fullName}/releases/${release.id}/assets?name=${encodeURIComponent(file.name)}`;
        const response=await fetch(uploadUrl,{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:`token ${authToken}`,Accept:'application/vnd.github+json','Content-Type':file.type||'application/octet-stream'},body:file});
        if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`La release a été créée, mais l’envoi de « ${file.name} » a échoué (HTTP ${response.status}). Ouvre la release pour terminer l’envoi.`);
        uploaded++;
      }
      closeModal();toast(`Release « ${tag} » créée${uploaded?` · ${uploaded} fichier(s) joint(s)`:''}`, 'success',4000);renderReleases(fullName,branch);
    }catch(err){
      if(createdRelease){closeModal();showError('Release créée — envoi incomplet',`${friendly(err)}\n\nRelease créée : ${createdRelease.html_url}\nLes autres fichiers peuvent être ajoutés depuis la page GitHub de la release.`);renderReleases(fullName,branch);}
      else{setBtnLoading(btn,false,`${ico('tag',14)} Créer la release`);showError('Création de release impossible',`${friendly(err)}\n\nVérifie que le token GitHub a Contents en écriture et, si le tag modifie un workflow, Workflows en écriture.`);}
    }
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   ÉCRAN 2 : ACCUEIL DU DÉPÔT
============================================================ */
async function renderRepoHome(fullName){
  setBody(`<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement du dépôt…</div>`);
  setScreenActions(`<button class="icon-btn" title="Renommer le dépôt" id="renameRepoBtn">${ico('pencil', 16)}</button>`);
  document.getElementById('renameRepoBtn').onclick = () => confirmRenameRepo(fullName);
  try{
    const [repo, branchesRaw] = await Promise.all([
      gh(`/repos/${fullName}`),
      ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/branches`).catch(() => [])
    ]);
    const branches = Array.isArray(branchesRaw) && branchesRaw.length ? branchesRaw : [{ name: repo.default_branch }];
    setScreenActions(`<button class="icon-btn" title="Renommer le dépôt" aria-label="Renommer le dépôt" id="renameRepoBtn">${ico('pencil',16)}</button>`,[
      {label:'Ouvrir le dépôt sur GitHub',icon:'github',fn:()=>confirmExternalNavigation(repo.html_url||`https://github.com/${fullName}`,'dépôt GitHub')},
      {label:'Télécharger le ZIP complet',icon:'download',fn:()=>exportCompleteRepoZip(fullName,branch,false)},
      {label:'Partager le ZIP complet',icon:'share',fn:()=>exportCompleteRepoZip(fullName,branch,true)},
      {label:'Importer un ZIP',icon:'upload',fn:()=>openZipUpload(fullName,branch)},
      {label:'Télécharger un APK release',icon:'download',fn:()=>navTo(renderReleases,'Releases',[fullName,branch],`dépôt ${fullName}`)},
      {label:'Modifier icône & splash screen',icon:'image',fn:()=>navTo(renderIconSplashStudio,'Icône & splash',[fullName,branch],`dépôt ${fullName}`)},
      {label:'Supprimer le dépôt',icon:'trash',danger:true,fn:()=>confirmDeleteRepo(fullName)}
    ]);
    document.getElementById('renameRepoBtn').onclick = () => confirmRenameRepo(fullName);
    const branch = repoBranchSelection[fullName] || repo.default_branch;
    repoBranchSelection[fullName] = branch;
    persistNavigationState();
    const runsData = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs?branch=${encodeURIComponent(branch)}&per_page=1`).catch(() => ({ workflow_runs: [] }));
    const lastRun = runsData.workflow_runs && runsData.workflow_runs[0];
    const lastRunLabel = lastRun ? `Dernier run : ${lastRun.conclusion || lastRun.status}` : 'Aucun run récent';
    setBody(`
      <div class="branch-picker">
        <span class="li-ico">${ico('branch', 16)}</span>
        <select id="branchSelect" class="branch-select">
          ${branches.map(b => `<option value="${escapeHtml(b.name)}" ${b.name === branch ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(b.name)}${b.name === repo.default_branch ? ' (par défaut)' : ''}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>
        <button class="icon-btn" id="createBranchBtn" title="Créer une branche depuis la branche sélectionnée" aria-label="Créer une branche">${ico('plus', 15)}</button>
        <button class="icon-btn" id="compareBranchBtn" title="Comparer avec ${escapeHtml(repo.default_branch)}" aria-label="Comparer les branches">${ico('git', 15)}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="tile-grid">
        <div class="nav-tile" id="tileActions">
          <div class="ico">${ico('code', 19)}</div>
          <div style="flex:1;"><div class="tt">Commits &amp; Logs</div><div class="ts">${escapeHtml(lastRunLabel)} · branche "${escapeHtml(branch)}"</div></div>
          <span class="chevron">${ico('chevronRight', 18)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="nav-tile" id="tileFiles">
          <div class="ico">${ico('folder', 19)}</div>
          <div style="flex:1;"><div class="tt">Fichiers &amp; Éditeur</div><div class="ts">Sur la branche "${escapeHtml(branch)}"</div></div>
          <span class="chevron">${ico('chevronRight', 18)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="nav-tile" id="tileZipUpload">
          <div class="ico">${ico('upload', 19)}</div>
          <div style="flex:1;"><div class="tt">Importer un ZIP</div><div class="ts">Envoyer une archive dans ce dépôt · branche "${escapeHtml(branch)}"</div></div>
          <span class="chevron">${ico('chevronRight', 18)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="nav-tile" id="tileReleases">
          <div class="ico">${ico('tag', 19)}</div>
          <div style="flex:1;"><div class="tt">Releases &amp; APK</div><div class="ts">Télécharger un asset ou créer une release</div></div>
          <span class="chevron">${ico('chevronRight', 18)}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="nav-tile" id="tileIconSplash">
          <div class="ico">${ico('image', 19)}</div>
          <div style="flex:1;"><div class="tt">Icône &amp; splash screen</div><div class="ts">Créer, prévisualiser et pousser les ressources Android</div></div>
          <span class="chevron">${ico('chevronRight', 18)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="repo-meta">
        <span class="meta-chip">${ico('branch', 13)} ${escapeHtml(repo.default_branch)} (défaut du dépôt)</span>
        <span class="meta-chip">${ico(repo.private ? 'lock' : 'globe', 13)} ${repo.private ? 'Privé' : 'Public'}</span>
        ${repo.language ? `<span class="meta-chip">${escapeHtml(repo.language)}</span>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div class="action-row repo-page-actions">
        <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="toggleVisBtn">${ico(repo.private ? 'globe' : 'lock', 14)} Rendre ${repo.private ? 'public' : 'privé'}</button>
      </div>
    `);
    document.getElementById('branchSelect').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      repoBranchSelection[fullName] = e.target.value;
      persistNavigationState();
      renderRepoHome(fullName);
    });
    document.getElementById('tileActions').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const b = repoBranchSelection[fullName];
      navTo(renderActions, 'Commits', [fullName, b], `branche "${b}"`);
    });
    document.getElementById('tileFiles').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const b = repoBranchSelection[fullName];
      navTo(renderFiles, 'Fichiers', [fullName, '', b], `branche "${b}"`);
    });
    document.getElementById('tileZipUpload').addEventListener('click', () => openZipUpload(fullName, repoBranchSelection[fullName]));
    document.getElementById('tileReleases').addEventListener('click', () => navTo(renderReleases, 'Releases', [fullName, repoBranchSelection[fullName] || repo.default_branch], `dépôt ${fullName}`));
    document.getElementById('tileIconSplash').addEventListener('click', () => navTo(renderIconSplashStudio, 'Icône & splash', [fullName, repoBranchSelection[fullName] || repo.default_branch], `dépôt ${fullName}`));
    document.getElementById('createBranchBtn').onclick = () => createBranchFrom(fullName, branch);
    document.getElementById('compareBranchBtn').onclick = () => compareRepoBranches(fullName, branch, repo.default_branch);
    document.getElementById('toggleVisBtn').onclick = async () => {
      const makePrivate = !repo.private;
      const warn = makePrivate
        ? "Rendre ce dépôt privé ? Le code ne sera plus visible publiquement (certaines intégrations externes peuvent être affectées)."
        : "Rendre ce dépôt public ? Tout le monde pourra voir le code, l'historique et les issues.";
      const ok = await confirmModal(warn, { confirmLabel: makePrivate ? 'Rendre privé' : 'Rendre public', danger: true });
      if (!ok) return;
      const btn = document.getElementById('toggleVisBtn');
      setBtnLoading(btn, true);
      try{
        await gh(`/repos/${fullName}`, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ private: makePrivate }) });
        const cached = allRepos.find(r => r.full_name === fullName);
        if (cached) cached.private = makePrivate;
        toast(`Dépôt maintenant ${makePrivate ? 'privé' : 'public'}`, 'success');
        renderRepoHome(fullName);
      } catch(err){
        showError('Erreur de changement de visibilité', friendly(err));
        setBtnLoading(btn, false, `${ico(repo.private ? 'globe' : 'lock', 14)} Rendre ${repo.private ? 'public' : 'privé'}`);
      }
    };
  } catch(err){
    setBody(`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert', 30)}</div>${escapeHtml(friendly(err))}</div>`);
    showError('Erreur de chargement du dépôt', friendly(err));
  }
}

/**
 * Renomme le dépôt via l'API GitHub (PATCH /repos/{owner}/{repo}).
 * Le nom d'un dépôt GitHub ne peut contenir que lettres, chiffres, points,
 * tirets et underscores — on le vérifie avant d'envoyer pour éviter un
 * aller-retour réseau inutile. Après succès, l'écran passe au nouveau
 * fullName (owner/nouveau-nom) sans empiler un nouvel écran.
 */
async function confirmRenameRepo(fullName){
  const [owner, oldName] = fullName.split('/');
  const newName = await promptModal('Nom actuel :', { placeholder: oldName, value: oldName, okLabel: 'Renommer', title: 'Renommer le dépôt' });
  if (!newName) return;
  const clean = newName.trim();
  if (!clean || clean === oldName) return;
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$/.test(clean)){
    showError('Nom invalide', "Le nom d'un dépôt GitHub ne peut contenir que des lettres, chiffres, points, tirets et underscores.");
    return;
  }
  const ok = await confirmModal(`Renommer "${oldName}" en "${clean}" ? Les liens vers l'ancien nom resteront redirigés quelque temps par GitHub, mais mets à jour tes favoris.`, { confirmLabel: 'Renommer' });
  if (!ok) return;
  try{
    const updated = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}`, {
      method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ name: clean })
    });
    const newFullName = updated.full_name || `${owner}/${clean}`;
    const cached = allRepos.find(r => r.full_name === fullName);
    if (cached){ cached.name = clean; cached.full_name = newFullName; }
    if (repoBranchSelection[fullName] !== undefined){
      repoBranchSelection[newFullName] = repoBranchSelection[fullName];
      delete repoBranchSelection[fullName];
    }
    if (actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName] !== undefined){
      actionsWorkflowSelection[newFullName] = actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName];
      delete actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName];
    }
    toast(`Dépôt renommé en "${clean}"`, 'success');
    navReplaceTop(newFullName, [newFullName]);
    renderRepoHome(newFullName);
  } catch(err){
    showError('Erreur de renommage', friendly(err));
  }
}

async function confirmDeleteRepo(fullName){
  const [owner, repoName] = fullName.split('/');
  const phrase = 'NP.CLEANE';
  openModal({
    title: 'Supprimer définitivement le dépôt',
    bodyHTML: `<div class="delete-repo-warning"><div class="delete-repo-warning-icon">${ico('alert', 18)}</div><div><strong>Cette action est irréversible.</strong><p>Le dépôt, ses fichiers, branches, issues et son historique seront supprimés de GitHub.</p></div></div><p class="delete-repo-target">Dépôt visé : <strong>${escapeHtml(fullName)}</strong></p><div class="delete-repo-confirm-line"><label class="delete-repo-label" for="deleteRepoPhrase">Pour confirmer, saisis exactement <code>${phrase}</code> :</label><button class="icon-btn" type="button" id="copyDeletePhrase" title="Copier ${phrase}" aria-label="Copier le texte de confirmation">${ico('copy', 15)}</button></div><input id="deleteRepoPhrase" class="modal-input" type="text" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="${phrase}">`,
    footerHTML: `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="deleteRepoCancel">Annuler</button><button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" id="deleteRepoConfirm" disabled>${ico('trash', 14)} Supprimer le dépôt</button>`
  });
  const input = document.getElementById('deleteRepoPhrase');
  const confirmButton = document.getElementById('deleteRepoConfirm');
  setTimeout(() => input.focus(), 80);
  document.getElementById('copyDeletePhrase').onclick = async () => {
    try{
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) await navigator.clipboard.writeText(phrase);
      else throw new Error('Presse-papiers indisponible');
      toast(`${phrase} copié`, 'success', 2000);
    } catch(e){
      const helper = document.createElement('textarea');
      helper.value = phrase; helper.style.position = 'fixed'; helper.style.opacity = '0';
      document.body.appendChild(helper); helper.select();
      const copied = document.execCommand('copy'); helper.remove();
      toast(copied ? `${phrase} copié` : 'Copie impossible — sélectionne la phrase affichée.', copied ? 'success' : 'info', 2500);
    }
  };
  input.addEventListener('input', () => { confirmButton.disabled = input.value !== phrase; });
  document.getElementById('deleteRepoCancel').onclick = closeModal;
  input.addEventListener('keydown', event => { if (event.key === 'Enter' && !confirmButton.disabled) confirmButton.click(); });
  confirmButton.onclick = async () => {
    if (input.value !== phrase || confirmButton.disabled) return;
    setBtnLoading(confirmButton, true);
    try{
      await gh(`/repos/${owner}/${repoName}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
      closeModal();
      allRepos = allRepos.filter(repo => repo.full_name !== fullName);
      delete repoBranchSelection[fullName];
      delete actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName];
      toast(`Dépôt « ${fullName} » supprimé`, 'success');
      navReset(renderRepos, 'Dépôts');
    } catch(err){
      setBtnLoading(confirmButton, false);
      showError('Suppression impossible', `${friendly(err)}\n\nLe jeton GitHub doit disposer des droits d’administration et de suppression sur ce dépôt (permission Administration en écriture pour un jeton finement défini, ou scope delete_repo pour un jeton classique).`);
    }
  };
}

/* ============================================================
   ÉCRAN 3a : LISTE DES COMMITS (fidèle à l'affichage GitHub)
   On utilise l'API check-runs de GitHub pour calculer le MÊME statut
   agrégé que celui affiché nativement sur github.com : si un seul
   workflow échoue sur ce commit, le commit est marqué en échec, même
   si un autre workflow sur ce même commit a réussi.
============================================================ */
function computeAggregateStatus(checkRunsData){
  if (!checkRunsData || !checkRunsData.check_runs || !checkRunsData.check_runs.length) return 'none';
  const runs = checkRunsData.check_runs;
  if (runs.some(r => r.status !== 'completed')) return 'pending';
  if (runs.some(r => ['failure', 'timed_out', 'cancelled', 'action_required', 'stale'].includes(r.conclusion))) return 'failure';
  return 'success';
}

function parseWorkflowDispatchInputs(source){
  const lines = source.split(/\r?\n/);
  const eventIndex = lines.findIndex(line => /^\s*workflow_dispatch\s*:\s*(?:#.*)?$/.test(line));
  const inlineDispatch = /^\s*on\s*:\s*\[[^\]]*\bworkflow_dispatch\b[^\]]*\]/m.test(source);
  if (eventIndex < 0) return inlineDispatch ? [] : null;
  const indentation = line => (line.match(/^\s*/) || [''])[0].length;
  const eventIndent = indentation(lines[eventIndex]);
  let inputsIndex = -1, inputsIndent = -1;
  for (let i = eventIndex + 1; i < lines.length; i++){
    const text = lines[i].trim();
    if (!text || text.startsWith('#')) continue;
    const indent = indentation(lines[i]);
    if (indent <= eventIndent) break;
    if (/^inputs\s*:/.test(text)){ inputsIndex = i; inputsIndent = indent; break; }
  }
  if (inputsIndex < 0) return [];
  const fields = [];
  let current = null;
  for (let i = inputsIndex + 1; i < lines.length; i++){
    const line = lines[i], text = line.trim();
    if (!text || text.startsWith('#')) continue;
    const indent = indentation(line);
    if (indent <= inputsIndent) break;
    const item = line.match(/^\s{1,}([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)\s*:\s*(?:#.*)?$/);
    if (item && indent === inputsIndent + 2){
      current = { name: item[1], description: '', type: 'string', required: false, defaultValue: '', options: [] };
      fields.push(current);
      continue;
    }
    if (!current) continue;
    const property = text.match(/^(description|type|required|default)\s*:\s*(.*)$/);
    if (property){
      let val = property[2].replace(/\s+#.*$/, '').trim().replace(/^(['"])(.*)\1$/, '$2');
      if (property[1] === 'description') current.description = val;
      if (property[1] === 'type') current.type = val.toLowerCase();
      if (property[1] === 'required') current.required = val === 'true';
      if (property[1] === 'default') current.defaultValue = val;
    } else if (current.type === 'choice' && text.startsWith('- ')){
      current.options.push(text.slice(2).trim().replace(/^(['"])(.*)\1$/, '$2'));
    }
  }
  return fields;
}

async function getWorkflowDispatchDefinition(fullName, workflow){
  let repo = allRepos.find(r => r.full_name === fullName);
  if (!repo) repo = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}`);
  const defaultBranch = repo.default_branch || 'main';
  const file = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(workflow.path)}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(defaultBranch)}`);
  if (!file.content || file.encoding !== 'base64') throw new Error('Le fichier YAML de ce workflow est illisible.');
  const source = b64ToUtf8(file.content);
  return { defaultBranch, inputs: parseWorkflowDispatchInputs(source), source };
}

async function openWorkflowConfiguration(fullName, currentBranch, workflowId){
  try{
    const [workflow, branchesData] = await Promise.all([
      gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/workflows/${workflowId}`),
      ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/branches`).catch(() => [])
    ]);
    const definition = await getWorkflowDispatchDefinition(fullName, workflow);
    const inputs = definition.inputs;
    if (inputs === null) throw new Error('Ce workflow ne déclare pas « workflow_dispatch »; GitHub ne permet donc pas son lancement manuel.');
    const branches = Array.isArray(branchesData) ? branchesData.map(b => b.name) : [];
    const branchOptions = branches.length ? branches : [currentBranch];
    const environmentData = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/environments?per_page=100`).catch(() => ({ environments: [] }));
    const environments = (environmentData.environments || []).map(environment => environment.name);
    const inputsHtml = inputs.map((input, index) => {
      const id = `workflowInput${index}`;
      const required = input.required ? 'required' : '';
      const label = `${escapeHtml(input.name)}${input.required ? ' *' : ''}`;
      let control;
      if (input.type === 'boolean') control = `<input id="${id}" type="checkbox" ${input.defaultValue === 'true' ? 'checked' : ''}>`;
      else if (input.type === 'choice' && input.options.length) control = `<select id="${id}" ${required}>${input.options.map(option => `<option ${option === input.defaultValue ? 'selected' : ''} value="${escapeHtml(option)}">${escapeHtml(option)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
      else if (input.type === 'environment' && environments.length) control = `<select id="${id}" ${required}><option value="">Choisir un environnement…</option>${environments.map(environment => `<option ${environment === input.defaultValue ? 'selected' : ''} value="${escapeHtml(environment)}">${escapeHtml(environment)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
      else if (input.type === 'number') control = `<input id="${id}" type="number" value="${escapeHtml(input.defaultValue)}" ${required}>`;
      else control = `<input id="${id}" type="text" value="${escapeHtml(input.defaultValue)}" ${required}>`;
      return `<div class="workflow-input-row"><label for="${id}">${label}</label>${input.description ? `<small>${escapeHtml(input.description)}</small>` : ''}${control}</div>`;
    }).join('');
    openModal({
      title: `${escapeHtml(workflow.name || 'Configure workflow')}`,
      bodyHTML: `<div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700;margin:0 0 14px;">Configure workflow</div><div class="branch-picker" style="margin-bottom:14px;"><span class="li-ico">${ico('branch', 16)}</span><span id="workflowBranchValue" style="flex:1;font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(currentBranch)}</span><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="workflowBranchChange">CHANGE</button><select id="workflowDispatchBranch" class="branch-select" hidden>${branchOptions.map(name => `<option value="${escapeHtml(name)}" ${name === currentBranch ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(name)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><div id="workflowDispatchInputs">${inputsHtml || '<div class="modal-hint">Ce workflow ne demande aucun paramètre.</div>'}</div>`,
      footerHTML: `<button class="btn btn-primary btn-block" id="dispatchStartBtn">${ico('play', 15)} RUN WORKFLOW</button>`
    });
    document.getElementById('workflowBranchChange').onclick = () => {
      const selector = document.getElementById('workflowDispatchBranch');
      selector.hidden = false;
      selector.focus();
    };
    document.getElementById('workflowDispatchBranch').onchange = (event) => {
      document.getElementById('workflowBranchValue').textContent = event.target.value;
      event.target.hidden = true;
    };
    document.getElementById('dispatchStartBtn').onclick = async () => {
      const btn = document.getElementById('dispatchStartBtn');
      const inputValues = {};
      for (let i = 0; i < inputs.length; i++){
        const field = inputs[i], control = document.getElementById(`workflowInput${i}`);
        if (field.required && !control.value.trim()){ control.focus(); toast(`Le champ « ${field.name} » est obligatoire.`, 'error'); return; }
        inputValues[field.name] = field.type === 'boolean' ? String(control.checked) : control.value;
      }
      setBtnLoading(btn, true);
      try{
        const ref = document.getElementById('workflowDispatchBranch').value;
        await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/workflows/${workflowId}/dispatches`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ ref, inputs: inputValues }) });
        closeModal();
        toast('Workflow démarré sur GitHub Actions', 'success');
        repoBranchSelection[fullName] = ref;
        persistNavigationState();
        setTimeout(() => renderActions(fullName, ref), 1200);
      } catch(err){
        showError('Démarrage impossible', `${friendly(err)}\n\nVérifie les paramètres du workflow et que le token GitHub dispose de l’autorisation Actions en écriture.`);
      }
    };
  } catch(err){ showError('Configuration du workflow impossible', friendly(err)); }
}

/**
 * Liste des vrais runs GitHub Actions de la branche.
 * IMPORTANT : GitHub affiche toujours les runs d'UN SEUL workflow à la fois
 * (ex. "Build Android App" seul, numéroté #47, #48, #49… dans son propre
 * ordre). Mélanger plusieurs workflows (nps_exec, Build Android App, NPS
 * Terminal…) dans une seule liste, comme le faisait l'ancienne version,
 * entrelace des numérotations indépendantes et donne l'impression de
 * doublons ou d'un run "manquant" qui n'a en fait jamais existé pour CE
 * workflow-là. On reproduit donc le vrai fonctionnement : un sélecteur de
 * workflow en haut (comme la liste des workflows sur GitHub), et la liste
 * en dessous vient de l'endpoint dédié à ce workflow précis.
 */
async function renderActions(fullName, branch){
  stopLiveBuildMonitor();
  branch = branch || repoBranchSelection[fullName];
  if (!branch){ showError('Branche introuvable', "Reviens à l'accueil du dépôt pour choisir une branche."); return; }
  setScreenActions(`<button class="icon-btn" title="Actualiser" id="refreshRunsBtn">${ico('refresh', 16)}</button>`);
  document.getElementById('refreshRunsBtn').onclick = () => renderActions(fullName, branch);

  setBody(`<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement des workflows…</div>`);

  const perPage = 100;
  let page = 1;
  let headSha = null;
  let loadedRuns = [];
  let workflows = [];
  let dispatchDefinition = null;
  let dispatchWorkflowId = null;

  try{
    const refData = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/refs/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`);
    headSha = refData.object.sha;
  } catch(e){ /* pas bloquant : juste pas de badge "ACTUEL" */ }

  try{
    const wfData = await ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/workflows`, 'workflows');
    workflows = (wfData || []).filter(w => w.state === 'active');
  } catch(e){ /* pas bloquant : on retombe sur "Tous les workflows" uniquement */ }

  if (actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName] === undefined){actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName] = workflows.length ? String(workflows[0].id) : '';persistNavigationState();}

  function currentEndpoint(p){
    const wfId = actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName];
    return wfId
      ? `/repos/${fullName}/actions/workflows/${wfId}/runs?per_page=${perPage}&page=${p}`
      : `/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs?per_page=${perPage}&page=${p}`;
  }
  async function loadPage(p){
    const data = await gh(currentEndpoint(p));
    return data.workflow_runs || [];
  }
  async function loadHistory(){
    const all = [];
    for(let p = 1; p <= 1000; p++){
      const batch = await loadPage(p);
      all.push(...batch);
      if(batch.length < perPage){
        page = p;
        const unique=new Map();
        all.forEach(run=>{const key=run.head_sha||String(run.id);const previous=unique.get(key);if(!previous||new Date(run.created_at||0)>new Date(previous.created_at||0))unique.set(key,run);});
        return [...unique.values()].sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.created_at||0)-new Date(a.created_at||0));
      }
    }
    throw new Error('GitHub a renvoyé plus de 100 000 runs. Affine la recherche par workflow pour charger tout l’historique.');
  }
  function filterChipsHtml(){
    if (!workflows.length) return '';
    const sel = actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName];
    const chips = [`<button class="wf-chip ${sel === '' ? 'active' : ''}" data-wf-id="">Tous</button>`]
      .concat(workflows.map(w => `<button class="wf-chip ${String(sel) === String(w.id) ? 'active' : ''}" data-wf-id="${w.id}">${escapeHtml(w.name)}</button>`));
    return `<div class="wf-filter-row">${chips.join('')}</div>`;
  }

  function manualWorkflowPanelHtml(){
    const selected = workflows.find(w => String(w.id) === String(actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName]));
    if (!selected || String(selected.id) !== String(dispatchWorkflowId) || !dispatchDefinition || dispatchDefinition.inputs === null) return '';
    return `<div class="workflow-dispatch-panel"><div class="workflow-dispatch-copy"><strong>${escapeHtml(selected.name)}</strong>Ce workflow accepte le déclenchement manuel (<code>workflow_dispatch</code>), même sans run dans l’historique.
