Branche : « ${escapeHtml(branch)} ».</div><button class="workflow-dispatch-btn" data-run-workflow="${selected.id}">${ico('play', 15)} RUN WORKFLOW</button></div>`;
  }

  function runCardHtml(run, seenWorkflows){
    const isPending = run.status !== 'completed';
    const isOk = !isPending && run.conclusion === 'success';
    const cls = isPending ? 'pending' : (isOk ? 'ok' : 'fail');
    const iconName = isPending ? 'clock' : (isOk ? 'check' : 'xCircle');
    // "ACTUEL" ne doit marquer qu'UNE seule fois par workflow (le run le plus
    // récent qui pointe sur le HEAD réel de la branche) — sinon deux runs
    // rejoués sur le même commit se retrouvent tous les deux tagués "ACTUEL",
    // ce qui est le bug visible sur la capture (#63 et #62 tous les deux tagués).
    const isCurrent = headSha && run.head_sha === headSha && !seenWorkflows.has(run.workflow_id);
    if (isCurrent) seenWorkflows.add(run.workflow_id);
    const title = run.display_title || run.name || 'Run';
    const actor = (run.triggering_actor && run.triggering_actor.login) || (run.actor && run.actor.login) || '';
    const label = isPending ? (run.status === 'queued' ? 'en attente' : 'en cours') : (run.conclusion || run.status);
    return `<div class="list-item" style="align-items:flex-start;flex-direction:column;gap:0;cursor:default;">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:11px;width:100%;">
        <span class="step-ico ${cls}" style="margin-top:2px;">${ico(iconName, 20)}</span>
        <div class="li-main" data-run-click="${run.id}" style="cursor:pointer;">
          <div class="li-title">${escapeHtml(title)}${isCurrent ? ' <span style="color:var(--ok);font-size:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.3px;">· ACTUEL</span>' : ''}</div>
          <div class="li-sub">${escapeHtml(run.name)} #${run.run_number}${actor ? ` (via ${escapeHtml(actor)})` : ''} · ${escapeHtml(label)}</div>
          <div class="repo-meta" style="margin-top:8px;">
            <span class="meta-chip">${ico('clock', 12)} ${runDuration(run)}</span>
            <span class="meta-chip">${ico('branch', 12)} ${escapeHtml(run.head_branch || branch)}</span>
            <span class="meta-chip" title="Date et heure locales">${ico('calendar', 12)} ${relDate(run.created_at)} · ${escapeHtml(formatRunDateTime(run.created_at))}</span>
          </div>
          <div style="margin-top:7px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--text-muted);">${escapeHtml(run.display_title || run.name || 'Exécution')} · #${run.run_number}${run.head_commit?.message ? ` · ${escapeHtml(run.head_commit.message.split('\n')[0])}` : ''}</div>
        </div>
        <button class="icon-btn kebab-btn" data-kebab-run="${run.id}" title="Actions Git">${ico('more', 16)}</button>
        ${run.status === 'completed' ? `<button class="icon-btn" data-rerun-list="${run.id}" title="Relancer ce run">${ico('refresh', 15)}</button>` : ''}
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }

  function attachHandlers(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.wf-chip[data-wf-id]').forEach(el => {
      el.onclick = async () => {
        actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName] = el.dataset.wfId;
        persistNavigationState();
        dispatchDefinition = null;
        dispatchWorkflowId = null;
        page = 1;
        loadedRuns = [];
        setBody(`<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement…</div>`);
        try{
          const selected = workflows.find(w => String(w.id) === String(el.dataset.wfId));
          if (selected){
            try{
              const definition = await getWorkflowDispatchDefinition(fullName, selected);
              if (definition.inputs !== null){ dispatchDefinition = definition; dispatchWorkflowId = selected.id; }
            } catch(e){ /* un workflow inaccessible ou non compatible n'affiche pas RUN WORKFLOW */ }
          }
          loadedRuns = await loadHistory();
          renderList(false);
        } catch(err){ setBody(`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert', 30)}</div>${escapeHtml(friendly(err))}</div>`); showError('Erreur de chargement', friendly(err)); }
      };
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-run-workflow]').forEach(el => {
      el.onclick = () => openWorkflowConfiguration(fullName, branch, el.dataset.runWorkflow);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-run-click]').forEach(el => {
      el.onclick = () => {
        const run = loadedRuns.find(r => String(r.id) === el.dataset.runClick);
        if (run) navTo(renderRunDetail, (run.head_sha || '').slice(0, 7), [fullName, run.head_sha], `branche "${run.head_branch || branch}"`);
      };
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-kebab-run]').forEach(el => {
      el.onclick = (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const run = loadedRuns.find(r => String(r.id) === el.dataset.kebabRun);
        if (run) openRunGitActions(fullName, { sha: run.head_sha, branch: run.head_branch || branch, date: run.created_at, message: run.display_title || run.name, runId: run.id });
      };
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-rerun-list]').forEach(el => {
      el.onclick = async (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const run = loadedRuns.find(r => String(r.id) === String(el.dataset.rerunList));
        if (!run) return;
        const started = await rerunWorkflowRun(fullName, run.id, el, `${run.name} #${run.run_number}`);
        if (started) setTimeout(() => renderActions(fullName, branch), 1200);
      };
    });

  }

  function renderList(){
    const seenWorkflows = new Set();
    const cards = loadedRuns.map(r => runCardHtml(r, seenWorkflows)).join('');
    const footer = loadedRuns.length
      ? `<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11.5px;padding:14px 0;">Historique unique par commit · ${loadedRuns.length} version(s).</div>`
      : '';
    const body = cards + footer || `<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('inbox', 30)}</div>Aucun run GitHub Actions dans ce dépôt${actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName] ? ' pour ce workflow' : ''}.</div>`;
    setBody(filterChipsHtml() + manualWorkflowPanelHtml() + body);
    attachHandlers();
  }

  try{
    const selectedWorkflow = workflows.find(w => String(w.id) === String(actionsWorkflowSelection[fullName]));
    if (selectedWorkflow){
      try{
        const definition = await getWorkflowDispatchDefinition(fullName, selectedWorkflow);
        if (definition.inputs !== null){ dispatchDefinition = definition; dispatchWorkflowId = selectedWorkflow.id; }
      } catch(e){ /* sans fichier lisible/config dispatch, le bouton reste masqué */ }
    }
    loadedRuns = await loadHistory();
    renderList();
    const stillOnThisScreen=()=>{const top=nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1];return !!top&&top.renderFn===renderActions&&top.args[0]===fullName&&top.args[1]===branch;};
    if(stillOnThisScreen()&&loadedRuns.some(run=>run.status!=='completed'))startLiveBuildMonitor(`actions:${fullName}:${branch}`,async()=>{
      if(!stillOnThisScreen())return false;
      const fresh=await loadPage(1);
      const freshIds=new Set(fresh.map(run=>String(run.id)));
      const older=loadedRuns.filter(run=>!freshIds.has(String(run.id)));
      const merged=[...fresh,...older],unique=new Map();
      merged.forEach(run=>{const key=run.head_sha||String(run.id);const previous=unique.get(key);if(!previous||new Date(run.created_at||0)>new Date(previous.created_at||0))unique.set(key,run);});
      loadedRuns=[...unique.values()].sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.created_at||0)-new Date(a.created_at||0));
      renderList();return loadedRuns.some(run=>run.status!=='completed');
    });
  } catch(err){
    setBody(filterChipsHtml() + `<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert', 30)}</div>${escapeHtml(friendly(err))}</div>`);
    attachHandlers();
    showError('Erreur de chargement des runs', friendly(err));
  }
}

/* ---------- Fiche complète d’une version / d’un run ---------- */
function runInfoRow(label,value,options={}){
  const display=value===undefined||value===null||value===''?'—':String(value);
  const content=options.href?`<a href="${escapeHtml(options.href)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:var(--accent);overflow-wrap:anywhere;">${escapeHtml(display)}</a>`:escapeHtml(display);
  const copy=options.copy?`<button type="button" class="icon-btn run-info-copy" data-copy-info="${escapeHtml(display)}" title="Copier ${escapeHtml(label)}" aria-label="Copier ${escapeHtml(label)}">${ico('copy',13)}</button>`:'';
  return `<div class="run-info-row"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><strong>${content}${copy}</strong></div>`;
}
function runInfoSection(title,body){return `<section class="run-info-section"><h3>${escapeHtml(title)}</h3>${body}</section>`;}
async function showRunInformation(fullName,ctx){
  openModal({title:'Informations de la version',bodyHTML:'<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement de toutes les informations…</div>'});
  try{
    const run=ctx.runId?await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${ctx.runId}`):null;
    if(!run)throw new Error('Les détails de ce run ne sont plus disponibles.');
    const [jobsData,artifacts,workflows,releases]=await Promise.all([
      gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${run.id}/jobs?per_page=100`).catch(()=>({jobs:[]})),
      ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${run.id}/artifacts`,'artifacts').catch(()=>[]),
      ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/workflows`,'workflows').catch(()=>[]),
      ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/releases`).catch(()=>[])
    ]);
    const workflow=workflows.find(item=>String(item.id)===String(run.workflow_id));
    const jobs=jobsData.jobs||[];
    const buildRelease=(releases||[]).find(release=>String(release.tag_name||'').toLowerCase()===`build-${run.run_number}`.toLowerCase())||null;
    const apkArtifacts=artifacts.filter(item=>/apk/i.test(item.name||''));
    const apkAssets=(buildRelease?.assets||[]).filter(item=>/\.apk$/i.test(item.name||''));
    const actor=run.triggering_actor?.login||run.actor?.login||'—';
    const commitMessage=run.head_commit?.message?.split('\n')[0]||ctx.message||'—';
    const status=run.status==='completed'?(run.conclusion||run.status):`${run.status||'—'} · en cours`;
    const inputSource=run.inputs&&Object.keys(run.inputs).length?run.inputs:null;
    const inputRows=inputSource?Object.entries(inputSource).map(([key,value])=>runInfoRow(key,value)).join(''):`<div class="modal-hint">GitHub ne renvoie pas toujours les paramètres saisis du déclenchement manuel. Le nom visible de l’application est toutefois repris dans le titre de la release quand elle existe.</div>`;
    const artifactRows=artifacts.length?artifacts.map((item,index)=>`<div class="run-info-row run-info-artifact"><span>${escapeHtml(item.name||'Artefact')} · ID</span><strong><span>${escapeHtml(String(item.id))} · ${formatTransferBytes(item.size_in_bytes||0)} · ${item.expired?'expiré':'actif'} <button type="button" class="icon-btn run-info-copy" data-copy-info="${escapeHtml(String(item.id))}" title="Copier l’ID artefact" aria-label="Copier l’ID artefact">${ico('copy',13)}</button></span><button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm run-info-artifact-download" data-info-artifact="${index}" ${item.expired?'disabled':''}>${ico('download',13)} ${/apk/i.test(item.name||'')?'Télécharger / installer':'Télécharger'}</button></strong></div>`).join(''):'<div class="modal-hint">Aucun artefact pour ce run.</div>';
    const jobRows=jobs.length?jobs.map(job=>{const start=job.started_at?new Date(job.started_at).getTime():0;const end=job.completed_at?new Date(job.completed_at).getTime():Date.now();const seconds=start?Math.max(0,Math.round((end-start)/1000)):0;const duration=formatCompactDuration(seconds);return runInfoRow(`${job.name} · ID job`,`${job.id} · ${job.conclusion||job.status||'—'} · ${start?duration:'durée inconnue'}`);}).join(''):'<div class="modal-hint">Détails des jobs indisponibles.</div>';
    const releaseBody=buildRelease?runInfoRow('Release',buildRelease.name||buildRelease.tag_name,{href:buildRelease.html_url})+runInfoRow('ID release',buildRelease.id)+runInfoRow('Tag',buildRelease.tag_name)+runInfoRow('Publication',buildRelease.published_at?`${relDate(buildRelease.published_at)} · ${formatRunDateTime(buildRelease.published_at)}`:'non publiée / brouillon')+(apkAssets.length?apkAssets.map(asset=>runInfoRow(`APK · ${asset.name}`,`${asset.id} · ${formatTransferBytes(asset.size||0)}`)).join(''):'<div class="modal-hint">Aucun APK joint à cette release.</div>'):'<div class="modal-hint">Aucune release build associée trouvée pour ce run.</div>';
    const body=document.querySelector('#modalOverlay .modal-body');
    if(!body)return;
    body.innerHTML=`<div class="run-info-book">
      <div class="run-info-hero"><strong>${escapeHtml(run.display_title||run.name||'Version')}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(status)} · ${escapeHtml(run.name||'Workflow')}</span></div>
      ${runInfoSection('Publication / exécution',runInfoRow('Nom de l’application / titre',buildRelease?.name||run.display_title||run.name)+runInfoRow('Workflow',workflow?.name||run.name)+runInfoRow('Événement',run.event)+runInfoRow('Déclenché par',actor)+runInfoRow('Branche',run.head_branch||ctx.branch)+runInfoRow('Commit',run.head_sha,{href:`https://github.com/${fullName}/commit/${run.head_sha}`})+runInfoRow('Message',commitMessage)+runInfoRow('Début',run.run_started_at?`${relDate(run.run_started_at)} · ${formatRunDateTime(run.run_started_at)}`:'—')+runInfoRow('Créé',`${relDate(run.created_at)} · ${formatRunDateTime(run.created_at)}`)+runInfoRow('Mis à jour',`${relDate(run.updated_at)} · ${formatRunDateTime(run.updated_at)}`)+runInfoRow('Durée',runDuration(run))+runInfoRow('Lien GitHub',run.html_url||`https://github.com/${fullName}/actions/runs/${run.id}`,{href:run.html_url}))}
      ${runInfoSection('Android build APK',runInfoRow('Workflow',workflow?.path||run.workflow_path||'—')+runInfoRow('Version du run',`#${run.run_number}`)+runInfoRow('Tentative',run.run_attempt||1)+runInfoRow('APK détectés',`${apkArtifacts.length} artefact(s) · ${apkAssets.length} publication(s)`)+inputRows)}
      ${runInfoSection('Artefacts publiés',artifactRows)}
      ${runInfoSection('Jobs / étapes',jobRows)}
      ${runInfoSection('Release et fichiers APK',releaseBody)}
      ${runInfoSection('Identifiants techniques',runInfoRow('ID du run',run.id,{copy:true})+runInfoRow('Node ID du run',run.node_id,{copy:true})+runInfoRow('ID workflow',run.workflow_id,{copy:true})+runInfoRow('ID check suite',run.check_suite_id,{copy:true})+runInfoRow('ID dépôt',run.repository_id,{copy:true})+runInfoRow('ID utilisateur',run.actor?.id||run.triggering_actor?.id,{copy:true})+runInfoRow('URL logs',run.logs_url,{href:run.logs_url})+runInfoRow('URL jobs',run.jobs_url,{href:run.jobs_url})+runInfoRow('URL artefacts',run.artifacts_url,{href:run.artifacts_url}))}
    </div>`;
    body.querySelectorAll('[data-copy-info]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>copyTextValue(button.dataset.copyInfo,button.title.replace(/^Copier /,'')));
    body.querySelectorAll('[data-info-artifact]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      const artifact=artifacts[Number(button.dataset.infoArtifact)];if(!artifact||artifact.expired)return;
      const url=`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/actions/artifacts/${artifact.id}/zip`;
      closeModal();releasePanelSelection[fullName]='artifacts';persistNavigationState();
      navTo(renderReleases,'Releases',[fullName,ctx.branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName]],`dépôt ${fullName}`);
      setTimeout(()=>/apk/i.test(artifact.name||'')?transferGithubArtifactApk(url,artifact.name,artifact.size_in_bytes):transferGithubResource(url,`${artifact.name||'artefact'}.zip`,'application/zip',artifact.size_in_bytes,{title:'Téléchargement de l’artefact',accept:'application/vnd.github+json'}),350);
    });
  }catch(error){
    const body=document.querySelector('#modalOverlay .modal-body');
    if(body)body.innerHTML=`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert',28)}</div>Informations indisponibles<p class="modal-hint">${escapeHtml(friendly(error))}</p><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="closeModal()">Fermer</button></div>`;
  }
}

async function showCommitChanges(fullName, ctx){
  const shortSha = (ctx.sha || '').slice(0, 7);
  openModal({ title: `Modifications · ${shortSha}`, bodyHTML: `<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement des modifications…</div>` });
  try{
    const data = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/commits/${encodeURIComponent(ctx.sha)}`);
    const files = data.files || [];
    const body = document.querySelector('#modalOverlay .modal-body');
    if (!body) return;
    if (!files.length){
      body.innerHTML = `<div class="modal-hint">Aucun fichier modifié trouvé pour ce commit.</div>`;
      return;
    }
    const statusLabel = { added: 'Fichier ajouté', removed: 'Fichier supprimé', renamed: 'Fichier renommé', copied: 'Fichier copié' };
    const rows = files.map(file => {
      const label = statusLabel[file.status] || 'Fichier modifié';
      const name = file.status === 'renamed' && file.previous_filename
        ? `${escapeHtml(file.previous_filename)} → ${escapeHtml(file.filename)}`
        : escapeHtml(file.filename);
      let content;
      if (file.patch){
        content = `<div class="commit-file-patch">${file.patch.split('\n').map(line => {
          const cls = /^\+(?!\+\+)/.test(line) ? 'add' : /^-(?!--)/.test(line) ? 'remove' : 'context';
          return `<div class="diff-line ${cls}">${escapeHtml(line) || '&nbsp;'}</div>`;
        }).join('')}</div>`;
      } else if (file.status === 'added'){
        content = `<div class="modal-hint">Nouveau fichier${file.binary ? ' (binaire)' : ''} — aucun aperçu textuel disponible.</div>`;
      } else if (file.status === 'removed'){
        content = `<div class="modal-hint">Fichier supprimé — aucun aperçu disponible.</div>`;
      } else {
        content = `<div class="modal-hint">Fichier binaire ou trop volumineux — aucun aperçu disponible.</div>`;
      }
      return `<div class="commit-file-block"><div class="commit-file-head"><span class="commit-file-label">${escapeHtml(label)}</span><span class="commit-file-name">${name}</span><span class="commit-file-stats">+${file.additions || 0} −${file.deletions || 0}</span></div>${content}</div>`;
    }).join('');
    body.innerHTML = `<div class="diff-summary"><strong>${files.length} fichier(s) modifié(s)</strong><span>+${data.stats?.additions || 0} ajoutées</span><span style="color:var(--danger);">−${data.stats?.deletions || 0} supprimées</span></div>${rows}`;
  }catch(error){
    const body = document.querySelector('#modalOverlay .modal-body');
    if (body) body.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert', 28)}</div>Modifications indisponibles<p class="modal-hint">${escapeHtml(friendly(error))}</p></div>`;
  }
}
/* ---------- Actions Git sur un commit (reset / branche / revert d'une seule commit) ---------- */
function openRunGitActions(fullName, ctx){
  const shortSha = (ctx.sha || '').slice(0, 7);
  const pageUrl=ctx.runId?`https://github.com/${fullName}/actions/runs/${ctx.runId}`:`https://github.com/${fullName}/commit/${ctx.sha}/checks`;
  const items = [
    { label: 'Informations complètes de la version', icon: 'info', fn: () => showRunInformation(fullName, ctx) },
    { label: 'Voir les modifications', icon: 'git', fn: () => showCommitChanges(fullName, ctx) },
    { label: 'Revenir à cette version', icon: 'restore', danger: true, fn: () => confirmHardReset(fullName, ctx) },
    { label: 'Créer une branche ici', icon: 'branch', fn: () => confirmCreateBranch(fullName, ctx) },
    { label: 'Annuler seulement cette commit', icon: 'undo', fn: () => confirmRevertSingle(fullName, ctx) },
    { label: 'Supprimer les runs de ce commit', icon: 'trash', danger: true, fn: () => confirmDeleteRun(fullName, ctx) }
  ];
  if (ctx.runId){
    items.unshift({ label: 'Télécharger les artefacts du run', icon: 'download', fn: () => showRunArtifacts(fullName, ctx.runId) });
    items.unshift({ label: 'Télécharger la dernière Release', icon: 'tag', fn: () => downloadLatestRelease(fullName) });
  }
  openActionSheet(`${shortSha} · ${ctx.branch || ''}`, items,{url:pageUrl,title:ctx.runId?`Run · ${shortSha}`:`Commit · ${shortSha}`});
}
async function showRunArtifacts(fullName,runId){
  openModal({title:'Artefacts GitHub Actions',bodyHTML:`<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement des artefacts…</div>`,footerHTML:`<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="artifactReleasesBtn">${ico('tag',14)} Télécharger une Release</button><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="artifactModalClose">Fermer</button>`});
  document.getElementById('artifactReleasesBtn').onclick=()=>{closeModal();downloadLatestRelease(fullName);};
  document.getElementById('artifactModalClose').onclick=closeModal;
  try{
    const [artifacts,run]=await Promise.all([
      ghAll(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${runId}/artifacts`,'artifacts'),
      gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${runId}`)
    ]);
    const modalBody=document.querySelector('#modalOverlay .modal-body');if(!modalBody)return;
    const runUrl=`https://github.com/${fullName}/actions/runs/${runId}`;
    const runMeta=`<div class="modal-hint" style="margin-bottom:10px;">Run #${escapeHtml(run.run_number)} · ${escapeHtml(run.head_branch||'')} · ${escapeHtml(relDate(run.created_at))} · ${escapeHtml(formatRunDateTime(run.created_at))}</div>`;
    modalBody.innerHTML=runMeta+(artifacts.length?artifacts.map((artifact,index)=>`<div class="release-asset"><span class="li-ico">${ico('download',16)}</span><div class="li-main"><div class="li-title">${escapeHtml(artifact.name)}</div><div class="li-sub">${(artifact.size_in_bytes/1048576).toFixed(2)} Mo · créé ${escapeHtml(relDate(artifact.created_at))} · ${escapeHtml(formatRunDateTime(artifact.created_at))} · expire ${escapeHtml(relDate(artifact.expires_at))}${artifact.expired?' · expiré':''}</div></div><button class="icon-btn" data-run-artifact-download="${index}" title="Télécharger" ${artifact.expired?'disabled':''}>${ico('download',15)}</button><button class="icon-btn" data-run-artifact-menu="${index}" title="Autres actions">${ico('more',15)}</button></div>`).join(''):`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('inbox',28)}</div>Aucun artefact disponible pour ce run.</div>`);
    modalBody.querySelectorAll('[data-run-artifact-download]').forEach(button=>{
      const remove=document.createElement('button');remove.type='button';remove.className='icon-btn';remove.title='Supprimer tout l’artefact';remove.setAttribute('aria-label','Supprimer tout l’artefact Actions');remove.innerHTML=ico('trash',15);remove.dataset.runArtifactDelete=button.dataset.runArtifactDownload;button.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',remove);
    });
    modalBody.querySelectorAll('[data-run-artifact-download]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=async()=>{
      const artifact=artifacts[Number(button.dataset.runArtifactDownload)];if(!artifact||artifact.expired){toast('Cet artefact a expiré.','error');return;}
      setBtnLoading(button,true);
      await transferGithubArtifactApk(`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/actions/artifacts/${artifact.id}/zip`,artifact.name,artifact.size_in_bytes);
      if(button.isConnected)setBtnLoading(button,false,ico('download',15));
    });
    modalBody.querySelectorAll('[data-run-artifact-delete]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      const artifact=artifacts[Number(button.dataset.runArtifactDelete)];if(artifact)deleteRunArtifact(fullName,artifact,runId);
    });
    modalBody.querySelectorAll('[data-run-artifact-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>{
      const artifact=artifacts[Number(button.dataset.runArtifactMenu)];if(!artifact)return;
      const items=[];
      if(!artifact.expired){
        const zipUrl=`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/actions/artifacts/${artifact.id}/zip`,zipName=`${artifact.name}.zip`;
        items.push({label:'Ouvrir l’archive ZIP avec une application externe',icon:'external',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(zipUrl,zipName,'application/zip',artifact.size_in_bytes,{title:'Ouverture de l’archive',accept:'application/vnd.github+json',openAfter:true})});
        items.push({label:'Télécharger l’archive ZIP complète',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(zipUrl,zipName,'application/zip',artifact.size_in_bytes,{title:'Téléchargement de l’archive',accept:'application/vnd.github+json'})});
        items.push({label:'Télécharger l’APK uniquement',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubArtifactApk(zipUrl,artifact.name,artifact.size_in_bytes)});
      }
      items.push({label:'Supprimer tout l’artefact Actions',icon:'trash',danger:true,fn:()=>deleteRunArtifact(fullName,artifact,runId)});
      openActionSheet(artifact.name,items,{url:runUrl,title:`Run · ${artifact.name}`});
    });
  }catch(err){showError('Chargement des artefacts impossible',friendly(err));}
}
async function deleteRunArtifact(fullName,artifact,runId){
  const confirmed=await confirmModal(`Supprimer l’artefact Actions « ${artifact.name} » de ce run ? Tous les fichiers de son archive — APK, médias et autres — seront supprimés ensemble.`,{title:'Supprimer l’artefact Actions ?',confirmLabel:'Supprimer l’artefact',danger:true});
  if(!confirmed)return;
  try{
    await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/artifacts/${artifact.id}`,{method:'DELETE'});
    toast('Artefact Actions supprimé.','success');
    showRunArtifacts(fullName,runId);
  }catch(error){showError('Suppression de l’artefact impossible',friendly(error));}
}
async function confirmDeleteRun(fullName, ctx){
  const shortSha = (ctx.sha || '').slice(0, 7);
  const ok = await confirmModal(`Supprimer tous les runs GitHub Actions associés au commit ${shortSha} ? Le code du dépôt n'est pas affecté, seul le journal Actions est nettoyé.`, { confirmLabel: 'Supprimer', danger: true });
  if (!ok) return;
  try{
    const data = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs?head_sha=${ctx.sha}&per_page=50`);
    const runs = data.workflow_runs || [];
    let deleted = 0, failed = 0;
    for (const r of runs){
      try{ await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${r.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }); deleted++; }
      catch(e){ failed++; }
    }
    toast(deleted ? `${deleted} run(s) supprimé(s) pour ce commit${failed ? `, ${failed} échec(s)` : ''}` : 'Aucun run trouvé pour ce commit', deleted ? 'success' : 'info');
    renderActions(fullName, ctx.branch);
  } catch(err){
    showError('Erreur de suppression du run', friendly(err));
  }
}

async function confirmHardReset(fullName, ctx){
  const branch = ctx.branch;
  const shortSha = (ctx.sha || '').slice(0, 7);
  if (!branch){ showError('Action impossible', "La branche cible est introuvable."); return; }
  const ok = await confirmModal(
    `Réinitialiser la branche "${branch}" sur le commit ${shortSha} ? Tous les commits ajoutés après disparaîtront de son historique, ET tous les runs Actions plus récents que celui-ci seront supprimés pour un retour propre (au lieu de rester en double) — y compris un éventuel nouveau run que le reset lui-même redéclenche. Cette action est difficile à annuler.`,
    { confirmLabel: 'Continuer', danger: true }
  );
  if (!ok) return;
  const typed = await promptModal('Pour confirmer cette action irréversible, tape RESET en majuscules :', { placeholder: 'RESET', okLabel: 'Réinitialiser', title: 'Confirmation finale' });
  if (!typed || typed.trim().toUpperCase() !== 'RESET'){
    if (typed !== null) toast('Confirmation incorrecte — action annulée.', 'error');
    return;
  }

  // On protège UNIQUEMENT le run précisément cliqué (ctx.runId) — pas "tous les
  // runs qui partagent ce sha". Un rerun manuel antérieur (ex. #52) peut partager
  // le même head_sha que le run ciblé (#45) sans être le run visé : le protéger
  // par sha l'aurait laissé passer devant #45. En ne gardant que son propre ID,
  // cleanupNewerRuns supprime bien tout le reste au même sha, y compris #52.
  const existingRunIds = new Set();
  if (ctx.runId !== undefined && ctx.runId !== null) existingRunIds.add(ctx.runId);

  try{
    await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/refs/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`, {
      method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ sha: ctx.sha, force: true })
    });
    // On ne fait pas confiance à une réponse "200 OK" les yeux fermés : on
    // relit la branche pour vérifier qu'elle pointe VRAIMENT sur ctx.sha
    // maintenant. Si une règle de protection de branche bloque le
    // force-push, GitHub peut renvoyer une erreur claire — mais dans le
    // doute, cette vérification transforme un échec silencieux en message
    // explicite au lieu de laisser l'écran se recharger sans rien changer.
    const after = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/refs/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`);
    if (after.object.sha !== ctx.sha){
      showError(
        'Réinitialisation refusée par GitHub',
        `La branche "${branch}" pointe toujours sur ${after.object.sha.slice(0, 7)} au lieu de ${shortSha}. GitHub a accepté la requête mais n'a pas appliqué le changement — c'est le signe d'une règle de protection de branche (ex. "Require pull request", "Restrict force pushes") active sur "${branch}". Il faut la désactiver temporairement dans les paramètres du dépôt sur GitHub pour pouvoir forcer ce retour en arrière.`
      );
      return;
    }
    toast(`Branche "${branch}" réinitialisée sur ${shortSha}`, 'success');
  } catch(err){
    showError('Erreur de réinitialisation', friendly(err));
    return;
  }
  try{
    await cleanupNewerRuns(fullName, ctx, existingRunIds);
  } catch(err){
    showError('Reset appliqué, mais le nettoyage a échoué', friendly(err));
  }
}

/**
 * Après un reset, supprime tous les runs GitHub Actions qui ne faisaient pas
 * partie de la version ciblée AVANT le reset — qu'il s'agisse de runs de
 * commits plus récents, ou d'un nouveau run que le push forcé du reset
 * redéclenche sur le commit cible lui-même. Résultat : la version ciblée
 * (ex. #45) reste seule en tête, rien de nouveau ne s'affiche devant elle.
 * Deux passes espacées de quelques secondes, car GitHub met parfois un
 * court instant à déclencher ce nouveau run après le push forcé.
 */
async function cleanupNewerRuns(fullName, ctx, existingRunIds = new Set()){
  const targetTime = new Date(ctx.date).getTime();
  async function purgePass(){
    const data = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs?per_page=100`);
    const runs = data.workflow_runs || [];
    const toDelete = runs.filter(r => !existingRunIds.has(r.id) && new Date(r.created_at).getTime() > targetTime);
    let deleted = 0, failed = 0;
    for (const r of toDelete){
      try{ await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${r.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }); deleted++; }
      catch(e){ failed++; }
    }
    return { deleted, failed };
  }
  try{
    const first = await purgePass();
    await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 4000));
    const second = await purgePass();
    const deleted = first.deleted + second.deleted;
    const failed = first.failed + second.failed;
    toast(deleted ? `Historique nettoyé : ${deleted} run(s) supprimé(s)${failed ? `, ${failed} échec(s)` : ''}` : 'Aucun run superflu à nettoyer', failed ? 'info' : 'success', 4500);
  } catch(err){
    toast('Reset effectué, mais le nettoyage des anciens runs a échoué : ' + friendly(err), 'error', 5000);
  }
  renderActions(fullName, ctx.branch);
}


async function confirmCreateBranch(fullName, ctx){
  const shortSha = (ctx.sha || '').slice(0, 7);
  const name = await promptModal(`Nom de la nouvelle branche à créer sur le commit ${shortSha} :`, { placeholder: 'ma-nouvelle-branche', okLabel: 'Créer' });
  if (!name) return;
  const clean = name.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '-');
  try{
    await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/refs`, {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ ref: `refs/heads/${clean}`, sha: ctx.sha })
    });
    toast(`Branche "${clean}" créée sur ${shortSha}`, 'success');
  } catch(err){
    showError('Erreur de création de branche', friendly(err));
  }
}

async function confirmRevertSingle(fullName, ctx){
  const shortSha = (ctx.sha || '').slice(0, 7);
  if (!ctx.branch){ showError('Action impossible', "La branche cible est introuvable."); return; }
  const ok = await confirmModal(
    `Annuler uniquement les changements du commit ${shortSha} ? Un nouveau commit sera ajouté sur "${ctx.branch}" pour inverser ce changement précis, sans toucher aux commits suivants.`,
    { confirmLabel: 'Annuler ce commit', danger: true }
  );
  if (!ok) return;
  try{
    await revertSingleCommit(fullName, ctx.branch, ctx.sha);
    toast(`Commit ${shortSha} annulé sur "${ctx.branch}"`, 'success');
  } catch(err){
    showError("Erreur d'annulation du commit", friendly(err));
  }
}

/**
 * Annule UNIQUEMENT le contenu d'un commit (comme `git revert <sha>`), en
 * ajoutant un nouveau commit par-dessus le HEAD actuel de la branche — aucun
 * commit existant n'est supprimé ni réécrit.
 * Limite connue : ne gère pas les commits de fusion (plusieurs parents), et
 * les fichiers renommés+modifiés en même temps sont restaurés sous leur ancien nom.
 */
async function revertSingleCommit(fullName, branch, commitSha){
  const commit = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/commits/${commitSha}`);
  if (!commit.parents || commit.parents.length !== 1){
    throw new Error(commit.parents && commit.parents.length > 1
      ? "Ce commit est une fusion (merge) : l'annulation simple n'est pas prise en charge."
      : "Ce commit n'a pas de parent (commit initial du dépôt) : rien à annuler.");
  }
  const parentSha = commit.parents[0].sha;
  const files = commit.files || [];
  if (!files.length) throw new Error('Aucun changement de fichier trouvé pour ce commit.');

  const refData = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/refs/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`);
  const headSha = refData.object.sha;
  const headCommit = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/commits/${headSha}`);
  const baseTreeSha = headCommit.tree.sha;

  const treeEntries = [];
  for (const f of files){
    if (f.status === 'added'){
      treeEntries.push({ path: f.filename, mode: '100644', type: 'blob', sha: null });
    } else if (f.status === 'removed'){
      const blobSha = await getBlobShaAtRef(fullName, f.filename, parentSha);
      treeEntries.push({ path: f.filename, mode: '100644', type: 'blob', sha: blobSha });
    } else if (f.status === 'renamed'){
      treeEntries.push({ path: f.filename, mode: '100644', type: 'blob', sha: null });
      const blobSha = await getBlobShaAtRef(fullName, f.previous_filename, parentSha);
      treeEntries.push({ path: f.previous_filename, mode: '100644', type: 'blob', sha: blobSha });
    } else {
      const blobSha = await getBlobShaAtRef(fullName, f.filename, parentSha);
      treeEntries.push({ path: f.filename, mode: '100644', type: 'blob', sha: blobSha });
    }
  }

  const newTree = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/trees`, {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ base_tree: baseTreeSha, tree: treeEntries })
  });
  const shortSha = commitSha.slice(0, 7);
  const newCommit = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/commits`, {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ message: `Revert commit ${shortSha} (via NPS GitHub Studio)`, tree: newTree.sha, parents: [headSha] })
  });
  await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/refs/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`, {
    method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ sha: newCommit.sha })
  });
}

async function getBlobShaAtRef(fullName, path, ref){
  const data = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(ref)}`);
  if (Array.isArray(data)) throw new Error(`"${path}" est un dossier, impossible de restaurer.`);
  return data.sha;
}

/* ============================================================
   ÉCRAN 3b : DÉTAIL D'UN COMMIT — tous les workflows qui lui sont associés
   (un même commit peut déclencher plusieurs workflows séparés ; c'est ce qui
   explique qu'un commit puisse être vert dans une vue mais qu'un de ses
   workflows soit rouge — chacun est listé ici individuellement).
============================================================ */
async function renderRunDetail(fullName, commitSha, liveRefresh=false){
  try{
    const data = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs?head_sha=${commitSha}&per_page=30`);
    const runsByCommit = new Map();
    (data.workflow_runs || []).forEach(run=>{
      const key=run.head_sha||String(run.id),previous=runsByCommit.get(key);
      if(!previous||new Date(run.created_at||0)>new Date(previous.created_at||0))runsByCommit.set(key,run);
    });
    const runs = [...runsByCommit.values()];
    if (!runs.length){
      setBody(`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('inbox', 30)}</div>Aucun workflow GitHub Actions associé à ce commit.</div>`);
      return false;
    }
    const activeRuns=runs.filter(run=>run.status!=='completed');
    const jobsPerRun = await Promise.all(runs.map(r => gh(`/repos/${fullName}/actions/runs/${r.id}/jobs`).catch(() => ({ jobs: [] }))));
    let html = activeRuns.length?`<div class="status-live" role="status"><span class="live-dot"></span><span>${activeRuns.length} build${activeRuns.length>1?'s':''} en cours · progression mise à jour automatiquement</span><span class="live-refresh-label">3 s</span></div>`:'';
    const stepRefs = [];
    runs.forEach((run, ri) => {
      const cls = run.conclusion === 'success' ? 'badge-ok' : (run.conclusion === 'failure' ? 'badge-fail' : 'badge-pending');
      html += `<div class="job-title" style="${ri === 0 ? '' : 'margin-top:20px;'}">
        <span style="flex:1;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">${escapeHtml(run.name)} (#${run.run_number})</span>
        <span class="badge ${cls}">${escapeHtml(run.conclusion || run.status)}</span>
        <button class="icon-btn btn-icon-only" data-share-run-url="${escapeHtml(run.html_url || '')}" data-share-run-title="${escapeHtml(run.name)} #${run.run_number}" title="Partager le lien GitHub">${ico('share', 14)}</button>
        ${run.status === 'completed' ? `<button class="icon-btn btn-icon-only" data-rerun-run="${run.id}" data-rerun-label="${escapeHtml(run.name)} #${run.run_number}" title="Relancer ce run">${ico('refresh', 14)}</button>` : ''}
      </div>`;
      (jobsPerRun[ri].jobs || []).forEach(job => {
        (job.steps || []).forEach(step => {
          const stepIco = step.conclusion === 'success' ? 'check' : (step.conclusion === 'failure' ? 'xCircle' : 'clock');
          const stepClass = step.conclusion === 'success' ? 'ok' : (step.conclusion === 'failure' ? 'fail' : 'pending');
          const isFail = step.conclusion === 'failure';
          const idx = stepRefs.length;
          stepRefs.push({ job, step, runId: run.id });
          const stepStatus=step.status==='in_progress'?'En cours':(step.status==='queued'?'En attente':(step.status==='completed'?(step.conclusion||'Terminé'):'En attente'));
          html += `<div class="step-item ${isFail ? 'fail' : ''}" data-step-idx="${idx}">
            <div class="step-left"><span class="step-ico ${stepClass}">${ico(stepIco, 16)}</span><span class="step-name">${escapeHtml(job.name)} → ${escapeHtml(step.name)}</span></div>
            <span class="step-hint step-log-action">${step.status!=='completed'?escapeHtml(stepStatus):(isFail ? `Voir l'erreur ${ico('chevronRight',13)}` : `Voir le log ${ico('chevronRight',13)}`)}</span>
          </div>`;
        });
      });
    });
    setBody(html);
    document.querySelectorAll('.step-item[data-step-idx]').forEach(el => {
      el.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const { job, step, runId } = stepRefs[Number(el.dataset.stepIdx)];
        viewStepLog(fullName, job, step, String(runId));
      });
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-share-run-url]').forEach(el => {
      el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const url = el.dataset.shareRunUrl;
        if (!url){ toast('Lien GitHub indisponible pour ce run', 'error'); return; }
        shareLink(url, el.dataset.shareRunTitle || 'Run GitHub Actions');
      });
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-rerun-run]').forEach(el => {
      el.addEventListener('click', async (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        await rerunWorkflowRun(fullName, el.dataset.rerunRun, el, el.dataset.rerunLabel);
      });
    });
    if(liveRefresh)return activeRuns.length>0;
    const top=nav.stack[nav.stack.length-1];
    if(activeRuns.length&&top&&top.renderFn===renderRunDetail&&top.args[0]===fullName&&top.args[1]===commitSha){
      startLiveBuildMonitor(`run-detail:${fullName}:${commitSha}`,()=>renderRunDetail(fullName,commitSha,true));
    }
    return activeRuns.length>0;
  } catch(err){
    setBody(`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert', 30)}</div>${escapeHtml(friendly(err))}</div>`);
    if(!liveRefresh)showError('Erreur de chargement des jobs', friendly(err));
    return true;
  }
}

function extractStepLog(fullLog, stepName){
  const lines = fullLog.split('\n');
  const nameLower = stepName.trim().toLowerCase();
  let capturing = false, collected = [], matchedAny = false;
  for (const line of lines){
    const g = line.match(/##\[group\](.*)$/);
    if (g){
      const label = g[1].trim().toLowerCase();
      capturing = label.includes(nameLower) || nameLower.includes(label);
      if (capturing){ matchedAny = true; collected.push(line); }
      continue;
    }
    if (/##\[endgroup\]/.test(line)){ if (capturing) collected.push(line); capturing = false; continue; }
    if (capturing) collected.push(line);
  }
  return matchedAny ? collected.join('\n') : null;
}
function cleanLogText(t){ return t.replace(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T[\d:.]+Z\s?/gm, ''); }

async function viewStepLog(fullName, job, step, runId){
  const title = `${job.name} → ${step.name}`;
  const jobUrl = job.html_url || `https://github.com/${fullName}/actions/runs/${runId}/job/${job.id}`;
  const stepAnchor = `${jobUrl}#step:${step.number}:1`;
  const openLink = `<a class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" href="${stepAnchor}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${ico('external', 14)} GitHub</a>`;
  const logFilename = `${sanitizeFilename(job.name)}_${sanitizeFilename(step.name)}.log`;

  // On charge et prépare TOUT le texte d'abord, puis on ouvre une seule modale déjà
  // complète : copier/exporter/partager ne dépendent alors plus d'aucun accès réseau.
  openModal({ title: escapeHtml(title), bodyHTML: `<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement du log…</div>` });

  try{
    let fullLog = jobLogCache.get(job.id);
    if (!fullLog){
      const res = await fetch(`https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/actions/jobs/${job.id}/logs`, { headers: { Authorization: `token ${authToken}` } });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Log indisponible (statut ${res.status}). Le run est peut-être trop ancien ou encore en cours.`);
      fullLog = await res.text();
      jobLogCache.set(job.id, fullLog);
    }
    const section = extractStepLog(fullLog, step.name) || fullLog;
    const cleaned = cleanLogText(section).trim();
    renderTextModal(escapeHtml(title), cleaned, { filename: logFilename, jobUrl: stepAnchor });
  } catch(err){
    const info = [
      `Étape : ${step.name}`, `Statut : ${step.status}`, `Conclusion : ${step.conclusion || 'en cours'}`,
      step.started_at ? `Démarré : ${new Date(step.started_at).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}` : '',
      step.completed_at ? `Terminé : ${new Date(step.completed_at).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}` : '',
      '', `Le log complet n'a pas pu être chargé automatiquement (${friendly(err)}).`,
      `Ouvre le lien GitHub ci-dessous pour le consulter et le copier directement.`
    ].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    renderTextModal(escapeHtml(title), info, { filename: logFilename, jobUrl: stepAnchor });
  }
}

async function copyEditorContents(area){
  const value = area.value;
  try{
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText){ await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value); }
    else throw new Error('Presse-papiers indisponible');
    toast('Contenu copié', 'success', 2200);
  } catch(e){
    area.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    area.setSelectionRange(0, value.length);
    const copied = document.execCommand('copy');
    toast(copied ? 'Contenu copié' : 'Texte sélectionné — utilise Copier dans le menu du téléphone', copied ? 'success' : 'info', 4000);
  }
}

function openZipUpload(initialRepo = '', initialBranch = ''){
  if (!authToken){ toast('Connecte-toi à GitHub avant d’envoyer une archive.', 'error'); return; }
  let zipFile = null;
  const options = allRepos.map(r => `<option value="${escapeHtml(r.full_name)}" ${r.full_name === initialRepo ? 'selected' : ''}>${escapeHtml(r.full_name)}${r.private ? ' · privé' : ''}</option>`).join('');
  openModal({
    title: 'Envoyer une archive ZIP',
    bodyHTML: `<div class="zip-form-row"><label for="zipRepoSelect">Destination</label><select id="zipRepoSelect"><option value="">＋ Créer un nouveau dépôt</option>${options}</select><div class="zip-form-note">Choisis un dépôt de destination ou crée-en un sous ton compte GitHub.</div></div>
      <div id="zipNewRepoFields"><div class="zip-form-row"><label for="zipRepoName">Nom du nouveau dépôt</label><input id="zipRepoName" type="text" placeholder="mon-nouveau-depot" autocomplete="off"><div class="zip-form-note">Le dépôt sera créé dans ton compte GitHub.</div></div><div class="zip-form-row"><label for="zipVisibility">Visibilité</label><select id="zipVisibility"><option value="private">Privé</option><option value="public">Public</option></select></div></div>
      <div id="zipBranchRow" class="zip-form-row"><label for="zipBranch">Branche de destination</label><input id="zipBranch" type="text" placeholder="main" value="${escapeHtml(initialBranch || (allRepos.find(r => r.full_name === initialRepo)?.default_branch) || 'main')}"></div>
      <div class="zip-form-row"><label>Fichier ZIP</label><div class="zip-drop" id="zipDropZone" role="button" tabindex="0"><div class="zip-drop-ico">${ico('upload', 22)}</div><strong>Choisir un fichier ZIP</strong><span>Appuie pour parcourir ou dépose ton archive ici</span></div><input id="zipFileInput" type="file" accept=".zip,application/zip" hidden><div id="zipFileName" class="zip-file-name"></div></div>
      <label class="zip-overwrite-option"><input type="checkbox" id="zipAllowOverwrite"><span>J’autorise le remplacement automatique des fichiers existants aux mêmes chemins. Si cette option reste décochée, une confirmation sera demandée avant de remplacer les fichiers en conflit.</span></label>
      <div id="zipProgress" class="zip-progress"></div>`,
    footerHTML: `<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="zipCancelBtn">Annuler</button><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="zipPushBtn" disabled>${ico('upload', 14)} Envoyer le ZIP</button>`
  });
  const select = document.getElementById('zipRepoSelect');
  const newFields = document.getElementById('zipNewRepoFields');
  const branchInput = document.getElementById('zipBranch');
  const branchRow = document.getElementById('zipBranchRow');
  const zipNameLabel = document.getElementById('zipFileName');
  const fileInput = document.getElementById('zipFileInput');
  const dropZone = document.getElementById('zipDropZone');
  const pushBtn = document.getElementById('zipPushBtn');
  const updateReady = () => {
    const creating = !select.value;
    const name = document.getElementById('zipRepoName').value.trim();
    const validName = /^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$/.test(name) && name !== '.' && name !== '..';
    pushBtn.disabled = !zipFile || (creating ? !validName : !branchInput.value.trim());
  };
  select.onchange = () => {
    const creating = !select.value;
    newFields.hidden = !creating;
    branchRow.hidden = creating;
    if (!creating){
      const repo = allRepos.find(r => r.full_name === select.value);
      branchInput.value = select.value === initialRepo && initialBranch ? initialBranch : (repo && repo.default_branch) || 'main';
    }
    updateReady();
  };
  select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
  document.getElementById('zipRepoName').addEventListener('input', updateReady);
  branchInput.addEventListener('input', updateReady);
  function acceptZip(file){
    if (!file) return;
    if (!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.zip')){ zipFile = null; zipNameLabel.classList.remove('ready'); zipNameLabel.textContent = ''; updateReady(); toast('Sélectionne un fichier .zip.', 'error'); return; }
    zipFile = file;
    zipNameLabel.classList.add('ready');
    zipNameLabel.innerHTML = `${ico('file', 14)}<span>${escapeHtml(file.name)} · ${(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} Ko</span>`;
    updateReady();
  }
  dropZone.onclick = () => fileInput.click();
  dropZone.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' '){ e.preventDefault(); fileInput.click(); } };
  dropZone.ondragover = e => { e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.add('drag'); };
  dropZone.ondragleave = () => dropZone.classList.remove('drag');
  dropZone.ondrop = e => { e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.remove('drag'); acceptZip(e.dataTransfer.files[0]); };
  fileInput.onchange = () => acceptZip(fileInput.files[0]);
  document.getElementById('zipCancelBtn').onclick = closeModal;
  document.getElementById('zipPushBtn').onclick = async () => {
    const file = zipFile;
    if (!file){ toast('Sélectionne une archive ZIP.', 'error'); return; }
    let fullName = select.value;
    const newName = document.getElementById('zipRepoName')?.value.trim() || '';
    if (!fullName && !newName){ toast('Entre le nom du nouveau dépôt.', 'error'); return; }
    if (!fullName && (!/^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$/.test(newName) || newName === '.' || newName === '..')){ toast('Le nom doit contenir uniquement des lettres, chiffres, points, tirets et underscores.', 'error'); return; }
    if (!window.JSZip){ showError('Lecture ZIP impossible', 'Le composant ZIP local est absent. Vérifie que jszip.min.js est placé à côté de index.html.'); return; }
    const btn = document.getElementById('zipPushBtn');
    const progress = document.getElementById('zipProgress');
    let finalBranch = 'main';
    setBtnLoading(btn, true);
    try{
      const zip = await JSZip.loadAsync(file, { checkCRC32: true });
      const zipFiles = Object.values(zip.files).filter(f => !f.dir);
      const entries = zipFiles.map(f => ({ zipEntry: f, path: f.name.replace(/\\/g, '/') }))
        .filter(f => f.path && !f.path.startsWith('/') && !f.path.split('/').some(part => part === '..' || part === '.'));
      if (!entries.length) throw new Error('Aucun fichier utilisable dans cette archive.');
      if (entries.length > 500) throw new Error('Cette archive contient plus de 500 fichiers. Réduis-la et réessaie.');
      if (fullName){
        const r = allRepos.find(x => x.full_name === fullName);
        finalBranch = branchInput.value.trim() || (r && r.default_branch) || 'main';
        await pushZipToRepository({ fullName, branch: finalBranch, entries, progress, allowOverwrite: document.getElementById('zipAllowOverwrite').checked });
      } else {
        progress.textContent = 'Création du dépôt…';
        const created = await gh('/user/repos', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ name: newName, private: document.getElementById('zipVisibility').value === 'private', auto_init: true }) });
        fullName = created.full_name;
        allRepos.unshift(created);
        const targetBranch = created.default_branch || 'main';
        finalBranch = targetBranch;
        await pushZipToRepository({ fullName, branch: targetBranch, entries, progress, allowOverwrite: document.getElementById('zipAllowOverwrite').checked });
      }
      const destination = fullName;
      const branch = finalBranch;
      closeModal();
      toast(`Archive envoyée dans ${destination}`, 'success', 4500);
      navReset(renderFiles, 'Fichiers', [destination, '', branch], `branche "${branch}"`);
    } catch(err){
      progress.textContent = '';
      showError('Envoi du ZIP impossible', friendly(err));
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, `${ico('upload', 14)} Envoyer le ZIP`);
    }
  };
}

async function pushZipToRepository({ fullName, branch, entries, progress, allowOverwrite }){
  progress.textContent = 'Vérification de la branche et des chemins…';
  let ref, refError;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 5; attempt++){
    try{ ref = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/ref/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`); break; }
    catch(err){ refError = err; if (attempt < 4) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 800 * (attempt + 1))); }
  }
  if (!ref) throw refError || new Error(`Branche « ${branch} » introuvable.`);
  const parentSha = ref.object.sha;
  const baseCommit = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/commits/${parentSha}`);
  const treeData = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/trees/${parentSha}?recursive=1`);
  if (treeData.truncated) throw new Error('GitHub n’a pas renvoyé l’arborescence complète du dépôt. Impossible de garantir la détection de tous les conflits; aucun fichier n’a été envoyé.');
  const existing = new Map((treeData.tree || []).filter(t => t.type === 'blob').map(t => [t.path, t.sha]));
  const collisions = entries.filter(e => existing.has(e.path));
  const denied = [];
  if (collisions.length && !allowOverwrite){
    progress.textContent = `${collisions.length} chemin(s) déjà présent(s) : confirmation requise…`;
    const names = collisions.slice(0, 8).map(e => `• ${e.path}`).join('\n');
    closeModal();
    const ok = await confirmModal(`${collisions.length} fichier(s) de l’archive existent déjà dans ${fullName} :\n\n${names}${collisions.length > 8 ? `\n… et ${collisions.length - 8} autre(s)` : ''}\n\nAutoriser leur remplacement ?`, { confirmLabel: 'Remplacer les fichiers', danger: true });
    if (!ok) denied.push(...collisions.map(e => e.path));
  }
  const accepted = entries.filter(e => !denied.includes(e.path));
  if (!accepted.length) throw new Error('Aucun fichier n’a été autorisé à l’envoi.');
  const blobs = [];
  let done = 0;
  let completed = 0;
  const workers = Array.from({ length: Math.min(4, accepted.length) }, async () => {
    while (true){
      const index = done++;
      if (index >= accepted.length) return;
      const entry = accepted[index];
      const content = await entry.zipEntry.async('uint8array');
      let binary = '';
      for (let offset = 0; offset < content.length; offset += 0x8000) binary += String.fromCharCode(...content.subarray(offset, offset + 0x8000));
      const encoded = btoa(binary);
      const blob = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/blobs`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ content: encoded, encoding: 'base64' }) });
      blobs[index] = { path: entry.path, mode: '100644', type: 'blob', sha: blob.sha };
      completed++;
      progress.textContent = `Préparation des fichiers : ${completed}/${accepted.length}…`;
    }
  });
  await Promise.all(workers);
  progress.textContent = 'Création du commit…';
  const newTree = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/trees`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ base_tree: baseCommit.tree.sha, tree: blobs }) });
  const commit = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/commits`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ message: `Import ZIP (${accepted.length} fichier(s)) via GitHub Studio`, tree: newTree.sha, parents: [parentSha] }) });
  await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/refs/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`, { method: 'PATCH', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sha: commit.sha }) });
  if (denied.length) toast(`${denied.length} conflit(s) ignoré(s) après refus`, 'info', 4000);
}

async function exportCompleteRepoZip(fullName, branch, share){
  if (!window.JSZip){ showError('Export ZIP indisponible', 'La bibliothèque ZIP locale n’a pas pu être chargée.'); return; }
  const filename=`${fullName.split('/').pop()}-${branch}-complete.zip`;
  const transfer=createTransferProgress(share?'Préparation du ZIP à partager':'Export du dépôt',filename,0,'Lecture de l’arborescence…');
  const update = (percent,text,loaded=null,total=0) => transfer.update(percent,text,loaded,total);
  try{
    const repo = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}`);
    const ref = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/ref/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`);
    const headSha = ref.object.sha;
    const tree = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/trees/${headSha}?recursive=1`);
    if (tree.truncated) throw new Error('GitHub a tronqué l’arborescence : impossible de garantir un ZIP complet.');
    const entries = (tree.tree || []).filter(item => item.type === 'blob' && item.path && !item.path.startsWith('/') && !item.path.split('/').includes('..'));
    update(10,`${entries.length} fichier(s) repéré(s) · lecture des commits…`);
    const commits = [];
    for(let page=1;page<=100;page++){
      update(null,`Récupération des commits · page ${page}…`);
      const data=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/commits?sha=${encodeURIComponent(branch)}&per_page=100&page=${page}`);
      commits.push(...data);
      if(data.length<100) break;
    }
    const totalBytes=entries.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+(Number(item.size)||0),0);
    let downloadedBytes=0,completedFiles=0;
    update(20,`${commits.length} commit(s) · lecture de ${entries.length} fichier(s)…`,0,totalBytes);
    const zip = new JSZip();
    zip.file('repository.json', JSON.stringify(repo,null,2));
    zip.file('commits.json', JSON.stringify(commits,null,2));
    let next=0, completed=0;
    async function worker(){
      while(true){
        const index=next++; if(index>=entries.length) return;
        const entry=entries[index];
        let fileBytes=0;
        const fileBlob=await fetchGithubBlobWithProgress(fullName,entry.sha,(delta,current,total)=>{
          downloadedBytes+=delta;fileBytes=current;
          const ratio=totalBytes?downloadedBytes/totalBytes:(completedFiles+(entry.size?current/entry.size:0))/Math.max(1,entries.length);
          update(20+Math.min(1,ratio)*48,`Téléchargement des fichiers · ${completedFiles}/${entries.length} · ${entry.path}`,downloadedBytes,totalBytes||undefined);
        });
        zip.file(entry.path,fileBlob);
        if(fileBytes===0&&entry.size){downloadedBytes+=entry.size;}
        completedFiles++;
        if(completedFiles%2===0||completedFiles===entries.length||entries.length<2)update(20+(totalBytes?downloadedBytes/totalBytes:completedFiles/Math.max(1,entries.length))*48,`Fichiers reçus · ${completedFiles}/${entries.length}`,downloadedBytes,totalBytes||downloadedBytes);
      }
    }
    await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,entries.length)},worker));
    update(69,'Compression du ZIP…');
    const archive=await zip.generateAsync({type:'blob',compression:'DEFLATE',compressionOptions:{level:4}},meta=>update(69+meta.percent*.2,`Compression du ZIP… ${Math.round(meta.percent)}%`));
    transfer.setTotal(archive.size);
    transfer.update(90,'ZIP prêt · transfert du fichier…',0,archive.size);
    if(share)await shareBlobFile(`${repo.name}-${branch}-complete.zip`,archive,'application/zip',transfer,90,100);
    else await saveBlob(`${repo.name}-${branch}-complete.zip`,archive,'application/zip',transfer,90,100);
  } catch(err){ transfer.fail(); showError('Export du dépôt impossible',friendly(err)); }
}

const MEDIA_IMAGE_EXT=new Set(['png','jpg','jpeg','gif','webp','bmp','svg','avif','heic','heif']);
const MEDIA_VIDEO_EXT=new Set(['mp4','webm','m4v','mov','ogv','ogg','3gp','mkv']);
const MEDIA_AUDIO_EXT=new Set(['mp3','m4a','aac','wav','ogg','flac','opus']);
const APK_FILE_EXT=new Set(['apk']);
function fileExtension(path){return String(path||'').split('.').pop().toLowerCase();}
function mimeForPath(path){
  const ext=fileExtension(path);
  const types={png:'image/png',jpg:'image/jpeg',jpeg:'image/jpeg',gif:'image/gif',webp:'image/webp',bmp:'image/bmp',svg:'image/svg+xml',avif:'image/avif',heic:'image/heic',heif:'image/heif',mp4:'video/mp4',webm:'video/webm',m4v:'video/x-m4v',mov:'video/quicktime',ogv:'video/ogg',ogg:MEDIA_VIDEO_EXT.has(ext)?'video/ogg':'audio/ogg', '3gp':'video/3gpp',mkv:'video/x-matroska',mp3:'audio/mpeg',m4a:'audio/mp4',aac:'audio/aac',wav:'audio/wav',flac:'audio/flac',opus:'audio/opus',apk:'application/vnd.android.package-archive',aab:'application/octet-stream',zip:'application/zip',pdf:'application/pdf',jar:'application/java-archive',aar:'application/octet-stream',json:'application/json',txt:'text/plain',md:'text/markdown',html:'text/html',htm:'text/html',xml:'application/xml'};
  return types[ext]||'application/octet-stream';
}
function mediaKind(path){const ext=fileExtension(path);return MEDIA_IMAGE_EXT.has(ext)?'image':MEDIA_VIDEO_EXT.has(ext)?'video':MEDIA_AUDIO_EXT.has(ext)?'audio':null;}
function isBinaryFilePath(path){return APK_FILE_EXT.has(fileExtension(path));}
function isTextFilePath(path){return fileExtension(path)!=='zip'&&!mediaKind(path)&&!isBinaryFilePath(path);}
function openRepositoryFile(fullName,path,branch,size=0){
  const name=String(path).split('/').pop()||'fichier';
  if(fileExtension(path)==='zip')openZipExplorer(fullName,path,branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName],Number(size)||0); else if(mediaKind(path)||isBinaryFilePath(path))navTo(renderBinaryFile,name,[fullName,path,branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName],Number(size)||0],`branche "${branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main'}"`);
  else navTo(renderEditor,name,[fullName,path,false,branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName]],`branche "${branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main'}"`);
}
function githubBlobPageUrl(fullName,path,branch){return `https://github.com/${fullName}/blob/${encodeURIComponent(branch||'main')}/${String(path).split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/')}`;}
function githubContentsApiUrl(fullName,path,branch){return `https://api.github.com/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(branch||'main')}`;}
async function deleteRepositoryFile(fullName,path,branch,afterDelete=refreshCurrentScreen){
  const filename=String(path).split('/').pop()||path,selectedBranch=branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main';
  const confirmed=await confirmModal(`Supprimer définitivement « ${filename} » (${path}) du dépôt ${fullName}, branche ${selectedBranch} ?`,{title:'Supprimer ce fichier du dépôt ?',confirmLabel:'Supprimer le fichier',danger:true});
  if(!confirmed)return;
  try{
    const file=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(selectedBranch)}`);
    await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}`,{method:'DELETE',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({message:`Supprimer ${path} via GitHub Studio`,sha:file.sha,branch:selectedBranch})});
    toast(`Fichier supprimé : ${filename}`,'success');
    afterDelete?.();
  }catch(error){showError('Suppression du fichier impossible',friendly(error));}
}
function openRepositoryFileActions(fullName,path,branch,size=0){
  const filename=String(path).split('/').pop()||'fichier',mime=mimeForPath(path),url=githubContentsApiUrl(fullName,path,branch);
  const apk=/\.apk$/i.test(filename);
  openActionSheet(filename,[
    {label:'Télécharger le fichier',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,filename,mime,size,{title:'Téléchargement du fichier',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',installAfter:apk,showInstallPanel:apk})},
    ...(apk?[{label:'Installer / réinstaller directement',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,filename,mime,size,{title:'Installation de l’APK',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',installAfter:true,showInstallPanel:true})}]:[]),
    {label:'Ouvrir avec une application externe',icon:'external',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,filename,mime,size,{title:'Ouverture du fichier',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',openAfter:true})},
    {label:'Partager le fichier',icon:'share',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,filename,mime,size,{title:'Préparation du partage',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',share:true})},
    {label:'Supprimer le fichier du dépôt',icon:'trash',danger:true,fn:()=>deleteRepositoryFile(fullName,path,branch,refreshCurrentScreen)}
  ],{url:githubBlobPageUrl(fullName,path,branch),title:filename});
}
async function renderBinaryFile(fullName,path,branch,fileSize=0){
  branch=branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main';
  const filename=String(path).split('/').pop()||'fichier',mime=mimeForPath(path),kind=mediaKind(path),url=githubContentsApiUrl(fullName,path,branch);
  setScreenActions('',[
    {label:'Télécharger ce fichier',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,filename,mime,fileSize,{title:'Téléchargement du fichier',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',installAfter:filename.toLowerCase().endsWith('.apk'),showInstallPanel:filename.toLowerCase().endsWith('.apk')})},
    ...(filename.toLowerCase().endsWith('.apk')?[{label:'Installer / réinstaller directement',icon:'download',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,filename,mime,fileSize,{title:'Installation de l’APK',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',installAfter:true,showInstallPanel:true})}]:[]),
    {label:'Ouvrir avec une application externe',icon:'external',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,filename,mime,fileSize,{title:'Ouverture du fichier',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',openAfter:true})},
    {label:'Partager le fichier',icon:'share',fn:()=>transferGithubResource(url,filename,mime,fileSize,{title:'Préparation du partage',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',share:true})},
    {label:'Supprimer ce fichier du dépôt',icon:'trash',danger:true,fn:()=>deleteRepositoryFile(fullName,path,branch,navBack)}
  ]);
  if(!kind){
    setBody(`<div class="binary-file-card"><div class="binary-file-icon">${ico(mime==='application/vnd.android.package-archive'?'download':'file',34)}</div><h3>${escapeHtml(filename)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(mime)} · ${fileSize?formatTransferBytes(fileSize):'taille transmise par GitHub'}</p><p class="modal-hint">Ce fichier binaire ne sera pas chargé dans l’éditeur texte, afin d’éviter les blocages. Utilise le menu ⋮ pour télécharger, partager ou l’ouvrir avec une application compatible.</p></div>`);
    return;
  }
  const cap=kind==='video'?80*1024*1024:kind==='audio'?32*1024*1024:40*1024*1024;
  setBody(`<div class="media-preview-wrap"><div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Chargement de l’aperçu…</div></div>`);
  try{
    const response=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store',headers:{Authorization:`token ${authToken}`,Accept:'application/vnd.github.raw'}});
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`GitHub a renvoyé HTTP ${response.status}.`);
    const contentLength=Number(response.headers?.get('content-length'))||fileSize||0;
    if(contentLength>cap){try{await response.body?.cancel();}catch(_){}
      setBody(`<div class="binary-file-card"><div class="binary-file-icon">${ico(kind==='image'?'image':kind==='video'?'play':'file',34)}</div><h3>${escapeHtml(filename)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(mime)} · ${formatTransferBytes(contentLength)}</p><p class="modal-hint">Aperçu en ligne limité à ${formatTransferBytes(cap)} pour éviter un plantage mémoire. Le téléchargement reste segmenté et disponible depuis le menu ⋮, avec ouverture dans une application externe.</p></div>`);return;
    }
    const transfer=createTransferProgress('Chargement de l’aperçu',filename,contentLength,'Lecture depuis GitHub…');
    const receivedBlob=await responseToBlobWithProgress(response,transfer,{endPercent:100,expectedBytes:contentLength,stage:'Réception'});
    transfer.finish('Aperçu prêt');
    const blob=receivedBlob.type===mime?receivedBlob:new Blob([receivedBlob],{type:mime});
    activeMediaObjectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    let mediaHtml='';
    if(kind==='image')mediaHtml=`<img class="media-preview-image" src="${activeMediaObjectUrl}" alt="${escapeHtml(filename)}" loading="eager">`;
    else if(kind==='video')mediaHtml=`<video class="media-preview-video" src="${activeMediaObjectUrl}" controls playsinline preload="metadata">La vidéo ne peut pas être lue sur cet appareil.</video>`;
    else mediaHtml=`<audio class="media-preview-audio" src="${activeMediaObjectUrl}" controls preload="metadata">Le fichier audio ne peut pas être lu sur cet appareil.</audio>`;
    const body=document.getElementById('screenBody');if(body)body.innerHTML=`<div class="media-preview-wrap"><div class="media-preview-meta"><strong>${escapeHtml(filename)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(mime)} · ${formatTransferBytes(blob.size)}</span></div>${mediaHtml}<p class="modal-hint">Aperçu local · les liens GitHub, téléchargement, partage, accueil et actualisation sont dans le menu ⋮.</p></div>`;
  }catch(error){setBody(`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert',28)}</div>${escapeHtml(friendly(error))}<p class="modal-hint">Tu peux toujours télécharger ou ouvrir ce média depuis le menu ⋮.</p></div>`);}
}

function zipEntryIsApk(item){return !!item&&!item.isDir&&/\.apk$/i.test(item.name||'');}
function zipEntrySize(item){return Number(item?.entry?._data?.uncompressedSize||0);}
async function installZipApk(item,blob){
  const uri=await streamBlobToAndroid(item.name,blob,mimeForPath(item.name),false,null,false,true);
  if(!uri)throw new Error('L’installation directe nécessite l’application Android GitHub Studio.');
  promptInstallDownloadedApk({uri,filename:sanitizeFilename(item.name)},{autoInstall:false});
}

async function openZipExplorer(fullName,path,branch,size=0){
  branch=branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main';
  const filename=String(path).split('/').pop()||'archive.zip';
  const transfer=createTransferProgress('Ouverture du ZIP',filename,size,'Téléchargement temporaire…');
  try{
    const response=await fetch(githubContentsApiUrl(fullName,path,branch),{cache:'no-store',headers:{Authorization:`token ${authToken}`,Accept:'application/vnd.github.raw'}});
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`GitHub a renvoyé HTTP ${response.status}.`);
    const blob=await responseToBlobWithProgress(response,transfer,{endPercent:100,expectedBytes:size,stage:'Lecture du ZIP…'});
    const zip=await JSZip.loadAsync(blob);
    transfer.finish('ZIP prêt');
    const state={zip,fullName,path,branch,rootPath:'',currentPath:'',hidden:new Set(),selected:null,previewUrl:null};
    window.activeZipExplorer=state;
    openModal({title:`Explorer · ${filename}`,bodyHTML:'<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Préparation du contenu…</div>',footerHTML:'<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="zipExplorerClose">Fermer et libérer</button>'});
    document.getElementById('zipExplorerClose').onclick=()=>{closeZipPreview(state);window.activeZipExplorer=null;closeModal();};
    renderZipExplorer(state);
  }catch(error){transfer.fail();showError('Ouverture du ZIP impossible',friendly(error));}
}
function zipExplorerEntries(state){
  const prefix=state.currentPath;
  const map=new Map();
  Object.values(state.zip.files).forEach(entry=>{
    const name=String(entry.name).replace(/\\/g,'/');
    if(!name.startsWith(prefix)||name===prefix)return;
    const rest=name.slice(prefix.length),slash=rest.indexOf('/');
    const key=slash<0?rest:rest.slice(0,slash+1);
    if(!key)return;
    const isDir=key.endsWith('/');
    if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,{name:key.replace(/\/$/,''),path:prefix+key,isDir,entry:isDir?null:entry});
  });
  return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>Number(b.isDir)-Number(a.isDir)||a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}
function renderZipExplorer(state){
  const modal=document.querySelector('#modalOverlay .modal-body');if(!modal)return;
  const entries=zipExplorerEntries(state),visible=entries.filter(item=>!state.hidden.has(item.path));
  const crumb=state.currentPath?`<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="zipUpBtn">${ico('chevronLeft',14)} Dossier parent</button>`:'';
  const rows=visible.map((item,index)=>{const apk=zipEntryIsApk(item),size=zipEntrySize(item),selected=state.selected===item.path;return `<div class="zip-entry-row${selected?' preview-selected':''}" data-zip-entry="${index}"><span class="zip-entry-icon ${apk?'apk':''}">${ico(item.isDir?'folder':apk?'download':'file',17)}</span><span class="zip-entry-copy"><span class="zip-entry-name">${escapeHtml(item.name)}${item.isDir?'/':''}</span><span class="zip-entry-meta"><span>${item.isDir?'Dossier':'Fichier'}</span>${!item.isDir&&size?`<span>· ${formatTransferBytes(size)}</span>`:''}${apk?'<span class="zip-apk-badge">APK · INSTALLABLE</span>':''}</span></span><button class="icon-btn" data-zip-menu="${index}" title="Options" aria-label="Options de ${escapeHtml(item.name)}">${ico('more',15)}</button><span class="chevron">${ico('chevronRight',16)}</span></div>`;}).join('')||'<div class="empty-state">Aucune entrée visible dans ce dossier.</div>';
  modal.innerHTML=`<div class="zip-explorer"><div class="zip-explorer-head"><div><strong>${escapeHtml(state.currentPath||'/')}</strong><span>${entries.length} entrée(s) dans ce dossier${state.hidden.size?` · ${state.hidden.size} masquée(s)`:''}</span></div><div class="action-row">${crumb}<button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="zipShowAllBtn" ${state.hidden.size?'':'disabled'}>Réafficher tout</button></div></div><div class="zip-entry-list" id="zipExplorerList">${rows}</div><div id="zipExplorerPreview" class="editor-preview" style="display:none;"></div></div>`;
  document.getElementById('zipShowAllBtn').onclick=()=>{closeZipPreview(state);renderZipExplorer(state);};
  document.getElementById('zipUpBtn')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{state.currentPath=state.currentPath.split('/').slice(0,-2).join('/')+(state.currentPath.split('/').length>2?'/':'');renderZipExplorer(state);});
  const visibleEntries=visible;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-zip-entry]').forEach(row=>row.onclick=async()=>{const item=visibleEntries[Number(row.dataset.zipEntry)];if(!item)return;if(item.isDir){state.currentPath=item.path;renderZipExplorer(state);}else await previewZipEntry(state,item);});
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-zip-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{
    event.stopPropagation();const item=visibleEntries[Number(button.dataset.zipMenu)];if(!item)return;
    const row=button.closest('[data-zip-entry]'),old=row.querySelector('.zip-inline-menu');if(old){old.remove();return;}
    const menu=document.createElement('div');menu.className='zip-inline-menu';
    const previewAction=state.selected===item.path?'<button type="button" data-zip-close-preview="1">Fermer l’aperçu</button>':'';
    menu.innerHTML=`${previewAction}${item.isDir?'':`<button type="button" data-zip-open="1">Ouvrir avec une application externe</button><button type="button" data-zip-download="1">${zipEntryIsApk(item)?'Télécharger / installer':'Télécharger'}</button>${zipEntryIsApk(item)?'<button type="button" data-zip-install="1">Installer / réinstaller directement</button>':''}<button type="button" data-zip-share="1">Partager</button>`}<button type="button" data-zip-hide="1">Masquer les autres entrées</button><button type="button" data-zip-unhide="1">Réafficher toutes les entrées</button>`;
    row.appendChild(menu);
    menu.addEventListener('click',event=>event.stopPropagation());
    menu.querySelector('[data-zip-open]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{const blob=await item.entry.async('blob');const uri=await streamBlobToAndroid(item.name,blob,mimeForPath(item.name),false,null,true);if(!uri)throw new Error('Ouverture temporaire impossible sur cet appareil.');}catch(error){showError('Ouverture externe impossible',friendly(error));}});
    menu.querySelector('[data-zip-download]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{const blob=await item.entry.async('blob');if(zipEntryIsApk(item))await installZipApk(item,blob);else await saveBlob(item.name,blob,mimeForPath(item.name));}catch(error){showError('Téléchargement impossible',friendly(error));}});
    menu.querySelector('[data-zip-install]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{await installZipApk(item,await item.entry.async('blob'));}catch(error){showError('Installation impossible',friendly(error));}});
    menu.querySelector('[data-zip-share]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{await shareBlobFile(item.name,await item.entry.async('blob'),mimeForPath(item.name));}catch(error){showError('Partage impossible',friendly(error));}});
    menu.querySelector('[data-zip-close-preview]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{closeZipPreview(state);renderZipExplorer(state);});
    menu.querySelector('[data-zip-hide]').onclick=()=>{entries.forEach(other=>{if(other.path!==item.path)state.hidden.add(other.path);});renderZipExplorer(state);};
    menu.querySelector('[data-zip-unhide]').onclick=()=>{state.hidden.clear();renderZipExplorer(state);};
  });
}
function closeZipPreview(state){
  if(state?.previewUrl){URL.revokeObjectURL(state.previewUrl);state.previewUrl=null;}
  if(state){state.selected=null;state.hidden.clear();}
  const preview=document.getElementById('zipExplorerPreview');
  if(preview){preview.innerHTML='';preview.style.display='none';}
}
async function previewZipEntry(state,item){
  if(state.selected!==item.path){
    state.selected=item.path;
    state.hidden.clear();
    zipExplorerEntries(state).forEach(other=>{if(other.path!==item.path)state.hidden.add(other.path);});
    renderZipExplorer(state);
  }
  const preview=document.getElementById('zipExplorerPreview');if(!preview)return;
  try{
    if(state.previewUrl)URL.revokeObjectURL(state.previewUrl);
    const blob=await item.entry.async('blob');
    const mime=mimeForPath(item.name),url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([blob],{type:mime}));
    state.previewUrl=url;
    if(mediaKind(item.name)==='image')preview.innerHTML=`<div class="media-preview-meta"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong></div><img class="media-preview-image" src="${url}" alt="${escapeHtml(item.name)}">`;
    else if(mediaKind(item.name)==='video')preview.innerHTML=`<div class="media-preview-meta"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong></div><video class="media-preview-video" src="${url}" controls playsinline preload="metadata"></video>`;
    else if(mediaKind(item.name)==='audio')preview.innerHTML=`<div class="media-preview-meta"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong></div><audio class="media-preview-audio" src="${url}" controls preload="metadata"></audio>`;
    else if(isBinaryFilePath(item.name))preview.innerHTML=`<div class="zip-extracted-preview"><div class="media-preview-meta"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong><span>APK extrait · ${formatTransferBytes(blob.size)} · prêt à installer</span></div><div class="action-row"><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="zipInstallBtn">${ico('play',14)} Installer / réinstaller</button><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="zipDownloadBtn">${ico('download',14)} Télécharger</button><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" id="zipOpenExternalBtn">${ico('external',14)} Ouvrir</button></div></div>`;
    else{const text=await item.entry.async('text');preview.innerHTML=`<div class="media-preview-meta"><strong>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</strong></div><pre class="code-preview">${escapeHtml(text.slice(0,500000))}</pre>`;}
    preview.style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('zipOpenExternalBtn')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{const uri=await streamBlobToAndroid(item.name,blob,mime,false,null,true);if(!uri)throw new Error('Ouverture temporaire impossible.');}catch(error){showError('Ouverture impossible',friendly(error));}});
