    document.getElementById('zipInstallBtn')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{await installZipApk(item,blob);}catch(error){showError('Installation impossible',friendly(error));}});
    document.getElementById('zipDownloadBtn')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{try{await saveBlob(item.name,blob,mime);}catch(error){showError('Téléchargement impossible',friendly(error));}});
  }catch(error){preview.innerHTML=`<div class="modal-hint">Aperçu impossible : ${escapeHtml(friendly(error))}</div>`;preview.style.display='block';}
}

async function renderMediaPreview(fullName,path,branch,size=0){return renderBinaryFile(fullName,path,branch,size);}

/* ============================================================
   ÉCRAN 3c : NAVIGATEUR DE FICHIERS
============================================================ */

async function deleteRepositoryDirectory(fullName,path,branch,afterDelete=refreshCurrentScreen){
  const selectedBranch=branch||repoBranchSelection[fullName]||'main';
  const confirmed=await confirmModal(`Supprimer définitivement le dossier « ${path} » et tout son contenu du dépôt ${fullName} ?`,{title:'Supprimer le dossier ?',confirmLabel:'Supprimer le dossier',danger:true});
  if(!confirmed)return;
  try{
    const ref=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/ref/heads/${encodeURIComponent(selectedBranch)}`),baseSha=ref.object.sha;
    const tree=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/trees/${baseSha}?recursive=1`),baseTreeSha=tree.sha||baseSha;
    const prefix=path.replace(/\/$/,'')+'/';
    const removals=(tree.tree||[]).filter(item=>item.type==='blob'&&(item.path===path||item.path.startsWith(prefix))).map(item=>({path:item.path,mode:item.mode||'100644',type:'blob',sha:null}));
    if(!removals.length){toast('Dossier vide ou introuvable.','info');return;}
    const newTree=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/trees`,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({base_tree:baseTreeSha,tree:removals})});
    const commit=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/commits`,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({message:`Supprimer le dossier ${path} via GitHub Studio`,tree:newTree.sha,parents:[baseSha]})});
    await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/refs/heads/${encodeURIComponent(selectedBranch)}`,{method:'PATCH',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({sha:commit.sha,force:false})});
    toast(`Dossier supprimé avec ${removals.length} fichier(s).`,'success');afterDelete?.();
  }catch(error){showError('Suppression du dossier impossible',friendly(error));}
}

const githubFileDateCache = new Map();
async function githubFileLastUpdatedAt(fullName,path,branch){
  const key=`${fullName}:${branch||''}:${path}`;
  if(githubFileDateCache.has(key))return await githubFileDateCache.get(key);
  const query=`?path=${encodeURIComponent(path)}${branch?`&sha=${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`:''}&per_page=1`;
  const pending=gh(`/repos/${fullName}/commits${query}`).then(commits=>commits?.[0]?.commit?.committer?.date||commits?.[0]?.commit?.author?.date||null).catch(error=>{githubFileDateCache.delete(key);throw error;});
  githubFileDateCache.set(key,pending);
  return pending;
}

async function renderFiles(fullName, path, branch){
  branch = branch || repoBranchSelection[fullName];
  const refParam = branch ? `?ref=${encodeURIComponent(branch)}` : '';
  setScreenActions(`<button class="icon-btn" id="repoFileSearchBtn" title="Rechercher dans le dépôt" aria-label="Rechercher dans le dépôt">${ico('search', 16)}</button><button class="icon-btn" id="newRepoFileBtn" title="Nouveau fichier">${ico('plus', 16)}</button><button class="icon-btn" id="refreshRepoFilesBtn" title="Actualiser les fichiers">${ico('refresh', 16)}</button>`);
  const directSearch=document.createElement('button');
  directSearch.type='button';
  directSearch.className='icon-btn files-search-direct';
  directSearch.id='repoFileSearchDirectBtn';
  directSearch.title='Rechercher dans le dépôt';
  directSearch.setAttribute('aria-label','Rechercher dans le dépôt');
  directSearch.innerHTML=ico('search',20);
  directSearch.onclick=()=>openRepositorySearch(fullName,branch);
  document.getElementById('screenActions')?.insertBefore(directSearch,document.getElementById('screenActions').firstElementChild);
  document.getElementById('repoFileSearchBtn').onclick = () => openRepositorySearch(fullName, branch);
  document.getElementById('newRepoFileBtn').onclick=()=>createNewFile(fullName,path,branch);
  document.getElementById('refreshRepoFilesBtn').onclick=()=>renderFiles(fullName,path,branch);
  try{
    const endpoint = (path ? `/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}` : `/repos/${fullName}/contents`) + refParam;
    const files = await gh(endpoint);
    if (!Array.isArray(files)) throw new Error("Ce chemin n'est pas un dossier.");
    const crumb = path ? `<div class="breadcrumb">${ico('folder', 14)}<b>${escapeHtml(path)}</b></div>` : '';
    if (!files.length){setBody(`${crumb}<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('inbox', 30)}</div>Dossier vide.</div>`);return;}
    const sorted = [...files].sort((a, b) => (a.type === b.type ? a.name.localeCompare(b.name) : (a.type === 'dir' ? -1 : 1)));
    const html = sorted.map((file,index) => {
      if(file.type==='dir')return `<div class="list-item" data-open-directory="${index}"><span class="li-ico">${ico('folder',17)}</span><div class="li-main"><div class="li-title">${escapeHtml(file.name)}</div></div><button class="icon-btn" data-dir-menu="${index}" title="Actions du dossier" aria-label="Actions de ${escapeHtml(file.name)}">${ico('more',15)}</button><span class="chevron">${ico('chevronRight',16)}</span></div>`;
      const kind=mediaKind(file.path),binary=isBinaryFilePath(file.path),icon=kind==='image'?'image':kind==='video'?'play':kind==='audio'?'file':binary?'download':'file';
      return `<div class="list-item" data-open-file="${index}"><span class="li-ico">${ico(icon,17)}</span><div class="li-main"><div class="li-title">${escapeHtml(file.name)}</div><div class="li-sub">${formatTransferBytes(file.size||0)}${binary?' · fichier binaire':''} · modifié <span data-file-date="${index}">date en chargement…</span></div></div><button class="icon-btn" data-file-menu="${index}" title="Actions du fichier" aria-label="Actions de ${escapeHtml(file.name)}">${ico('more',15)}</button><span class="chevron">${ico('chevronRight',16)}</span></div>`;
    }).join('');
    setBody(crumb+html);
     const fileEntries=sorted.map((file,index)=>({file,index})).filter(entry=>entry.file.type==='file');
     let dateCursor=0;
     Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,fileEntries.length)},async()=>{
       while(dateCursor<fileEntries.length){
         const {file,index}=fileEntries[dateCursor++];
         const target=document.querySelector(`[data-file-date="${index}"]`);
         if(!target)continue;
         try{
           const date=await githubFileLastUpdatedAt(fullName,file.path,branch);
           if(target.isConnected)target.textContent=date?`${relDate(date)} · ${formatRunDateTime(date)}`:'date indisponible';
         }catch(error){if(target.isConnected)target.textContent='date indisponible';}
       }
     }));
     document.querySelectorAll('[data-open-directory]').forEach(item=>item.onclick=()=>{const file=sorted[Number(item.dataset.openDirectory)];if(file)navTo(renderFiles,file.name,[fullName,file.path,branch],`branche "${branch}"`);});
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-dir-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{event.stopPropagation();const file=sorted[Number(button.dataset.dirMenu)];if(file)openActionSheet(file.name,[{label:'Supprimer le dossier et son contenu',icon:'trash',danger:true,fn:()=>deleteRepositoryDirectory(fullName,file.path,branch,()=>renderFiles(fullName,path,branch))}]);});
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-open-file]').forEach(item=>item.onclick=()=>{const file=sorted[Number(item.dataset.openFile)];if(file)openRepositoryFile(fullName,file.path,branch,file.size);});
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-file-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{event.stopPropagation();const file=sorted[Number(button.dataset.fileMenu)];if(file)openRepositoryFileActions(fullName,file.path,branch,file.size);});
  }catch(err){setBody(`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert',30)}</div>${escapeHtml(friendly(err))}</div>`);showError('Erreur de chargement des fichiers',friendly(err));}
}

async function openRepositorySearch(fullName, branch){
  openModal({ title: 'Rechercher dans le dépôt', bodyHTML: `<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Indexation de l’arborescence…</div>` });
  try{
    const ref=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/ref/heads/${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`);
    const tree=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/git/trees/${ref.object.sha}?recursive=1`);
    if(tree.truncated)throw new Error('L’arborescence dépasse la limite renvoyée par GitHub. Affine la recherche dans une sous-branche du dépôt.');
    const files=(tree.tree||[]).filter(item=>item.type==='blob');
    document.querySelector('#modalOverlay .modal-body').innerHTML=`<input id="repoSearchQuery" class="modal-input" type="search" placeholder="Nom, chemin ou mot dans le contenu…" autocomplete="off"><div class="modal-hint" id="repoSearchCount">${files.length} fichier(s) indexé(s) sur ${escapeHtml(branch)}. Saisis au moins 2 caractères pour rechercher aussi dans le contenu.</div><div id="repoSearchResults"></div>`;
    const input=document.getElementById('repoSearchQuery'),count=document.getElementById('repoSearchCount'),results=document.getElementById('repoSearchResults');
    let timer=null,searchRun=0;
    const renderMatches=(matches,query,contentMatches=new Set(),searching=false)=>{
      results.innerHTML=matches.slice(0,100).map(item=>{
        const index=files.indexOf(item),inContent=contentMatches.has(item.path);
        return `<div class="list-item" data-search-row="${index}" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="li-ico">${ico('file',15)}</span><span class="li-main"><span class="li-title">${escapeHtml(item.path)}</span><span class="li-sub">${inContent?`Contient « ${escapeHtml(query)} »`:query&&item.path.toLowerCase().includes(query)?'Correspondance dans le nom ou le chemin':''}${Number.isFinite(item.size)?` · ${formatTransferBytes(item.size)}`:''}</span></span><button class="icon-btn" type="button" data-search-menu="${index}" title="Autres actions" aria-label="Autres actions pour ${escapeHtml(item.path)}">${ico('more',15)}</button></div>`;
      }).join('')||(searching?`<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Recherche du contenu contenant « ${escapeHtml(query)} »…</div>`:'<div class="modal-hint">Aucun fichier correspondant.</div>');
      if(matches.length>100)results.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<div class="modal-hint">100 premiers fichiers affichés sur ${matches.length} résultats.</div>`);
      results.querySelectorAll('[data-search-row]').forEach(row=>{
        const open=()=>{const item=files[Number(row.dataset.searchRow)];if(item){closeModal();openRepositoryFile(fullName,item.path,branch,item.size||0);}};
        row.onclick=event=>{if(!event.target.closest('[data-search-menu]'))open();};
        row.onkeydown=event=>{if(event.target.closest('[data-search-menu]'))return;if(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' '){event.preventDefault();open();}};
      });
      results.querySelectorAll('[data-search-menu]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{event.stopPropagation();const item=files[Number(button.dataset.searchMenu)];if(item)openRepositoryFileActions(fullName,item.path,branch,item.size||0);});
    };
    const runSearch=async()=>{
      const query=input.value.trim().toLowerCase(),run=++searchRun;
      if(!query){count.textContent=`${files.length} fichier(s) indexé(s) sur ${branch}. Saisis au moins 2 caractères pour rechercher aussi dans le contenu.`;renderMatches(files.slice(0,60),'');return;}
      const pathMatches=files.filter(item=>item.path.toLowerCase().includes(query));
      if(query.length<2){count.textContent=`${pathMatches.length} fichier(s) correspondent au nom ou au chemin. Saisis au moins 2 caractères pour rechercher le contenu.`;renderMatches(pathMatches,query);return;}
      const searchable=files.filter(item=>Number(item.size||0)<=1024*1024&&isTextFilePath(item.path));
      count.textContent=`${pathMatches.length} correspondance(s) dans le nom/chemin · recherche dans ${searchable.length} fichier(s) texte…`;
      renderMatches(pathMatches,query,new Set(),true);
      const queue=searchable.slice(),contentMatches=[];let checked=0;
      async function worker(){
        while(queue.length&&run===searchRun){
          const item=queue.shift();
          try{
            const data=await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(item.path)}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(branch)}`);
            if(data?.content&&b64ToUtf8(data.content).toLowerCase().includes(query))contentMatches.push(item);
          }catch(_){ }
          checked++;
          if(run===searchRun&&checked%8===0)count.textContent=`Recherche dans le contenu… ${checked}/${searchable.length} fichier(s) · ${pathMatches.length} résultat(s) par chemin.`;
        }
      }
      await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,Math.max(1,searchable.length))},()=>worker()));
      if(run!==searchRun)return;
      const allMatches=new Map();pathMatches.forEach(item=>allMatches.set(item.path,item));contentMatches.forEach(item=>allMatches.set(item.path,item));
      const sorted=[...allMatches.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.path.localeCompare(b.path));
      const contentPaths=new Set(contentMatches.map(item=>item.path));
      count.textContent=`${contentMatches.length} fichier(s) contiennent « ${query} » · ${pathMatches.length} correspondent au nom ou au chemin · ${searchable.length} fichiers texte examinés.`;
      renderMatches(sorted,query,contentPaths);
    };
    input.addEventListener('input',()=>{clearTimeout(timer);searchRun++;const current=input.value.trim();if(!current){count.textContent=`${files.length} fichier(s) indexé(s) sur ${branch}.`;renderMatches(files.slice(0,60),'');return;}const query=current.toLowerCase();const pathMatches=files.filter(item=>item.path.toLowerCase().includes(query));count.textContent=`${pathMatches.length} correspondance(s) dans le nom ou le chemin · recherche du contenu dans un instant…`;renderMatches(pathMatches,query,new Set(),query.length>=2);timer=setTimeout(runSearch,700);});
    input.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
      if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();const raw=input.value.trim().replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g,'');const exact=files.find(item=>item.path.toLowerCase()===raw.toLowerCase());if(exact){closeModal();openRepositoryFile(fullName,exact.path,branch,exact.size||0);return;}clearTimeout(timer);runSearch();return;}
      if(event.key==='Escape'){input.value='';input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));}
    });
    renderMatches(files.slice(0,60),'');input.focus();
  }catch(err){
    const body=document.querySelector('#modalOverlay .modal-body');
    if(body)body.innerHTML=`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('alert',28)}</div>Recherche impossible<p class="modal-hint">${escapeHtml(friendly(err))}</p><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" onclick="closeModal()">Fermer</button></div>`;
    showError('Recherche impossible',friendly(err));
  }
}

async function createNewFile(fullName, path, branch){
  const name = await promptModal('Nom du nouveau fichier (ex: build.gradle) :', { placeholder: 'nom-du-fichier.ext' });
  if (!name) return;
  navTo(renderEditor, name, [fullName, joinPath(path, name), true, branch]);
}

/* ============================================================
   ÉCRAN 3d : ÉDITEUR
============================================================ */
function buildLineDiff(before, after){
  const oldLines = before === '' ? [] : before.split('\n'), newLines = after === '' ? [] : after.split('\n');
  if (oldLines.length * newLines.length > 160000){
    return { rows: [...oldLines.slice(0, 100).map(text => ({type:'remove', text})), ...newLines.slice(0, 100).map(text => ({type:'add', text}))], truncated: true };
  }
  const matrix = Array.from({length: oldLines.length + 1}, () => new Uint16Array(newLines.length + 1));
  for (let i = oldLines.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) for (let j = newLines.length - 1; j >= 0; j--)
    matrix[i][j] = oldLines[i] === newLines[j] ? matrix[i+1][j+1] + 1 : Math.max(matrix[i+1][j], matrix[i][j+1]);
  const rows = []; let i = 0, j = 0, added = 0, removed = 0;
  while (i < oldLines.length || j < newLines.length){
    if (i < oldLines.length && j < newLines.length && oldLines[i] === newLines[j]){ rows.push({type:'context',text:oldLines[i]}); i++; j++; }
    else if (j < newLines.length && (i === oldLines.length || matrix[i][j+1] > matrix[i+1][j])){ rows.push({type:'add',text:newLines[j++]}); added++; }
    else { rows.push({type:'remove',text:oldLines[i++]}); removed++; }
  }
  return { rows, added, removed, truncated:false };
}
function showEditorDiff(path, before, after){
  const diff = buildLineDiff(before, after);
  const rows = diff.rows.map(row => `<div class="diff-line ${row.type}">${row.type === 'add' ? '+' : row.type === 'remove' ? '−' : ' '}${escapeHtml(row.text) || '&nbsp;'}</div>`).join('');
  openModal({ title: `Comparaison · ${escapeHtml(path.split('/').pop())}`, bodyHTML: `<div class="diff-summary"><strong>+${diff.added || 0} lignes ajoutées</strong><span style="color:var(--danger);">−${diff.removed || 0} supprimées</span></div>${diff.truncated ? '<div class="modal-hint">Fichier volumineux : aperçu des 100 premières lignes de chaque version.</div>' : ''}<div class="diff-file-list">${rows}</div>` });
}
function markdownPreviewHtml(text){
  const escaped = escapeHtml(text);
  let fenced = false;
  const lines = escaped.split('\n');
  const output = []; let paragraph = [], list = [];
  const flushParagraph = () => { if(paragraph.length){ output.push(`<p>${paragraph.join('<br>')}</p>`); paragraph=[]; } };
  const flushList = () => { if(list.length){ output.push(`<ul>${list.map(item=>`<li>${item}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`); list=[]; } };
  for (const line of lines){
    if (line.trim().startsWith('```')){ flushParagraph(); flushList(); output.push(fenced ? '</code></pre>' : '<pre><code>'); fenced=!fenced; continue; }
    if (fenced){ output.push(line); continue; }
    const heading = line.match(/^(#{1,3})\s+(.*)$/);
    if (heading){ flushParagraph(); flushList(); const level=heading[1].length; output.push(`<h${level}>${heading[2]}</h${level}>`); continue; }
    if (/^\s*[-*]\s+/.test(line)){ flushParagraph(); list.push(line.replace(/^\s*[-*]\s+/,'')); continue; }
    flushList();
    if (!line.trim()){ flushParagraph(); continue; }
    paragraph.push(line.replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code>$1</code>').replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g,'<strong>$1</strong>'));
  }
  flushParagraph(); flushList();
  if(fenced) output.push('</code></pre>');
  return output.join('');
}
function refreshEditorPreview(path, content, frame, markdown){
  const ext = path.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
  if (['html','htm'].includes(ext)) frame.srcdoc = content;
  else if (ext === 'md') markdown.innerHTML = markdownPreviewHtml(content);
  else if (ext === 'json'){
    try{ markdown.innerHTML = `<pre>${escapeHtml(JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(content), null, 2))}</pre>`; }
    catch(e){ markdown.innerHTML = `<pre>${escapeHtml(content)}</pre>`; }
  } else markdown.innerHTML = `<pre>${escapeHtml(content)}</pre>`;
}
async function renderEditor(fullName, path, isNew, branch){
  branch = branch || repoBranchSelection[fullName];
  if(!isNew&&(mediaKind(path)||isBinaryFilePath(path))){return renderBinaryFile(fullName,path,branch,0);}
  const refParam = branch ? `?ref=${encodeURIComponent(branch)}` : '';
  setScreenActions(`
    <button class="icon-btn" id="compareFileBtn" title="Comparer avant / après" aria-label="Comparer les modifications" disabled>${ico('git',15)}</button>
    ${isNew?'':`<button class="icon-btn" data-editor-action="external" title="Ouvrir avec une application externe" aria-label="Ouvrir avec une application externe">${ico('external',15)}</button>`}
    <button class="icon-btn" data-editor-action="share" title="Partager le contenu du fichier">${ico('share',15)}</button>
    <button class="icon-btn" data-editor-action="copy" title="Copier le contenu">${ico('copy',15)}</button>`);
  setBody(`
    <div class="editor-context-panel" id="editorInfoPanel" hidden>
      <div class="editor-context-path">${ico('file', 14)}<span style="flex:1;min-width:0;">${escapeHtml(path)}</span><span class="dirty-badge" id="dirtyBadge" style="display:none;">Modifié</span></div>
      ${branch ? `<div class="editor-context-branch">${ico('branch', 13)}<b>${escapeHtml(branch)}</b></div>` : ''}
      <div class="editor-file-meta" id="editorFileMetadata">Taille : chargement… · Dernière modification : chargement…</div>
    </div>
    <div class="editor-quick-actions" aria-label="Actions rapides de l’éditeur">
      <button class="icon-btn" id="editorSearchToggleBtn" type="button" title="Rechercher dans le fichier" aria-label="Rechercher dans le fichier" aria-expanded="false">${ico('search',17)}</button>
      <button class="icon-btn" id="saveBtn" type="button" title="Enregistrer" aria-label="Enregistrer" disabled>${ico('save',17)}</button>
      <button class="icon-btn" id="previewFileBtn" type="button" title="Aperçu du fichier" aria-label="Afficher l’aperçu du fichier" hidden>${ico('eye',17)}</button>
      <div class="editor-more-wrap">
        <button class="icon-btn" id="editorMoreBtn" type="button" title="Informations et navigation" aria-label="Informations et navigation" aria-haspopup="menu" aria-expanded="false">${ico('chevronDown',17)}</button>
        <div class="editor-more-menu" id="editorMoreMenu" role="menu" aria-label="Informations et navigation">
          <button type="button" id="editorInfoBtn" role="menuitem">${ico('info',16)}<span>Informations du fichier</span></button>
          <button type="button" id="editorTopBtn" role="menuitem">${ico('chevronUp',16)}<span>Aller en haut du fichier</span></button>
          <button type="button" id="editorBottomBtnTop" role="menuitem">${ico('chevronDown',16)}<span>Aller en bas du fichier</span></button>
          ${isNew?'':`<button type="button" class="danger" id="deleteBtn" role="menuitem">${ico('trash',16)}<span>Supprimer</span></button>`}
          <button type="button" data-editor-action="download" role="menuitem">${ico('download',16)}<span>Télécharger le fichier</span></button>
          <button type="button" data-editor-action="import" role="menuitem">${ico('upload',16)}<span>Importer un fichier</span></button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <span class="editor-find-count quick-find-count" id="editorQuickFindCount" aria-live="polite"></span>
    </div>
    <div class="editor-find-row" id="editorFindRow" role="search" aria-label="Rechercher dans le fichier" hidden>
      <label class="editor-search-field" for="editorFindInput">${ico('search',16)}<input id="editorFindInput" type="search" placeholder="Chercher dans le fichier…" autocomplete="off" aria-label="Texte à rechercher dans le fichier"></label>
      <span class="editor-find-count" id="editorFindCount" aria-live="polite"></span>
    </div>
    <div class="editor-shell" id="editorShell"><div class="editor-gutter" id="editorLineNumbers" aria-hidden="true">1</div><div class="editor-code-pane"><pre class="editor-highlight" id="editorHighlight" aria-hidden="true"><code id="editorHighlightCode"></code></pre><textarea class="editor" id="editorArea" wrap="off" autocapitalize="off" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" disabled placeholder="Chargement…"></textarea></div></div>
    <iframe class="editor-preview-frame" id="editorPreviewFrame" title="Aperçu HTML" sandbox=""></iframe>
    <div class="preview-markdown" id="editorPreviewMarkdown" hidden></div>
    <input type="file" id="importFileInput" style="display:none;" accept=".txt,.js,.jsx,.ts,.tsx,.html,.htm,.css,.json,.md,.xml,.yml,.yaml,.py,.java,.kt,.gradle,.properties,.dart,.c,.cpp,.h,.sh,.gitignore,.env,text/*">
  `);
  const importInput = document.getElementById('importFileInput');
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-editor-action]').forEach(el => el.onclick = async () => {
    closeOverflowMenus();
    if (el.dataset.editorAction === 'import') importInput.click();
    if (el.dataset.editorAction === 'share') await shareText(path.split('/').pop() || 'fichier.txt', area.value);
    if (el.dataset.editorAction === 'external') await transferGithubResource(githubContentsApiUrl(fullName,path,branch),path.split('/').pop()||'fichier',mimeForPath(path),0,{title:'Ouverture du fichier',accept:'application/vnd.github.raw',openAfter:true});
    if (el.dataset.editorAction === 'download') downloadText(path.split('/').pop() || 'fichier.txt', area.value);
    if (el.dataset.editorAction === 'copy') await copyEditorContents(area);
  });
  importInput.onchange = async () => {
    const file = importInput.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    // Fichiers texte "normaux" jusqu'à 5 Mo — au-delà, très probablement pas
    // un fichier de code source, et un textarea n'est de toute façon pas fait
    // pour ça.
    if (file.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      toast("Fichier trop volumineux (max 5 Mo pour un import dans l'éditeur).", 'error');
      importInput.value = '';
      return;
    }
    try {
      const text = await file.text();
      const area = document.getElementById('editorArea');
      area.disabled = false;
      area.value = text;
      area.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
      toast(`"${file.name}" importé — pense à Enregistrer pour l'envoyer sur GitHub.`, 'success');
    } catch (err) {
      showError('Import impossible', friendly(err));
    } finally {
      importInput.value = '';
    }
  };
  const area = document.getElementById('editorArea');
  const saveBtn = document.getElementById('saveBtn');
  const editorInfoPanel = document.getElementById('editorInfoPanel');
  const editorMoreBtn = document.getElementById('editorMoreBtn');
  const editorMoreMenu = document.getElementById('editorMoreMenu');
  const editorInfoBtn = document.getElementById('editorInfoBtn');
  const editorTopBtn = document.getElementById('editorTopBtn');
  const editorBottomBtnTop = document.getElementById('editorBottomBtnTop');
  const compareBtn = document.getElementById('compareFileBtn');
  const previewBtn = document.getElementById('previewFileBtn');
  const previewFrame = document.getElementById('editorPreviewFrame');
  const previewMarkdown = document.getElementById('editorPreviewMarkdown');
  const editorShell = document.getElementById('editorShell');
  const lineNumbers = document.getElementById('editorLineNumbers');
  const highlightPane = document.getElementById('editorHighlight');
  const highlightCode = document.getElementById('editorHighlightCode');
  const findInput = document.getElementById('editorFindInput');
  const findRow = document.getElementById('editorFindRow');
  const searchToggleBtn = document.getElementById('editorSearchToggleBtn');
  const findCount = document.getElementById('editorFindCount');
  const quickFindCount = document.getElementById('editorQuickFindCount');
  const fileMetadata = document.getElementById('editorFileMetadata');
  let lastUpdatedAt = null;
  const languageByExtension={html:'markup',htm:'markup',xml:'markup',svg:'markup',xhtml:'markup',css:'css',scss:'css',less:'css',js:'javascript',mjs:'javascript',cjs:'javascript',jsx:'jsx',ts:'typescript',tsx:'tsx',json:'json',jsonc:'json',py:'python',java:'java',kt:'kotlin',kts:'kotlin',gradle:'groovy',groovy:'groovy',dart:'dart',c:'c',h:'c',cpp:'cpp',cc:'cpp',cxx:'cpp',hpp:'cpp',rs:'rust',sh:'bash',bash:'bash',zsh:'bash',yml:'yaml',yaml:'yaml',md:'markdown',markdown:'markdown',sql:'sql',properties:'properties',ini:'ini',cfg:'ini',toml:'ini',dockerfile:'docker',docker:'docker',gitignore:'bash',env:'bash'};
  const editorFileName=path.split('/').pop().toLowerCase();
  const editorLanguage=editorFileName==='gradlew'?'bash':(languageByExtension[editorFileName]||languageByExtension[editorFileName.split('.').pop()]||null);
  function markEditorSearchMatches(){
    const query=findInput.value;
    if(!query||query.length===0)return;
    const needle=query.toLocaleLowerCase();
    const walker=document.createTreeWalker(highlightCode,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
    const nodes=[];let node;
    while(node=walker.nextNode())nodes.push(node);
    nodes.forEach(textNode=>{
      const value=textNode.nodeValue||'',lower=value.toLocaleLowerCase();
      let from=0,index;
      if(!lower.includes(needle))return;
      const fragment=document.createDocumentFragment();
      while((index=lower.indexOf(needle,from))!==-1){
        if(index>from)fragment.appendChild(document.createTextNode(value.slice(from,index)));
        const mark=document.createElement('mark');mark.className='editor-search-hit';mark.textContent=value.slice(index,index+query.length);fragment.appendChild(mark);
        from=index+query.length;
      }
      if(from<value.length)fragment.appendChild(document.createTextNode(value.slice(from)));
      textNode.parentNode?.replaceChild(fragment,textNode);
    });
  }
  function updateEditorHighlight(){
    const source=area.value;
    if(source.length>450000){highlightCode.textContent=source;markEditorSearchMatches();return;}
    const grammar=editorLanguage&&window.Prism?.languages?.[editorLanguage];
    highlightCode.className=editorLanguage?`language-${editorLanguage}`:'';
    highlightCode.innerHTML=grammar?Prism.highlight(source,grammar,editorLanguage):escapeHtml(source);
    markEditorSearchMatches();
  }
  function syncEditorScroll(){
    lineNumbers.scrollTop=area.scrollTop;
    highlightPane.scrollTop=area.scrollTop;
    highlightPane.scrollLeft=area.scrollLeft;
  }
  function updateLineNumbers(){
    const count=Math.max(1,area.value.split('\n').length);
    lineNumbers.textContent=Array.from({length:count},(_,index)=>String(index+1)).join('\n');
    updateEditorHighlight();
    syncEditorScroll();
  }
  function updateFindCount(activeIndex=-1){
    const query=findInput.value;
    if(!query){findCount.textContent='';if(quickFindCount)quickFindCount.textContent='';return;}
    const source=area.value.toLocaleLowerCase(),needle=query.toLocaleLowerCase();
    let total=0,at=0;
    while((at=source.indexOf(needle,at))!==-1){total++;at+=needle.length;}
    const label=total?(activeIndex>=0?`${activeIndex+1}/${total}`:`${total} résultat${total>1?'s':''}`):'0 résultat';
    findCount.textContent=label; if(quickFindCount)quickFindCount.textContent=label;
  }
  function findInEditor(direction){
    const query=findInput.value;
    if(!query){findInput.focus();return;}
    const source=area.value.toLocaleLowerCase(),needle=query.toLocaleLowerCase();
    const found=direction>0?source.indexOf(needle,area.selectionEnd):source.lastIndexOf(needle,Math.max(0,area.selectionStart-1));
    const index=found>=0?found:(direction>0?source.indexOf(needle):source.lastIndexOf(needle));
    if(index<0){updateFindCount();return;}
    area.focus();area.setSelectionRange(index,index+query.length);
    const line=area.value.slice(0,index).split('\n').length-1;
    const lineHeight=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(area).lineHeight)||19;
    area.scrollTop=Math.max(0,line*lineHeight-area.clientHeight/2);
    lineNumbers.scrollTop=area.scrollTop;
    let ordinal=0,scan=0;
    while((scan=source.indexOf(needle,scan))!==-1){ordinal++;if(scan===index)break;scan+=needle.length;}
    updateFindCount(ordinal-1);
  }
  function updateEditorFileMetadata(){
    const byteCount=new TextEncoder().encode(area.value).length;
    const dateLabel=lastUpdatedAt?`${relDate(lastUpdatedAt)} · ${formatRunDateTime(lastUpdatedAt)}`:(isNew?'pas encore enregistré':'date indisponible');
    fileMetadata.textContent=`Taille : ${formatTransferBytes(byteCount)} · Dernière modification : ${dateLabel}`;
  }
  let searchOpen=false,infoOpen=false;
  searchToggleBtn.onclick=()=>{searchOpen=!searchOpen;findRow.hidden=!searchOpen;searchToggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(searchOpen));if(searchOpen){findInput.focus();}};
  editorMoreBtn.onclick=event=>{event.stopPropagation();const opening=!editorMoreMenu.classList.contains('open');closeOverflowMenus();editorMoreMenu.classList.toggle('open',opening);editorMoreBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(opening));};
  editorInfoBtn.onclick=()=>{infoOpen=!infoOpen;editorInfoPanel.hidden=!infoOpen;editorInfoBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(infoOpen));editorMoreMenu.classList.remove('open');editorMoreBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');};
  editorTopBtn.onclick=()=>{area.focus();area.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});syncEditorScroll();editorMoreMenu.classList.remove('open');editorMoreBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');};
  editorBottomBtnTop.onclick=()=>{area.focus();area.setSelectionRange(area.value.length,area.value.length);area.scrollTo({top:area.scrollHeight,behavior:'smooth'});syncEditorScroll();editorMoreMenu.classList.remove('open');editorMoreBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');};
  area.addEventListener('input',()=>{updateFindCount();updateDirtyState();});
  area.addEventListener('scroll',syncEditorScroll);
  findInput.addEventListener('input',()=>{updateFindCount();updateEditorHighlight();});
  findInput.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();findInEditor(event.shiftKey?-1:1);}});
  const previewExt = path.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
  const supportsPreview = ['html','htm','md','json','yaml','yml','txt'].includes(previewExt);
  previewBtn.hidden = !supportsPreview;
  // Aperçu fermé par défaut : code et aperçu occupent le même espace, jamais
  // les deux à la fois (pas de second écran empilé sous le code).
  let previewOpen = false;
  const dirtyBadge = document.getElementById('dirtyBadge');
  const baseName = path.split('/').pop() || 'fichier.txt';
  let sha = undefined;
  let originalContent = '';

  // Le bouton Enregistrer ne s'active que si le contenu a réellement changé,
  // pour éviter tout envoi accidentel sans modification.
  function updateDirtyState(){
    const dirty = area.value !== originalContent;
    updateLineNumbers();
    updateEditorFileMetadata();
    saveBtn.disabled = !dirty;
    compareBtn.disabled = !dirty;
    dirtyBadge.style.display = dirty ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
    if (previewOpen) refreshEditorPreview(path, area.value, previewFrame, previewMarkdown);
  }
  compareBtn.onclick = () => showEditorDiff(path, originalContent, area.value);
  previewBtn.onclick = () => {
    previewOpen = !previewOpen;
    // Bascule exclusive : le code se masque quand l'aperçu s'affiche, et
    // inversement, toujours dans le même écran.
    editorShell.hidden = previewOpen;
    if (previewOpen){findRow.hidden=true;editorInfoPanel.hidden=true;} else {findRow.hidden=!searchOpen;editorInfoPanel.hidden=!infoOpen;}
    previewFrame.classList.toggle('visible', previewOpen && ['html','htm'].includes(previewExt));
    previewMarkdown.hidden = !previewOpen || ['html','htm'].includes(previewExt);
    previewBtn.title = previewOpen ? 'Retour au code' : 'Aperçu du fichier';
    previewBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', previewBtn.title);
    previewBtn.innerHTML = ico(previewOpen ? 'code' : 'eye', 15);
    if (previewOpen) refreshEditorPreview(path, area.value, previewFrame, previewMarkdown);
  };

  if (isNew){
    area.disabled = false;
    area.value = '';
    area.placeholder = 'Écris le contenu du nouveau fichier…';
    originalContent = '';
    saveBtn.disabled = false; // fichier neuf : on peut enregistrer même vide
  } else {
    try{
      const data = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}${refParam}`);
      if (Array.isArray(data)) throw new Error("Ceci est un dossier, pas un fichier.");
      if (data.content && data.encoding === 'base64'){
        area.value = b64ToUtf8(data.content);
      } else if (data.download_url){
        const rawRes = await fetch(data.download_url);
        if (!rawRes.ok) throw new Error('Impossible de télécharger le contenu brut du fichier.');
        area.value = await rawRes.text();
      } else {
        throw new Error('Contenu indisponible : fichier trop volumineux ou binaire.');
      }
      sha = data.sha;
      area.disabled = false;
      originalContent = area.value;
      saveBtn.disabled = true; // rien de modifié juste après le chargement
      githubFileLastUpdatedAt(fullName,path,branch).then(date=>{lastUpdatedAt=date;updateEditorFileMetadata();}).catch(()=>updateEditorFileMetadata());
    } catch(err){
      area.placeholder = 'Erreur de chargement';
      area.disabled = true;
      showError('Erreur de lecture du fichier', friendly(err));
    }
  }

  updateLineNumbers();
  updateEditorFileMetadata();
  if (previewOpen) refreshEditorPreview(path, area.value, previewFrame, previewMarkdown);
  function buildTriggeredByPath(filePath){
    return /^(app\/|gradle\/|gradle\.properties$|gradlew(?:\.bat)?$|settings\.gradle$|build\.gradle$|\.github\/workflows\/)/i.test(String(filePath||''));
  }
  document.getElementById('saveBtn').onclick = async () => {
    const btn = document.getElementById('saveBtn');
    setBtnLoading(btn, true);
    try{
      if (sha === undefined){
        try{ const check = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}${refParam}`); sha = check.sha; } catch(e){ /* n'existe pas encore : création */ }
      }
      const body = { message: `Update ${path} via NPS Editor`, content: utf8ToB64(area.value) };
      if (sha) body.sha = sha;
      if (branch) body.branch = branch;
      const data = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}`, {
        method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body)
      });
      sha = data.content.sha;
      originalContent = area.value;
      lastUpdatedAt = data.commit?.committer?.date || data.commit?.author?.date || lastUpdatedAt;
      updateDirtyState();
      toast(buildTriggeredByPath(path)
        ? 'Fichier enregistré : le build Android sera déclenché automatiquement par GitHub Actions.'
        : 'Fichier enregistré avec succès !', 'success');
    } catch(err){
      showError("Erreur d'enregistrement", friendly(err));
    } finally {
      setBtnLoading(btn, false, `${ico('save', 15)} Enregistrer`);
      updateDirtyState();
    }
  };

  const deleteBtn = document.getElementById('deleteBtn');
  if (deleteBtn){
    deleteBtn.onclick = async () => {
      const ok = await confirmModal(`Supprimer "${path}" ? Cette action est irréversible.`, { confirmLabel: 'Supprimer' });
      if (!ok) return;
      setBtnLoading(deleteBtn, true);
      try{
        if (sha === undefined){ const check = await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}${refParam}`); sha = check.sha; }
        const delBody = { message: `Delete ${path} via NPS Editor`, sha };
        if (branch) delBody.branch = branch;
        await gh(`/repos/${fullName}/contents/${encodePath(path)}`, {
          method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(delBody)
        });
        toast('Fichier supprimé.', 'success');
        navBack();
      } catch(err){
        showError('Erreur de suppression', friendly(err));
      } finally {
        setBtnLoading(deleteBtn, false, `${ico('trash', 15)} Supprimer`);
      }
    };
  }
}

/* ============================================================
   DÉMARRAGE
============================================================ */
(async function initApp(){
  const savedToken = localStorage.getItem('nps_gh_studio_token');
  if (savedToken) {
    authToken = savedToken;
    try {
      const [repos, user] = await Promise.all([
        ghAll('/user/repos?sort=updated&affiliation=owner,collaborator,organization_member'),
        gh('/user')
      ]);
      allRepos = repos;
      currentUser = user;
      document.getElementById('connDot').classList.add('on');
      const accBtn = document.getElementById('accountBtn');
      accBtn.innerHTML = `<img src="${escapeHtml(user.avatar_url)}">`;
      accBtn.style.borderRadius = '50%';
    } catch (err) {
      // Token sauvegardé invalide/expiré/révoqué : on l'oublie proprement,
      // sans bloquer le démarrage de l'app — l'utilisateur devra juste se
      // reconnecter dans ce cas précis (token cassé, pas juste "app rouverte").
      authToken = null;
      localStorage.removeItem('nps_gh_studio_token');
      localStorage.removeItem(NAVIGATION_STATE_KEY);
    }
  }
  if(restoreNavigationState())paint(false);
  else navReset(renderRepos, 'Dépôts');
})();

/* GitHub Studio — Icon & Splash Studio
 * Inspiré des parcours visuels de ic-ne-splash-dit (image importée, cadrage, fond,
 * taille/ratio, preview, assets détectés et remplacement ciblé dans le dépôt).
 */
let activeIconSplashStudio = null;
const STUDIO_ASSET_PATTERN = /(?:ic_launcher|icon[-_](?:192|512)|favicon|splash|launch_screen|splash_screen)/i;

function renderIconSplashStudio(fullName, branch){
  branch = branch || repoBranchSelection[fullName] || 'main';
  const repo = allRepos.find(item => item.full_name === fullName);
  const state = activeIconSplashStudio = {
    fullName, branch, repo, section:'icon', subview:{icon:'edit',splash:'edit'},
    iconImage:new Image(), splashImage:new Image(), iconObjectUrl:null, splashObjectUrl:null,
    iconName:'', splashName:'', targets:{icon:[],splash:[]}, objectUrls:[],
    iconBg:'#111827', iconFit:'cover', splashBg:'#000000', splashScale:30, splashRatio:'portrait'
  };
  setScreenActions(`<a class="icon-btn" href="${escapeHtml(repo?.html_url||`https://github.com/${fullName}`)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" title="Ouvrir le dépôt sur GitHub">${ico('github',16)}</a>`);
  setBody(`
    <div class="studio-tabs"><button class="chip active" data-studio-section="icon">${ico('image',14)} Icône Android</button><button class="chip" data-studio-section="splash">${ico('expand',14)} Splash screen</button></div>
    <div class="modal-hint">Dépôt : <strong>${escapeHtml(fullName)}</strong> · Branche : <strong>${escapeHtml(branch)}</strong>. Les images restent locales tant que tu ne demandes pas leur envoi à GitHub.</div>
    <section id="studioIconSection">
      <div class="studio-tabs"><button class="chip active" data-studio-subview="icon:edit">Créer / modifier</button><button class="chip" data-studio-subview="icon:files">Ressources existantes</button></div>
      <div id="studioIconEditView">
        <div class="card"><label class="studio-upload" for="studioIconInput"><div class="zip-drop-ico">${ico('upload',22)}</div><strong>Choisir une image pour l’icône</strong><span>PNG, JPG ou WebP · conserve l’original et ajuste seulement le rendu</span></label><input id="studioIconInput" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" hidden><div id="studioIconFilename" class="zip-file-name"></div></div>
        <div class="card"><div class="studio-section-head"><strong>Aperçu de l’icône</strong><span class="studio-small">Fond transparent réservé à la couche adaptive</span></div><div class="studio-preview-actions"><button class="icon-btn" data-studio-preview-more="icon" title="Exporter, partager ou modifier l’icône" aria-label="Options de l’aperçu de l’icône" disabled>${ico('more',18)}</button></div><div class="studio-preview"><canvas id="studioIconCanvas" width="512" height="512" aria-label="Aperçu de l’icône"></canvas></div>
          <div class="studio-controls" style="margin-top:10px;"><div class="studio-control"><label for="studioIconFit">Cadrage</label><select id="studioIconFit" class="modal-input"><option value="cover">Remplir le carré (recadrer au centre)</option><option value="contain">Conserver l’image entière</option></select></div><div class="studio-control"><label for="studioIconBg">Couleur de fond</label><input id="studioIconBg" type="color" class="modal-input" value="#111827" style="height:38px;padding:4px;"></div></div>
          <div class="action-row" style="margin-top:10px;"><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="studioIconDownload" disabled>${ico('download',14)} Télécharger pack Android</button><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="studioIconPush" disabled>${ico('upload',14)} Remplacer dans GitHub</button></div>
          <div id="studioIconStatus" class="modal-hint">Importe une image pour afficher l’aperçu. Le remplacement des fichiers ne se fait qu’après confirmation.</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="studioIconFilesView" hidden><div class="modal-hint">Assets PNG/JPG/WebP et couleur de fond adaptative détectés dans la branche choisie. Coche les chemins à remplacer.</div><div id="studioIconTargets"></div></div>
    </section>
    <section id="studioSplashSection" hidden>
      <div class="studio-tabs"><button class="chip active" data-studio-subview="splash:edit">Créer / modifier</button><button class="chip" data-studio-subview="splash:files">Ressources existantes</button></div>
      <div id="studioSplashEditView">
        <div class="card"><label class="studio-upload" for="studioSplashInput"><div class="zip-drop-ico">${ico('upload',22)}</div><strong>Choisir une image pour le splash</strong><span>PNG, JPG ou WebP · logo centré, proportions respectées</span></label><input id="studioSplashInput" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" hidden><div id="studioSplashFilename" class="zip-file-name"></div></div>
        <div class="card"><div class="studio-section-head"><strong>Aperçu du splash screen</strong><span id="studioSplashSize" class="studio-small">Portrait Android</span></div><div class="studio-preview-actions"><button class="icon-btn" data-studio-preview-more="splash" title="Exporter, partager ou modifier le splash screen" aria-label="Options de l’aperçu du splash screen" disabled>${ico('more',18)}</button></div><div class="studio-preview studio-preview-splash"><canvas id="studioSplashCanvas" width="1080" height="1920" aria-label="Aperçu du splash screen"></canvas></div>
          <div class="studio-controls" style="margin-top:10px;"><div class="studio-control"><label for="studioSplashScale">Taille du logo : <span id="studioSplashScaleLabel">30 %</span></label><input id="studioSplashScale" type="range" min="10" max="65" value="30"></div><div class="studio-control"><label for="studioSplashRatio">Format</label><select id="studioSplashRatio" class="modal-input"><option value="portrait">Portrait Android · 1080 × 1920</option><option value="square">Carré · 1080 × 1080</option><option value="landscape">Paysage · 1920 × 1080</option></select></div><div class="studio-control"><label for="studioSplashBg">Couleur de fond</label><input id="studioSplashBg" type="color" class="modal-input" value="#000000" style="height:38px;padding:4px;"></div></div>
          <div class="action-row" style="margin-top:10px;"><button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" id="studioSplashDownload" disabled>${ico('download',14)} Télécharger pack splash</button><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" id="studioSplashPush" disabled>${ico('upload',14)} Remplacer dans GitHub</button></div>
          <div id="studioSplashStatus" class="modal-hint">Importe une image pour afficher l’aperçu. Le remplacement des fichiers ne se fait qu’après confirmation.</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="studioSplashFilesView" hidden><div class="modal-hint">Assets de splash screen trouvés dans la branche choisie. Coche les chemins à remplacer.</div><div id="studioSplashTargets"></div></div>
    </section>`);

  document.querySelectorAll('[data-studio-section]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>setIconSplashSection(button.dataset.studioSection));
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-studio-subview]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>setIconSplashSubview(...button.dataset.studioSubview.split(':')));
  document.getElementById('studioIconInput').onchange=event=>loadStudioImage('icon',event.target.files?.[0]);
  document.getElementById('studioSplashInput').onchange=event=>loadStudioImage('splash',event.target.files?.[0]);
  document.getElementById('studioIconFit').onchange=event=>{state.iconFit=event.target.value;drawStudioIcon();};
  document.getElementById('studioIconBg').oninput=event=>{state.iconBg=event.target.value;drawStudioIcon();};
  document.getElementById('studioSplashBg').oninput=event=>{state.splashBg=event.target.value;drawStudioSplash();};
  document.getElementById('studioSplashScale').oninput=event=>{state.splashScale=Number(event.target.value);drawStudioSplash();};
  document.getElementById('studioSplashRatio').onchange=event=>{state.splashRatio=event.target.value;drawStudioSplash();};
  document.getElementById('studioIconDownload').onclick=()=>downloadStudioPackage('icon');
  document.getElementById('studioSplashDownload').onclick=()=>downloadStudioPackage('splash');
  document.getElementById('studioIconPush').onclick=()=>pushStudioAssets('icon');
  document.getElementById('studioSplashPush').onclick=()=>pushStudioAssets('splash');
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-studio-preview-more]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>openStudioPreviewActions(button.dataset.studioPreviewMore));
  drawStudioPlaceholders();
  loadStudioTargets(state);
}

function setIconSplashSection(section){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio;if(!state)return;state.section=section;
  document.getElementById('studioIconSection').hidden=section!=='icon';
  document.getElementById('studioSplashSection').hidden=section!=='splash';
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-studio-section]').forEach(button=>button.classList.toggle('active',button.dataset.studioSection===section));
}
function setIconSplashSubview(type,view){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio;if(!state)return;state.subview[type]=view;
  document.getElementById(type==='icon'?'studioIconEditView':'studioSplashEditView').hidden=view!=='edit';
  document.getElementById(type==='icon'?'studioIconFilesView':'studioSplashFilesView').hidden=view!=='files';
  document.querySelectorAll(`[data-studio-subview^="${type}:"]`).forEach(button=>button.classList.toggle('active',button.dataset.studioSubview===`${type}:${view}`));
}
function drawStudioPlaceholders(){
  const icon=document.getElementById('studioIconCanvas');
  if(icon){const context=icon.getContext('2d');context.fillStyle='#111827';context.fillRect(0,0,512,512);context.fillStyle='#8b949e';context.font='16px sans-serif';context.textAlign='center';context.fillText('Importe une image',256,260);}
  const splash=document.getElementById('studioSplashCanvas');
  if(splash){const context=splash.getContext('2d');context.fillStyle='#000';context.fillRect(0,0,splash.width,splash.height);context.fillStyle='#8b949e';context.font='42px sans-serif';context.textAlign='center';context.fillText('Importe une image',splash.width/2,splash.height/2);}
}
function loadStudioImage(type,file,onlyPath=null){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio;if(!state||!file)return;
  state.editOnlyPath=onlyPath;
  if(!file.type.startsWith('image/')){toast('Choisis une image PNG, JPG ou WebP.','error');return;}
  if(file.size>25*1024*1024){toast('Cette image dépasse 25 Mo. Choisis une image plus légère.','error');return;}
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(file),image=type==='icon'?state.iconImage:state.splashImage;
  const previous=type==='icon'?state.iconObjectUrl:state.splashObjectUrl;
  if(previous)URL.revokeObjectURL(previous);
  if(type==='icon'){state.iconObjectUrl=url;state.iconName=file.name;document.getElementById('studioIconFilename').textContent=`${file.name} · ${(file.size/1048576).toFixed(1)} Mo`;}
  else{state.splashObjectUrl=url;state.splashName=file.name;document.getElementById('studioSplashFilename').textContent=`${file.name} · ${(file.size/1048576).toFixed(1)} Mo`;}
  image.onload=()=>{
    if(image.naturalWidth*image.naturalHeight>40000000){
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);image.src='';toast('Cette image dépasse 40 mégapixels. Réduis sa résolution avant de continuer.','error');return;
    }
    if(type==='icon')drawStudioIcon();else drawStudioSplash();
  };
  image.onerror=()=>toast('Impossible de lire ce fichier image.','error');
  image.src=url;
}
function drawStudioIconCanvas(size,transparentForeground=false){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio,canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=canvas.height=size;
  const context=canvas.getContext('2d');
  if(!transparentForeground){context.fillStyle=state.iconBg;context.fillRect(0,0,size,size);}
  const image=state.iconImage;if(!image.naturalWidth)return canvas;
  const area=transparentForeground?size*.66:size;
  const scale=state.iconFit==='cover'?Math.max(area/image.naturalWidth,area/image.naturalHeight):Math.min(area/image.naturalWidth,area/image.naturalHeight);
  const width=image.naturalWidth*scale,height=image.naturalHeight*scale;
  context.drawImage(image,(size-width)/2,(size-height)/2,width,height);
  return canvas;
}
function drawStudioIcon(){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio;if(!state?.iconImage.naturalWidth)return;
  const output=document.getElementById('studioIconCanvas'),canvas=drawStudioIconCanvas(512,false);
  const context=output.getContext('2d');context.clearRect(0,0,512,512);context.drawImage(canvas,0,0);
  document.getElementById('studioIconDownload').disabled=false;
  document.getElementById('studioIconPush').disabled=false;
  document.querySelector('[data-studio-preview-more="icon"]')?.removeAttribute('disabled');
  document.getElementById('studioIconStatus').textContent=state.editOnlyPath?`Aperçu prêt · seule la ressource ${state.editOnlyPath} sera modifiée.`:'Aperçu prêt · les icônes adaptatives gardent leur transparence et leur zone de sécurité.';
}
function studioSplashDimensions(){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio;
  return state.splashRatio==='square'?[1080,1080]:state.splashRatio==='landscape'?[1920,1080]:[1080,1920];
}
function drawStudioSplashCanvas(){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio,[width,height]=studioSplashDimensions();
  const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=width;canvas.height=height;
  const context=canvas.getContext('2d');context.fillStyle=state.splashBg;context.fillRect(0,0,width,height);
  const image=state.splashImage;
  if(image.naturalWidth){
    const max=width*state.splashScale/100;
    const scale=Math.min(max/image.naturalWidth,max/image.naturalHeight);
    const drawWidth=image.naturalWidth*scale,drawHeight=image.naturalHeight*scale;
    context.drawImage(image,(width-drawWidth)/2,(height-drawHeight)/2,drawWidth,drawHeight);
  }
  return canvas;
}
function drawStudioSplash(){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio;if(!state?.splashImage.naturalWidth)return;
  const canvas=drawStudioSplashCanvas(),output=document.getElementById('studioSplashCanvas');
  output.width=canvas.width;output.height=canvas.height;
  output.getContext('2d').drawImage(canvas,0,0);
  document.getElementById('studioSplashScaleLabel').textContent=`${state.splashScale} %`;
  document.getElementById('studioSplashSize').textContent=`${canvas.width} × ${canvas.height}`;
  document.getElementById('studioSplashDownload').disabled=false;
  document.getElementById('studioSplashPush').disabled=false;
  document.querySelector('[data-studio-preview-more="splash"]')?.removeAttribute('disabled');
  document.getElementById('studioSplashStatus').textContent=state.editOnlyPath?`Aperçu prêt · seule la ressource ${state.editOnlyPath} sera modifiée.`:'Aperçu prêt · l’image est centrée, les proportions sont conservées.';
}
function studioCanvasBlob(canvas,type='image/png'){
  return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>canvas.toBlob(blob=>blob?resolve(blob):reject(new Error('Encodage de l’image impossible.')),type));
}
async function studioBlobBytes(blob){return new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());}
async function studioCanvasData(canvas,type='image/png'){
  const blob=await studioCanvasBlob(canvas,type);return {blob,bytes:await studioBlobBytes(blob)};
}
function studioCanvasForType(type){return type==='icon'?drawStudioIconCanvas(512,false):drawStudioSplashCanvas();}
function runStudioImageAction(task){return Promise.resolve().then(task).catch(error=>{if(error?.name!=='AbortError')showError('Action image impossible',friendly(error));});}
async function openStudioPreviewActions(type){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio;if(!state)return;
  const title=type==='icon'?'icône':'splash screen';
  openActionSheet(`Aperçu · ${title}`,[
    {label:`Télécharger cette image PNG`,icon:'download',fn:()=>runStudioImageAction(async()=>saveBlob(`${state.repo?.name||'android'}-${type==='icon'?'icone':'splash'}.png`,await studioCanvasBlob(studioCanvasForType(type)),'image/png'))},
    {label:'Partager cette image',icon:'share',fn:()=>runStudioImageAction(async()=>shareBlobFile(`${state.repo?.name||'android'}-${type==='icon'?'icone':'splash'}.png`,await studioCanvasBlob(studioCanvasForType(type)),'image/png'))},
    {label:'Modifier la source de cette image…',icon:'pencil',fn:()=>document.getElementById(type==='icon'?'studioIconInput':'studioSplashInput').click()}
  ]);
}
function openStudioAssetActions(type,index){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio,item=state?.targets[type]?.[index];if(!item)return;
  if(/ic_launcher_background\.xml$/i.test(item.path)){toast('Ce fichier est une couleur XML, pas une image.','info');return;}
  const filename=item.path.split('/').pop()||'image.png',mime=item.previewBlob?.type||studioMimeForPath(item.path);
  openActionSheet(filename,[
    {label:'Télécharger cette image',icon:'download',fn:()=>runStudioImageAction(async()=>saveBlob(filename,await studioGetTargetBlob(state,item),mime))},
    {label:'Partager cette image',icon:'share',fn:()=>runStudioImageAction(async()=>shareBlobFile(filename,await studioGetTargetBlob(state,item),mime))},
    {label:'Modifier uniquement cette image…',icon:'pencil',fn:()=>runStudioImageAction(async()=>{const blob=await studioGetTargetBlob(state,item);state.targets[type].forEach((target,targetIndex)=>{target.selected=targetIndex===index;});document.querySelectorAll(`#${type==='icon'?'studioIconTargets':'studioSplashTargets'} .studio-target-check`).forEach(input=>{input.checked=Number(input.dataset.studioIndex)===index;});setIconSplashSection(type);setIconSplashSubview(type,'edit');loadStudioImage(type,new File([blob],filename,{type:mime}),item.path);})}
  ]);
}
function studioMimeForPath(path){const ext=(path.split('.').pop()||'').toLowerCase();return ext==='webp'?'image/webp':(ext==='jpg'||ext==='jpeg'?'image/jpeg':'image/png');}
async function studioGetTargetBlob(state,item){
  if(item.previewBlob)return item.previewBlob;
  const progress=createTransferProgress('Chargement de l’image',item.path,item.size||0,'Connexion à GitHub…');
  try{
    item.previewBlob=await fetchGithubBlobWithProgress(state.fullName,item.sha,(_delta,loaded,total,stage)=>{
      progress.update(total?Math.min(92,loaded/total*92):null,stage||'Téléchargement depuis GitHub…',loaded,total||undefined);
    });
    progress.finish('Image prête');
    return item.previewBlob;
  }catch(error){progress.fail();throw error;}
}
async function downloadStudioPackage(type){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio;if(!state||!window.JSZip)return;
  const filename=`${state.repo?.name||state.fullName.split('/').pop()}-${type==='icon'?'android-icon-pack':'splash-pack'}.zip`;
  const transfer=createTransferProgress(type==='icon'?'Export du pack d’icônes':'Export du pack splash',filename,0,'Préparation des images…');
  try{
    const zip=new JSZip();
    let completed=0,total=type==='icon'?11:2;
    const step=label=>{completed++;transfer.update(completed/total*72,`${label} · ${completed}/${total}`);};
    if(type==='icon'){
      const density=[['mdpi',48,108],['hdpi',72,162],['xhdpi',96,216],['xxhdpi',144,324],['xxxhdpi',192,432]];
      for(const [name,size,foregroundSize] of density){
        const directory=`app/src/main/res/mipmap-${name}/`;
        const regular=await studioCanvasData(drawStudioIconCanvas(size,false));
        step(`Icône ${name}`);
        const foreground=await studioCanvasData(drawStudioIconCanvas(foregroundSize,true));
        step(`Premier plan adaptatif ${name}`);
        zip.file(`${directory}ic_launcher.png`,regular.bytes);
        zip.file(`${directory}ic_launcher_round.png`,regular.bytes);
        zip.file(`${directory}ic_launcher_foreground.png`,foreground.bytes);
      }
      zip.file('app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml','<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>\n<adaptive-icon xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android">\n    <background android:drawable="@color/ic_launcher_background"/>\n    <foreground android:drawable="@mipmap/ic_launcher_foreground"/>\n</adaptive-icon>\n');
      zip.file('app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml','<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>\n<adaptive-icon xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android">\n    <background android:drawable="@color/ic_launcher_background"/>\n    <foreground android:drawable="@mipmap/ic_launcher_foreground"/>\n</adaptive-icon>\n');
      zip.file('app/src/main/res/values/ic_launcher_background.xml',`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>\n<resources>\n    <color name="ic_launcher_background">${state.iconBg}</color>\n</resources>\n`);
      zip.file('icon-512.png',(await studioCanvasData(drawStudioIconCanvas(512,false))).bytes);
      step('Icône principale haute résolution');
      zip.file('README.txt','Copiez le contenu de app/src/main/res dans le dossier res de votre module Android. L’image adaptive foreground est transparente et laisse une marge de sécurité au masque Android. Vérifiez les chemins du projet avant tout remplacement.\n');
    }else{
      const canvas=drawStudioSplashCanvas();
      zip.file('app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi/splash.png',(await studioCanvasData(canvas)).bytes);
      step('Image splash Android');
      zip.file('splash-screen.png',(await studioCanvasData(canvas)).bytes);
      step('Aperçu splash');
      zip.file('README.txt','Copiez app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi/splash.png dans les ressources de votre module Android, puis vérifiez que votre thème fait référence à ce drawable. Le ratio choisi est '+canvas.width+' × '+canvas.height+'.\n');
    }
    transfer.update(74,'Compression du ZIP…');
    const blob=await zip.generateAsync({type:'blob',compression:'DEFLATE',compressionOptions:{level:5}},meta=>transfer.update(74+meta.percent*.18,`Compression du ZIP… ${Math.round(meta.percent)}%`));
    transfer.setTotal(blob.size);
    await saveBlob(filename,blob,'application/zip',transfer,92,100);
  }catch(error){transfer.fail();showError('Création du pack impossible',friendly(error));}
}

async function loadStudioTargets(state){
  for(const url of state.objectUrls)URL.revokeObjectURL(url);state.objectUrls=[];
  const iconBox=document.getElementById('studioIconTargets'),splashBox=document.getElementById('studioSplashTargets');
  if(!iconBox||!splashBox)return;
  iconBox.innerHTML=splashBox.innerHTML=`<div class="loading-row"><span class="spin-lg"></span> Recherche des ressources d’icône et splash…</div>`;
  try{
    const ref=await gh(`/repos/${state.fullName}/git/ref/heads/${encodeURIComponent(state.branch)}`);
    const tree=await gh(`/repos/${state.fullName}/git/trees/${ref.object.sha}?recursive=1`);
    if(tree.truncated)throw new Error('GitHub a tronqué l’arborescence du dépôt; les ressources Android ne peuvent pas être détectées de manière fiable.');
    const blobs=(tree.tree||[]).filter(item=>item.type==='blob'&&STUDIO_ASSET_PATTERN.test(item.path));
    const imageExt=/\.(png|jpe?g|webp)$/i;
    const icons=blobs.filter(item=>/(?:ic_launcher|icon[-_](?:192|512)|favicon)/i.test(item.path)&&!/(?:splash|launch_screen)/i.test(item.path)&&(imageExt.test(item.path)||(/ic_launcher_background\.xml$/i.test(item.path))));
    const splash=blobs.filter(item=>/(?:splash|launch_screen|splash_screen)/i.test(item.path)&&imageExt.test(item.path));
    state.targets.icon=icons.map(item=>({...item,selected:true}));
    state.targets.splash=splash.map(item=>({...item,selected:true}));
    renderStudioTargets(state,'icon');renderStudioTargets(state,'splash');
    const imageTargets=[...state.targets.icon,...state.targets.splash].filter(item=>imageExt.test(item.path));
    let next=0;
    async function worker(){while(true){const index=next++;if(index>=imageTargets.length)return;const item=imageTargets[index];try{const response=await fetch(`https://api.github.com/repos/${state.fullName}/git/blobs/${item.sha}`,{headers:{Authorization:`token ${authToken}`,Accept:'application/vnd.github.raw'}});if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);const blob=await response.blob();item.previewBlob=blob;item.previewUrl=URL.createObjectURL(blob);state.objectUrls.push(item.previewUrl);const img=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-studio-thumb]')].find(element=>element.dataset.studioThumb===item.path);if(img)img.src=item.previewUrl;}catch(error){}}
    }
    await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,imageTargets.length)},worker));
  }catch(error){
    const message=`Ressources non chargées : ${friendly(error)}`;
    if(iconBox.isConnected)iconBox.innerHTML=`<div class="empty-state">${escapeHtml(message)}</div>`;
    if(splashBox.isConnected)splashBox.innerHTML=`<div class="empty-state">${escapeHtml(message)}</div>`;
  }
}
function renderStudioTargets(state,type){
  const box=document.getElementById(type==='icon'?'studioIconTargets':'studioSplashTargets');if(!box)return;
  const entries=state.targets[type]||[];
  if(!entries.length){box.innerHTML=`<div class="empty-state"><div class="ic">${ico('inbox',26)}</div>Aucun asset ${type==='icon'?'d’icône':'de splash'} compatible trouvé sur cette branche. Télécharge le pack et ajoute ses fichiers au projet Android.</div>`;return;}
  box.innerHTML=entries.map((item,index)=>{
    const isConfig=/ic_launcher_background\.xml$/i.test(item.path);
    return `<div class="studio-target-row"><label class="studio-target list-item"><input type="checkbox" class="studio-target-check" data-studio-type="${type}" data-studio-index="${index}" checked>${isConfig?`<span class="li-ico">${ico('tag',20)}</span>`:`<img data-studio-thumb="${escapeHtml(item.path)}" alt="" style="width:40px;height:40px;object-fit:contain;border-radius:6px;background:var(--bg-2);">`}<span class="li-main"><span class="li-title">${escapeHtml(item.path)}</span><span class="li-sub">${isConfig?'Fond de couleur adaptive':`${item.size?`${(item.size/1024).toFixed(0)} Ko · `:''}${escapeHtml(item.path.split('.').pop().toUpperCase())}`}</span></span></label>${isConfig?'':`<button type="button" class="icon-btn studio-asset-more" data-studio-target-more="${type}" data-studio-index="${index}" title="Exporter, partager ou modifier cette image" aria-label="Options de ${escapeHtml(item.path)}">${ico('more',18)}</button>`}</div>`;
  }).join('')+`<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm btn-block" style="margin-top:9px;" data-studio-push-list="${type}">${ico('upload',14)} Remplacer les ressources cochées</button>`;
  box.querySelectorAll('.studio-target-check').forEach(input=>input.onchange=()=>{entries[Number(input.dataset.studioIndex)].selected=input.checked;});
  box.querySelectorAll('[data-studio-target-more]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=event=>{event.stopPropagation();openStudioAssetActions(type,Number(button.dataset.studioIndex));});
  box.querySelector('[data-studio-push-list]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>pushStudioAssets(type));
}
function studioIconSizeForPath(path,foreground=false){
  const match=path.match(/mipmap-(mdpi|hdpi|xhdpi|xxhdpi|xxxhdpi)/i);
  const values={mdpi:48,hdpi:72,xhdpi:96,xxhdpi:144,xxxhdpi:192};
  const density=match?match[1].toLowerCase():null;
  return density?(foreground?values[density]*2.25:values[density]):(foreground?432:512);
}
function canvasBytesToBase64(bytes){
  let binary='';for(let offset=0;offset<bytes.length;offset+=0x8000)binary+=String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(offset,offset+0x8000));return btoa(binary);
}
async function studioImageEntry(state,type,item){
  if(/ic_launcher_background\.xml$/i.test(item.path)){
    const response=await gh(`/repos/${state.fullName}/contents/${encodePath(item.path)}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(state.branch)}`);
    let text=b64ToUtf8(response.content||'');
    if(!/<color\s+name=["']ic_launcher_background["'][^>]*>\s*#[0-9a-f]{6,8}\s*<\/color>/i.test(text))throw new Error(`${item.path} ne contient pas la couleur ic_launcher_background attendue; ce fichier ne sera pas modifié.`);
    text=text.replace(/(<color\s+name=["']ic_launcher_background["'][^>]*>\s*)#[0-9a-f]{6,8}(\s*<\/color>)/i,`$1${state.iconBg}$2`);
    return new TextEncoder().encode(text);
  }
  const foreground=/ic_launcher_foreground/i.test(item.path);
  const canvas=type==='icon'?drawStudioIconCanvas(studioIconSizeForPath(item.path,foreground),foreground):drawStudioSplashCanvas();
  const ext=item.path.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
  if(foreground&&ext!=='png')throw new Error(`${item.path} est une couche adaptive transparente; elle doit rester au format PNG.`);
  const mime=ext==='webp'?'image/webp':(ext==='jpg'||ext==='jpeg'?'image/jpeg':'image/png');
  const {bytes,blob}=await studioCanvasData(canvas,mime);
  if(blob.type!==mime)throw new Error(`Le navigateur ne sait pas créer le format .${ext} requis pour ${item.path}; utilise un fichier PNG.`);
  return bytes;
}
async function pushStudioAssets(type){
  const state=activeIconSplashStudio;if(!state)return;
  const source=type==='icon'?state.iconImage:state.splashImage;
  if(!source.naturalWidth){toast(`Importe d’abord une image pour le ${type==='icon'?'pack d’icône':'splash screen'}.`,'error');setIconSplashSubview(type,'edit');return;}
  const selected=(state.targets[type]||[]).filter(item=>item.selected);
  if(!selected.length){toast('Coche au moins un chemin détecté à remplacer.','info');return;}
  const paths=selected.map(item=>item.path);
  const preview=paths.slice(0,8).map(path=>`• ${path}`).join('\n');
  const confirmed=await confirmModal(`Remplacer ${paths.length} ressource(s) ${type==='icon'?'d’icône':'de splash'} dans ${state.fullName}, sur la branche ${state.branch} ?\n\n${preview}${paths.length>8?`\n… et ${paths.length-8} autre(s)`:''}\n\nUn seul commit sera créé.`,{confirmLabel:'Remplacer les ressources',danger:true});
  if(!confirmed)return;
  const button=document.querySelector(`[data-studio-push-list="${type}"]`)||document.getElementById(type==='icon'?'studioIconPush':'studioSplashPush');
  if(button)setBtnLoading(button,true);
  const statusBox=document.getElementById(type==='icon'?'studioIconStatus':'studioSplashStatus');
  try{
    statusBox.textContent='Préparation des images…';
    const entries=[];
    for(const item of selected)entries.push({path:item.path,bytes:await studioImageEntry(state,type,item)});
    await pushStudioEntries(state,type,entries,statusBox);
    toast(`${entries.length} ressource(s) remplacée(s) dans ${state.fullName}`,'success',4000);
    await loadStudioTargets(state);
  }catch(error){statusBox.textContent=`Erreur : ${friendly(error)}`;showError('Remplacement impossible',`${friendly(error)}\n\nVérifie la permission Contents en écriture du token GitHub.`);}
  finally{if(button?.isConnected)setBtnLoading(button,false,`${ico('upload',14)} Remplacer ${type==='icon'?'dans GitHub':'dans GitHub'}`);}
}
async function pushStudioEntries(state,type,entries,statusBox){
  const ref=await gh(`/repos/${state.fullName}/git/ref/heads/${encodeURIComponent(state.branch)}`);
  const parentSha=ref.object.sha,base=await gh(`/repos/${state.fullName}/git/commits/${parentSha}`);
  const blobs=[];let next=0,done=0;
  async function worker(){while(true){const index=next++;if(index>=entries.length)return;const entry=entries[index];const encoded=canvasBytesToBase64(entry.bytes);const blob=await gh(`/repos/${state.fullName}/git/blobs`,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({content:encoded,encoding:'base64'})});blobs[index]={path:entry.path,mode:'100644',type:'blob',sha:blob.sha};done++;statusBox.textContent=`Préparation des fichiers · ${done}/${entries.length}…`;}}
  await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,entries.length)},worker));
  const tree=await gh(`/repos/${state.fullName}/git/trees`,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({base_tree:base.tree.sha,tree:blobs})});
  statusBox.textContent='Création du commit…';
  const commit=await gh(`/repos/${state.fullName}/git/commits`,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({message:`Mettre à jour les ${type==='icon'?'icônes':'splash screens'} Android via GitHub Studio`,tree:tree.sha,parents:[parentSha]})});
  await gh(`/repos/${state.fullName}/git/refs/heads/${encodeURIComponent(state.branch)}`,{method:'PATCH',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({sha:commit.sha})});
  statusBox.textContent=`${entries.length} ressource(s) poussée(s) · commit ${commit.sha.slice(0,7)}.`;
}

</script>
</body>
</html>
